- 覆盖检查：本篇补上"自造上位词"与"四种词性转换"，覆盖了 G7—G8 未涉及的转换类型；36 属开放空，答案不唯一，已在边界层说明。

### 2024湖北专升本小蓝书预测卷九 · Passage 5

How do you find happiness every single day? How do you get more out of life and live life to the fullest? There are three easy ways to help you to enjoy your daily life.

One way to find happiness within you is to show your appreciation(感激)to others. Showing appreciation gives happiness to others, at the same time, we can receive happiness in return by writing a letter of appreciation and for the reply we get.

Often maybe you are strict with yourself by telling yourself you could have done things better. Have a look at your life and think about what you have achieved today. So take 5 minutes right now to write down what you have achieved in life and what you take pride in.

Have you ever kept a diary? Sometimes writing things down will give you a clear thinking and allows you to let go of things. When you write in a diary, you have the ability to express yourself as you truly would like to be.

Each of the above three easy steps takes only 15 minutes. Not only can it produce happiness for you, but also it will help others be in good feelings. Why not have a try right now?

| Title: ____36____ to find happiness | |
|---|---|
| Showing appreciation to others | Write a ____37____ to give happiness to others and receive happiness in return. |
| Appreciating yourself | Write down your achievements and what you take ____38____ in. |
| Having a conversation with yourself | Writing in diaries will give you a ____39____ thinking. |



| Conclusion (结论) | Do as any of the three steps above, and it will produce ____40____ for you. |

**〔G10〕小蓝书预测卷九 · Passage 5（36—40）十层研究**

**36｜Ways（Steps / How 亦可，开放空）**
1 表标题"Title: ____36____ to find happiness"，下辖"感激他人／欣赏自己／与自己对话"三条；原文"There are three easy ways to help you to enjoy your daily life."与"Each of the above three easy steps takes only 15 minutes."。
2 候选：Ways（原文 three ways）；Steps（原文 three steps）；How（首句 How do you find happiness）；Tips（同义）；Reasons（因果）。
3 Ways→"Ways to find happiness"与三条并列、与 three easy ways 对应，复数＋to do 成立。Steps→同样在原文出现，语法亦通，属并列可接受答案。How→"How to find happiness"是标准标题式，亦通，但表格已用 to find，How 与 to do 连用略显重复。Tips/Reasons→原文无据或义偏。
4 干扰：首句连续两个 How 问句，最诱人填 How；末段"three easy steps"又诱人填 Steps；三条内容都是动作，诱人自造 Tips。
5 意图：考"标题概括空的可接受答案集"，分"只认一个标准答案"者。本题三个候选都在原文出现，属开放空。
6 边界：若标题已给"____ of finding happiness"，则填 Ways（of 后接 -ing，不用 to）；若下辖是"原因"，填 Reasons。介词 of/to 决定候选集。
7 错：Way（单数，与三条不称）；Hows（不可数/不成立）；Reason（下辖非原因）。
8 R10.1 若空位是标题的"方式"位（___ to do sth），优先取原文 topic sentence 中表"方式"的复数名词（ways/steps）；原文同时出现多个同义方式词时，答案不唯一，答题取最先出现者，判卷接受同义。适用条件：下辖行为并列动作；例外：标题后是 of 时改用 -ing 结构。
9 04→RC-1.3 概括归纳→标题方式词；与 R9.1"自造上位词"区别：本条有原文词可抄，R9.1 无。与 A1「原文唯一对应词优先」冲突：用「Steps」当标题，覆盖不了全部行；优先级：A8 栏头/标题概括 ＞ A1。本空特有依据：首句连续两个 How 问句，最诱人填 How。
10 自检：①下辖几条？②原文 topic sentence 用 ways 还是 steps？③to 还是 of？

**37｜letter**
1 表"Showing appreciation to others｜Write a ____37____ to give happiness to others and receive happiness in return."；原文"by writing a letter of appreciation and for the reply we get"。
2 候选：letter（原文）；note（近义）；card（近义）；diary（下条步骤词）；list（无据）。
3 letter→"Write a letter"与原文 writing a letter of appreciation 对应，a＋单数可数成立。note→"write a note"可通，原文 letter。card→可通，原文无据。diary→下一行"kept a diary"是"与自己对话"那一步的词，跨步骤混用。list→无据。
4 干扰：下一行 Write down your achievements 与 kept a diary 都在附近，诱人填 diary；中文"写张卡片"也常见。
5 意图：考"步骤不串行"，分"看到 write 就取邻近名词"者。
6 边界：若空位是"write a letter of ____"填 appreciation；若"keep a ____"填 diary。动词 write 后接不同宾语，须看该行主题（感激他人）。
7 错：diary（跨步骤）；notes（复数与 a 冲突）；appreciation（把 of 后名词提前，句法不合）。
8 R10.2 表格分步骤时，取词范围限于该行对应的原文句；邻行（尤其下一行）的词一律不作候选。适用条件：表格按步骤/方面分行；例外：该行内容需跨句合并时，取合并范围内的词。
9 04→RC-1.1→行内取词；与 R4.4"介词宾语"并用：about/in 之后仍受行范围限制。与 A6「词性/同义转换」冲突：取「note」属于同义转写，不是原文对应；优先级：A1 原文唯一对应 ＞ A6。本空特有依据：下一行 Write down your achievements 与 kept a diary 都在附近，诱人填 diary。
10 自检：①这一行对应原文哪句？②我填的词来自本行还是邻行？③a 后是单数吗？

**38｜pride**
1 表"Appreciating yourself｜Write down your achievements and what you take ____38____ in."；原文"write down what you have achieved in life and what you take pride in."。
2 候选：pride（原文）；interest（同结构）；part（同结构）；pleasure（同结构）；confidence（形近）。
3 pride→take pride in 固定短语，与原文逐字合。interest→take interest in 亦成立，但原文 pride，且本行主题是"欣赏自己"，interest 义偏。part→take part in 义"参与"，与"以…为荣"无关。pleasure→take pleasure in 可通，原文 pride。confidence→take confidence in 不成立。
4 干扰：take ___ in 是"万能槽"，pride/interest/part/pleasure 四个短语都成立，只有原文那个对；confidence 与 proud 语义近，诱人。
5 意图：考"固定短语中取原文词，而非取任一可成立短语"，分"填得出一个短语就交卷"者。
6 边界：若空位是"be proud ____"填 of；若"take ____ in your work"单看可填 pride/interest/pleasure，须回原文定。
7 错：interest（同结构替换）；proud（形容词入名词位）；prides（不可数）。
8 R10.3 若空位处于 take ___ in / be proud ___ 等固定短语，先列可成立的搭配，再回原文取唯一词；能成立≠正确。适用条件：空位处于 take ___ in / be proud ___ 等固定短语。例外（本空错误路径）：填 interest（同结构替换）；错在把「空位处于 take ___ in / be proud ___ 等固定短语」当成唯一情形，换到那种位置时，入空的恰恰是「of」，而不是「pride」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9 04→RC-1.1→固定短语子类；与 R10.2 行范围并用：先定行，再定短语。与 A6「词性/同义转换」冲突：把「interest」当同义替换，会改掉原文用词；优先级：A1 原文唯一对应 ＞ A6。本空特有依据：take ___ in 是"万能槽"，pride/interest/part/pleasure 四个短语都成立，只有原文那个对。
10 自检：①这个短语有几种填法？②原文用的是哪个？③词性/数对吗？

**39｜clear**
1 表"Having a conversation with yourself｜Writing in diaries will give you a ____39____ thinking."；原文"Sometimes writing things down will give you a clear thinking and allows you to let go of things."。
2 候选：clear（原文）；good（泛义）；positive（近义）；deep（近义）；clean（形近）。
3 clear→"a clear thinking"与原文逐字合（原文搭配略反常，但为原文）。good→"a good thinking"可通，原文 clear。positive→可通，原文无据。deep→可通，义偏"深刻"。clean→与 clear 形近义异。
4 干扰：clear/clean 形近；中文"清晰的思维"诱人直接译成 clear thinking，反而对了，但"a clear thinking"的冠词用法会让人怀疑而改成 good。
5 意图：考"原文搭配优先于语法直觉"，分"觉得 a clear thinking 别扭就改词"者。
6 边界：若空位是"give you ____ thinking"（无 a），clear 更自然；若"a ____ mind"填 clear。冠词 a 在此是原文的瑕疵，照抄为安。
7 错：clean（形近）；clearer（比较级无据）；clarity（名词，a clarity thinking 不成立）。
8 R10.4 若原文搭配与语法直觉冲突（a clear thinking），以原文为准；考试填原词，不擅自改词或改冠词。适用条件：原文搭配与语法直觉冲突（a clear thinking）。例外（本空错误路径）：填 clean（形近）；错在把「原文搭配与语法直觉冲突（a clear thinking）」当成唯一情形，本条的适用前提不再成立，入空的就不再是「clear」这一路，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9 04→RC-1.1→原文优先原则；与 R3.5"同义群取原词"同源。与 A6「词性/同义转换」冲突：按「good」改写，词性/搭配与原位不合；优先级：A1 原文唯一对应 ＞ A6。本空特有依据：clear/clean 形近。
10 自检：①原文这一句的形容词是哪个？②我是否因"读着别扭"换了词？③冠词 a 是原文自带吗？

**40｜happiness**
1 表"Conclusion (结论)｜Do as any of the three steps above, and it will produce ____40____ for you."；原文"Not only can it produce happiness for you, but also it will help others be in good feelings."。
2 候选：happiness（原文）；joy（同义）；pleasure（同义）；good feelings（原文后半）；benefits（概括）。
3 happiness→"produce happiness for you"与原文逐字合，produce＋抽象名词成立。joy/pleasure→义同，原文 happiness。good feelings→原文是"help others be in good feelings"，受益者是他人，不是 you。benefits→概括，原文无。
4 干扰：后半句 good feelings 就在同句，且"produce"后接"感受"很顺，诱人填 good feelings；中文"带来快乐"可对应 joy/pleasure。
5 意图：考"结论句取原文宾语＋分清'给你'与'给他人'"，分"取同句后半词"者。
6 边界：若空位是"help others be in good ____"填 feelings；若"it will produce ____"且 for you 在句末，取 happiness。受益者是判别键。
7 错：good feelings（受益人错位，且 2 词）；joy（同义替换）；happy（形容词入名词位）。
8 R10.5 结论空的取词范围是该结论句的主句宾语；同句并列分句的宾语若受益者不同，不得挪用。适用条件：空位词性与原文对应词一致，且题面无改写。例外（本空错误路径）：填 good feelings（受益人错位，且 2 词）；错在把「空位词性与原文对应词一致，且题面无改写」当成唯一情形，换到那种位置时，入空的恰恰是「feelings」，而不是「happiness」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9 04→RC-1.3→结论句宾语；与 R6.5"所有格后中心名词"并列，均属"结论句取词"。与 A6「词性/同义转换」冲突：换「joy」更顺口，但原文用的是另一个词；优先级：A1 原文唯一对应 ＞ A6。本空特有依据：后半句 good feelings 就在同句，且"produce"后接"感受"很顺，诱人填 good feelings。
10 自检：①for you 还是 for others？②主句的宾语是哪个词？③我填了几个词？

**组小结（G10 · 小蓝书预测卷九）**
- 考点分布：标题方式词（开放空）1（36）；行内取词防串行 1（37）；固定短语取唯一词 1（38）；原文搭配优先 1（39）；结论句宾语＋受益人 1（40）。
- 规则：R10.1 标题方式词取原文 ways/steps，答案可多解（36）；R10.2 分步骤表取词限于本行原文句（37）；R10.3 固定短语先列可成立项再回原文取唯一（38）；R10.4 原文搭配优先于语法直觉（39）；R10.5 结论空取主句宾语、不挪用他受益者（40）。
- 冲突与优先级：R10.3/R10.4 与"语法/搭配正确即可"冲突，原文唯一性优先；R10.5 与"同句就近取词"冲突，受益者优先。
- 未归类：36 为开放空，答案不唯一，已单列，不入唯一解规则。
- 覆盖检查：本篇补上"开放空""跨行防串""固定短语多解"三类，均为前几篇未覆盖。

### 2024湖北专升本小蓝书预测卷二 · Passage 5

You probably have heard of the Four Great Inventions of ancient China which brought great changes to the world in the past. Now, China's four great innovations (革新) in modern times are widely known among people from all over the world.

Online shopping is really convenient for Chinese people. From clothes to foods, from physical goods to virtual products, you can buy almost anything online. The popularity of online shopping has made the express delivery (快递) industry develop quickly. People can get the things they buy online in only two or three days after placing the orders, and it saves them a lot of time.

In Western countries, people usually pay by credit (信用) card or in cash (现金). But in China,



more and more people, even old people, like to pay for things with their phones. Since the day mobile payments got popular in China, many Chinese people's wallets have been empty with only a few coins in them, which were left probably a few months ago.

Shared bikes are "the perfect first & last mile solution" for people. It's very convenient to use a shared bike without worrying about where to park it. Although it caused a lot of problems at first, most of them were solved with the help of the government.

China has the largest high-speed railway network (网络) in the world at present. The construction of high-speed railway in China has made great progress in the past 15 years. Some of the railways were even built under extreme natural environment. Chinese people are getting great benefits from the high-speed railway.

| China's four great innovations in modern times | |
| --- | --- |
| Introduction | The Four Great Inventions of ancient China changed the world ______36______ in history, and now the world's attention is attracted by four innovations in modern China. |
| Online shopping | People can buy almost anything online. The popularity of online shopping has pushed the ______37______ of the express delivery industry. |
| Mobile payments | Different from people in Western countries, more and more Chinese people like to pay on their ______38______. |
| Shared bikes | Shared bikes are convenient. People don't need to ______39______ about where to park them. |
| High-speed railway | The high-speed railway network in China is ______40______ than that in any other country. |

**〔G11〕小蓝书预测卷二 · Passage 5（36—40）十层研究**

**36｜greatly**
1 表"…The Four Great Inventions of ancient China changed the world ____36____ in history…"；原文"which brought great changes to the world in the past"。空在动宾之后、状语之前，副词位。
2 候选：greatly（great changes 的副词化）；completely；widely；rapidly。
3 greatly→change the world greatly 与 bring great changes 对应，修饰 changed 成立。completely→"彻底改变"，原文无据。widely→可通，义偏"范围广"而非"程度大"。rapidly→原文无"快"义。
4 干扰：原文 great 是形容词，诱人直接填 great（词性错）；in history 又诱人填 early/once。
5 意图：考"形容词定语→副词状语"的转换，分"照抄 great"者。
6 边界：若空位是"brought ____ changes"填 great（形容词）；若"changed the world ____"则必为副词。
7 错：great（词性错）；great change（超 1 空）；greatly 拼成 greatlly。
8 R11.1 若空位修饰动词且句中另有状语，填副词；原文的形容词定语须转副词（great changes→change greatly）。适用条件：空位修饰动词且句中另有状语。例外（本空错误路径）：填 great（词性错）；错在把「空位修饰动词且句中另有状语」当成唯一情形，一旦空位的词性/位置换成形容词，答案就要从「greatly」换成「great」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9 04→RC-1.2 词性转换；与 R9.2 短语压缩同族：原文词形不符时转换。与 A1「原词直取」冲突：按「completely」填，修饰关系不成立；优先级：A3 空位词性 ＞ A1。本空特有依据：原文 great 是形容词，诱人直接填 great（词性错）。
10 自检：①空位修饰谁？②原文是形容词还是副词？③拼写对吗？

**37｜development**
1 表"The popularity of online shopping has pushed the ____37____ of the express delivery industry."；原文"has made the express delivery industry develop quickly"。空在 the 与 of 之间，名词位。
2 候选：development（develop 的名词化）；growth；speed；progress；popularity。
3 development→"the development of the industry"与 make...develop 对应，the＋名词＋of 成立。growth→同义，原文 develop。speed→"the speed of"可通，但原文说的是"发展快"。progress→同义，原文 develop。popularity→与前句 popularity 重复。
4 干扰：同句前半 popularity 就在眼前，诱人重复；quickly 诱人填 speed。
5 意图：考"动词 develop→名词 development"的 of 结构，分"填 speed 走捷径"者。
6 边界：若空位是"has made the industry ____ quickly"填 develop（原形）；若"the ____ industry"填 developing。
7 错：develop（动词入名词位）；popularity（重复）；developing（-ing 不能作 the...of 的中心词）。
8 R11.2 若空位是"the ___ of + 名词"，填名词；原文若为动词（develop），名词化为 development/growth。适用条件：空位是"the ___ of + 名词"。例外（本空错误路径）：填 develop（动词入名词位）；错在把「空位是"the ___ of + 名词"」当成唯一情形，一旦空位的词性/位置换成原形，答案就要从「development」换成「develop」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9 04→RC-1.2→of 结构名词化；与 R9.4"并列名词化"同族。与 A1「原词直取」冲突：直取「growth」保留原形态，与空前结构不合；优先级：A3 空位词性/形态 ＞ A1。本空特有依据：同句前半 popularity 就在眼前，诱人重复。
10 自检：①the...of 之间要名词吗？②原文动词是哪个？③名词形式对吗？

**38｜phones**
1 表"Mobile payments｜…more and more Chinese people like to pay on their ____38____."；原文"like to pay for things with their phones"。空在 their 之后，复数名词位；介词由 with 改为 on。
2 候选：phones（原文）；mobile phones；cards；cash；phone。
3 phones→"pay on their phones"（在手机上支付）成立，与 with their phones 同指。mobile phones→2 词，原文 phones，多此一举。cards→是西方人的方式，与本行"中国人"对立。cash→反向。phone→单数与 their 不称。
4 干扰：上一行"pay by credit card or in cash"就在同段，诱人填 cards/cash；"on their ____"又像"on their way"。
5 意图：考"介词改写后仍取同一名词＋their 后复数"，分"被上句西方方式带走"者。
6 边界：若空位是"pay by ____"填 card/credit card；若"pay in ____"填 cash。介词 with/by/on/in 决定搭配。
7 错：cards（取西方方式）；cash（同上）；phone（单复数）。
8 R11.3 若介词被改写（with→on）而名词不变，仍取原文名词；their 后须复数；同段对比方的名词不作候选。适用条件：介词被改写（with→on）而名词不变。例外（本空错误路径）：填 cards（取西方方式）；错在把「介词被改写（with→on）而名词不变」当成唯一情形，换到那种位置时，入空的恰恰是「card/credit card」，而不是「phones」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9 04→RC-1.2→介词改写；与 R4.4"介词宾语"配合：先定介词，再取名词。与 A6「词性/同义转换」冲突：把「cards」当同义替换会改掉原文用词；优先级：A1 原文唯一对应 ＞ A6。本空特有依据：上一行"pay by credit card or in cash"就在同段，诱人填 cards/cash。
10 自检：①介词是 on 还是 with/by？②their 后是复数吗？③这个词是不是对比方的？

**39｜worry**
1 表"Shared bikes｜…People don’t need to ____39____ about where to park them."；原文"It’s very convenient to use a shared bike without worrying about where to park it."。空在 need to 之后、about 之前，动词原形位。
2 候选：worry（原文）；care；think；complain；bother。
3 worry→worry about 固定搭配，与 without worrying about 对应，need to 后原形，成立。care→care about 可通，义"在意"，弱于"担心"。think→think about 可通，义偏"考虑"。complain→complain about 可通，原文无。bother→bother about 少用。
4 干扰：about 前可填 worry/care/think/complain 四个，只有原文那个对；"don't need to"又诱人填 care（不必在意）。
5 意图：考"without V-ing→need to V 的转换＋about 搭配"，分"按 about 随手填"者。
6 边界：若空位是"without ____ about"填 worrying（介词后 -ing）；若"don’t ____ about it"可填 worry/care，须看原文。
7 错：worrying（need to 后误用 -ing）；care（同结构替换）；worried（形容词）。
8 R11.4 若原文用 without + V-ing，表格改为 need to / should 后，动词还原原形；介词 about 只作搭配校验，不作选词依据。适用条件：原文用 without + V-ing。例外：若空位所在结构换成另一形态位（to 后→原形、介词后→-ing）；此时本条的适用前提不再成立，入空的就不再是「worry」这一路，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9 04→RC-1.2→否定短语转换；与 R1.5"by doing→to do"同族，方向相反（此处 V-ing→原形）。与 A1「原词直取」冲突：按「care」的形态填，to/介词位不成立；优先级：A3 空位词性/形态 ＞ A1。本空特有依据：about 前可填 worry/care/think/complain 四个，只有原文那个对。
10 自检：①need to 后要什么形态？②原文是 without+什么？③about 搭得上吗？

**40｜larger**
1 表"High-speed railway｜The high-speed railway network in China is ____40____ than that in any other country."；原文"China has the largest high-speed railway network in the world at present."。空在 is 与 than 之间，比较级位。
2 候选：larger（largest 的比较级转换）；bigger；longer；the largest；more。
3 larger→"is larger than that in any other country"，最高级 the largest in the world 与比较级 larger than any other 等价，成立。bigger→义近，但原文 largest（规模），且 bigger 口语。longer→偏"长度"。the largest→"is the largest than"不成立。more→缺形容词。
4 干扰：原文 largest 是最高级，诱人直接填 the largest；"network"又诱人填 longer（线路长）。
5 意图：考"最高级↔比较级 any other 的等价改写"，分"照抄最高级"者。
6 边界：若空位是"is ____ network in the world"填 the largest（最高级）；有 than 就必须比较级。than 是硬信号。
7 错：the largest（与 than 冲突）；largest（缺 the，且与 than 冲突）；longer（义偏）。
8 R11.5 若句中出现 than (any other)，空位必为比较级；原文最高级（the largest in the world）须转为比较级（larger than any other）。适用条件：句中出现 than (any other)。例外（本空错误路径）：填 the largest（与 than 冲突）；错在把「句中出现 than (any other)」当成唯一情形，一旦空位的词性/位置换成最高级，答案就要从「larger」换成「the largest」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9 04→RC-1.2→比较结构转换；与 R2.4"as...as 原级"构成比较三态（原级/比较级/最高级）的判别表。与 A6「词性/同义转换」冲突：取「bigger」属于同义转写，不是原文对应；优先级：A1 原文唯一对应 ＞ A6。本空特有依据：原文 largest 是最高级，诱人直接填 the largest。
10 自检：①句中有 than 吗？②原文是最高级吗？③比较级形式对吗？

**组小结（G11 · 小蓝书预测卷二）**
- 考点分布：形容词转副词 1（36）；动词转名词（of 结构）1（37）；介词改写仍取原名词 1（38）；without V-ing→need to V 1（39）；最高级转比较级 1（40）。
- 规则：R11.1 修饰动词用副词（36）；R11.2 the ___ of 填名词（37）；R11.3 介词改写不换名词、their 后复数（38）；R11.4 否定短语转换后动词还原（39）；R11.5 than 必比较级（40）。
- 冲突与优先级：R11.1/R11.2/R11.5 与"照抄原文词形"冲突，空位形态优先；R11.3 与"同段对比词"冲突，主语归属优先。
- 未归类：无。
- 覆盖检查：本篇补上"比较三态"与"介词改写"，与 G9 的四种转换合起来，词形转换类基本齐备。

### 2024湖北专升本小蓝书预测卷二十 · Passage 5

**When a thought has found words**

Poetry is the forgotten child of literature. Few people read it for pleasure. In Western high schools, poetry is seldom taught because it is considered to be out of date and have little to do with the life of today's students.

However, compared with the situation in the West, poetry is still an important part of the curriculum (课程) in China. Recently, the Ministry of Education has increased the number of ancient Chinese poems for students to memorize and recite, from 14 to 72. Why is it important to learn poems?

First of all, poetry is a necessary part of learning traditional Chinese culture. It is a path to understanding your history and your society. It is also the key to understanding the thoughts and feelings that are common to everyone but that we may be unable to express, the joy of Li Bai dancing with the moon, for example. Everyone has feelings of joy, love, loneliness, sadness and even anger, and a good poem can put those feelings into words and bring us self-understanding.

Poems can also express beauty. In a few short lines, even something common can become beautiful. Here is a poem called *Fog* by Carl Sandberg: The fog comes/on silent haunches ( 弓腰 ) /and then moves on. Yes, a fog does move smoothly, silently and like a cat, and Sandberg catches that feeling and image, and makes it beautiful.

Of course, to really enjoy poetry, it has to be read aloud. After all, a poem is really just a song without music. Most ancient poems, like Homer's *Epics* ( 《荷马史诗》 ) and China's *Book of Songs*, were spoken for hundreds of years before they were written.

The American poet Robert Frost said, "Poetry is when an emotion has found its thought and the thought has found words." You are lucky that you now have seventy-two poems to learn!

| **Title: When a thought has found words** | |
| --- | --- |
| **Outline** | **Detailed information** |
| ______36______ | In the West, poetry is considered to be ______ 37 from the life of today's students. |
| | In China, poetry remains an important part of the curriculum. |



| **Outline** | **Detailed information** |
| --- | --- |
| ______38______ for learning poetry | ◇Poems help you ______39______ history and society.<br>◇Poems help express your personal thoughts and feelings.<br>◇Poems can express beauty of common things with very few lines. |
| A ______40______ on enjoying poetry | Reading aloud is the best way to enjoy poetry. In fact, many ancient poems were spoken before they were written. |

**〔G12〕小蓝书预测卷二十 · Passage 5（36—40）十层研究**

**36｜Situations（Different situations 亦可，开放空）**
1 表第一个 Outline 行"____36____"，右栏"西：诗歌被认为过时；中：仍是课程重要部分"——一个"两地对比"的概括行。
2 候选：Situations（原文 the situation in the West）；Differences；Poetry；Attitudes。
3 Situations→概括"西方/中国"两种处境，与原文"compared with the situation in the West"对应。Differences→强调"差异"，但右栏还写了中国的情况，不只差异。Poetry→太泛，且标题已有。Attitudes→只覆盖"被认为过时"这一态度面。
4 干扰：右栏两行都在写"地位/态度"，诱人填 Attitudes；原文 situation 只出现一次且带定冠词，学生不敢用。
5 意图：考"对比行标题要覆盖左右两栏"，分"抓一半就概括"者。
6 边界：若右栏只有"西方"一行，可填 Situation in the West；若只有"差异"，填 Differences。
7 错：Attitudes（以偏概全）；Poetry（与标题重复）；Situation（单数，与两地不称）。
8 R12.1 对比型行标题的概括空，须同时覆盖对比双方，取原文对比句中的上位名词（situation）；不取只覆盖一方的词。适用条件：空位是栏头/标题的概括位，且须统摄下辖各行。例外（本空错误路径）：填 Attitudes（以偏概全）；错在把「空位是栏头/标题的概括位，且须统摄下辖各行」当成唯一情形，换到那种位置时，入空的恰恰是「Situation in the West」，而不是「Situations」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9 04→RC-1.3 概括归纳→对比行标题；与 R9.1"自造上位词"同族，此处有原文词可抄。与 A1「原文唯一对应词优先」冲突：抄「Differences」会概括不住下辖各行；优先级：A8 栏头/标题概括 ＞ A1。本空特有依据：右栏两行都在写"地位/态度"，诱人填 Attitudes。
10 自检：①这一行覆盖几方？②原文对比句的名词是哪个？③我的词能覆盖双方吗？

**37｜far**
1 表"In the West, poetry is considered to be ____37____ from the life of today’s students."；原文"it is considered to be out of date and have little to do with the life of today’s students"。空在 be 与 from 之间，形容词/副词位。
2 候选：far（far from＝远离）；different（different from）；away（away from）；separate（separate from）。
3 far→"be far from the life"＝have little to do with，义合，far from 为固定搭配。different→"different from"语法成立，但只表"不同"，未表"脱节"。away→"be away from"表"不在场"，与"脱节"义不合。separate→"separate from"可通，义偏"分离"，且原文无。
4 干扰：from 前最顺的是 different（different from 高频），诱人；原文 out of date 又诱人直接填 out of date（超 1 空、且不与 from 搭配）。
5 意图：考"意译压缩（have little to do with→be far from）"，分"按 from 的惯性搭配填 different"者。
6 边界：若空位是"considered to be ____"（无 from）填 out of date；有 from 则须是能与 from 搭配的词。from 是硬信号。
7 错：different（搭配对、语义弱）；away（义错）；out of date（与 from 不搭，且多词）。
8 R12.2 若原文用"have little to do with / be unrelated to"，表格改写为"be ___ from"，填 far（far from）；先由 from 定搭配，再由原句定义。适用条件：原文用"have little to do with / be unrelated to"。例外（本空错误路径）：填 different（搭配对、语义弱）；错在把「原文用"have little to do with / be unrelated to"」当成唯一情形，一旦空位的词性/位置换成无 from，答案就要从「far」换成「out of date」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9 04→RC-1.2 同义改写→意译压缩；与 R9.2 同族（短语→单词）。与 A1「原词直取」冲突：直取「different」词性不合，空位要的是另一类词；优先级：A3 空位词性 ＞ A1。本空特有依据：from 前最顺的是 different（different from 高频），诱人。
10 自检：①空后是 from 吗？②原文这句什么意思？③哪个词与 from 搭配且表"脱节"？

**38｜Reasons**
1 表"____38____ for learning poetry"，下辖三条"Poems help you…／express…／express beauty…"；原文"Why is it important to learn poems?"后接 First of all…。
2 候选：Reasons（原文 Why 问句的回答）；Importance；Benefits；Purposes；Ways。
3 Reasons→"Reasons for learning poetry"＋for+动名词，与下辖三条"为什么学"对应，成立。Importance→"Importance for learning"搭配错（importance of）。Benefits→"Benefits for learning"可通，但下辖是"为什么重要"的理由，义近。Purposes→"Purposes for"搭配弱。Ways→下辖不是方法。
4 干扰：原文"Why is it important"里的 important 诱人填 Importance；三条都以 Poems help 开头，诱人填 Benefits。
5 意图：考"问句→栏头名词"，分"抄 important"者。
6 边界：若栏头是"____ of learning poetry"填 Importance（of 搭配）；for 决定用 Reasons。
7 错：Importance（for 搭配错）；Benefit（单数＋搭配弱）；Reason（单数，与三条不称）。
8 R12.3 若栏头空后是 for + V-ing，填 Reasons/Purposes 一类可数复数名词；原文的 Why 问句即是栏头的来源。适用条件：栏头空后是 for + V-ing。例外（本空错误路径）：填 Importance（for 搭配错）；错在把「栏头空后是 for + V-ing」当成唯一情形，一旦空位的词性/位置换成of 搭配，答案就要从「Reasons」换成「Importance」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9 04→RC-1.3→问句转栏头；与 R8.2"topic sentence 取词"并列。与 A1「原文唯一对应词优先」冲突：用「Importance」当标题，覆盖不了全部行；优先级：A8 栏头/标题概括 ＞ A1。本空特有依据：原文"Why is it important"里的 important 诱人填 Importance。
10 自检：①空后是 for 还是 of？②原文有没有 Why 问句？③复数吗？

**39｜understand**
1 表"Poems help you ____39____ history and society."；原文"It is a path to understanding your history and your society."。空在 help you 后，动词原形位。
2 候选：understand（understanding 的原形）；learn；know；study；realize。
3 understand→help you understand，与 understanding 对应，原形成立。learn→"learn history"搭配更常见，但原文是 understanding，且 learn 偏"学习"而非"理解"。know→可通，义偏"知道"。study→可通，义偏"研究"。realize→"realize history"搭配弱。
4 干扰：history and society 与 learn 是超高频搭配，诱人填 learn；help sb do 又让学生犹豫是否要 to。
5 意图：考"动名词→原形＋不按搭配直觉换词"，分"见 history 就填 learn"者。
6 边界：若空位是"a path to ____ history"填 understanding（介词 to 后 -ing）；help sb 后则原形。
7 错：learn（搭配直觉替换原文）；understanding（help sb 后误用 -ing）；to understand（help sb 后不加 to）。
8 R12.4 若空位在 help sb 之后，填动词原形；原文的动名词（understanding）须还原为原形，且不因搭配高频而换词。适用条件：空位在 help sb 之后。例外：若空位所在结构换成另一形态位（to 后→原形、介词后→-ing）；此时本条的适用前提不再成立，入空的就不再是「understand」这一路，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9 04→RC-1.2→动名词还原；与 R9.5 同族（help sb 触发）。与 A1「原词直取」冲突：「learn」的形态与本空要求差一层（-ing/原形/过去式）；优先级：A3 空位词性/形态 ＞ A1。本空特有依据：history and society 与 learn 是超高频搭配，诱人填 learn。
10 自检：①help sb 后要不要 to？②原文是 -ing 还是原形？③我是否按搭配换了词？

**40｜tip（suggestion 亦可，开放空）**
1 表"A ____40____ on enjoying poetry｜Reading aloud is the best way to enjoy poetry."；原文"Of course, to really enjoy poetry, it has to be read aloud."。空在 A 与 on 之间，单数名词位。
2 候选：tip（建议）；suggestion；note；lesson；way。
3 tip→"A tip on enjoying poetry"搭配成立，与"朗读最佳"这一建议对应。suggestion→"A suggestion on"可通，稍正式。note→"A note on"意为"关于…的说明"，义偏。lesson→"A lesson on"意为"一课"，义偏。way→与右栏"the best way"重复。
4 干扰：右栏"the best way to enjoy poetry"里的 way 就在眼前，诱人填 way；note 与 on 搭配也常见。
5 意图：考"建议类栏头词"，分"抄右栏 way"者。本题为开放空，tip/suggestion 均可。
6 边界：若栏头是"A ____ of enjoying poetry"填 way（way of doing）；on 决定用 tip/suggestion。
7 错：way（重复右栏，且 way on 搭配错）；advices（不可数）；Tips（复数与 A 冲突）。
8 R12.5 建议类栏头空，取 tip/suggestion/advice（不可数）一类词；介词 on 提示"关于…的建议"，of 则提示 way。适用条件：空位是栏头/标题的概括位，且须统摄下辖各行。例外（本空错误路径）：填 way（重复右栏，且 way on 搭配错）；错在把「空位是栏头/标题的概括位，且须统摄下辖各行」当成唯一情形，换到那种位置时，入空的恰恰是「way」，而不是「tip」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9 04→RC-1.3→栏头建议词；开放空，答案不唯一。与 A1「原文唯一对应词优先」冲突：原文没有可直接抄的上位词，「suggestion」只是下位词或干扰；优先级：A8 栏头/标题概括 ＞ A1。本空特有依据：右栏"the best way to enjoy poetry"里的 way 就在眼前，诱人填 way。
10 自检：①空后是 on 还是 of？②右栏是否已出现该词？③A 后是单数吗？

**组小结（G12 · 小蓝书预测卷二十）**
- 考点分布：对比行标题 1（36，开放）；意译压缩 far from 1（37）；Why 问句转栏头 1（38）；动名词还原 1（39）；建议栏头 1（40，开放）。
- 规则：R12.1 对比行标题须覆盖双方（36）；R12.2 have little to do with→be far from（37）；R12.3 for+V-ing 栏头用 Reasons（38）；R12.4 help sb 后原形、不换词（39）；R12.5 建议栏头取 tip/suggestion（40）。
- 冲突与优先级：R12.2 与"from 的惯性搭配（different）"冲突，原句定义优先；R12.4 与"高频搭配（learn history）"冲突，原文词优先。
- 未归类：36、40 为开放空，答案不唯一，单列。
- 覆盖检查：本篇补上"意译压缩"与"问句转栏头"，并第二次出现开放空（36、40），说明模拟卷的栏头空普遍不唯一。

### 2024湖北专升本小蓝书预测卷二十一 · Passage 5

Nowadays, many people have realized the importance of leadership skills. Poor leaders may cause many problems. But good leaders do better in solving problems than poor ones. Without good leaders, problems can't be solved. Therefore, good leaders are very important.

What's more, good leaders are good at working with others. Usually they are good listeners as well. But being good leaders isn't easy. So let me give you some suggestions to help you become a good leader.

Learn from books. Maybe nobody teaches you how to be a good leader quickly, but you can learn it on your own. Read biographies of great leaders from home and abroad. By reading, you can know that great leaders experience a lot and have their secrets of success. You can learn a lot from them. Then you will be more confident of being a good leader.

Learn from student leaders in your school. There is no doubt that those leaders who are popular with students in your school must have their advantages over others. Don't you want to know why they are so successful? Take an active part in the activities organized by the excellent leaders and learn from them at one. Then you will know the answer.

Practice what you have learned. Find the students that have the same interests as you have and start a club. Activities should be organized regularly in the club. When the members in your club have trouble in solving problems, you should communicate with them and find out the causes of the problems. After problems are solved, you can share pleasure with them.



As time passes by, you will get more trust like those popular leaders. Sooner or later, you will be a good leader.

In short, being a good leader needs courage and practice. Get started before it's too late! As long as keep trying, you will develop your leadership skills soon.

| **How to be a good leader** | |
| --- | --- |
| **Roles** | A good leader is a problem solver, a good partner, a good ______36______ and a good organizer. |
| **Suggestions** | ·Learn from books. Reading biographies of great leader is a quick way for you to lean something useful from their experiences and ______37______ of success.<br>·Learn from ______38______ student leaders who often organize activities for others.<br>·Practice what you have learned. You will get ______39______ easily like those popular leaders if you set up a club to practice what you have learned and hold some activities. |
| **conclusion** | You will become a successful leader as long as you stick to ______40______. |

**〔G13〕小蓝书预测卷二十一 · Passage 5（36—40）十层研究**

**36｜listener**
1 表"Roles｜A good leader is a problem solver, a good partner, a good ____36____ and a good organizer."；原文"good leaders are good at working with others. Usually they are good listeners as well."。空在 a good 与 and 之间，单数名词位，与 solver/partner/organizer 并列。
2 候选：listener（原文 listeners 的单数）；speaker；teacher；friend。
3 listener→a good listener 与 good listeners 对应，单数配 a，与前三项同为"角色名词"，成立。speaker→与 listeners 反义，无据。teacher→无据。friend→可通，但原文 role 清单里是 listener。
4 干扰：上文"working with others"诱人填 partner（已给）；"good at working with others"又诱人填 teammate。
5 意图：考"并列角色清单取原文名词＋单数化"，分"见 a good 就按语义填 friend"者。
6 边界：若并列项用复数（good ____s）填 listeners；若"a good ____ of others"填 listener（仍单数）。
7 错：listeners（与 a 冲突）；listening（-ing 不是角色名词）；friend（语义替换）。
8 R13.1 并列角色清单（a solver, a partner, a ___）中，空位取原文同一清单的名词并单数化；清单外的近义词不作候选。适用条件：空位词性与原文对应词一致，且题面无改写。例外：若空位改由另一词性承担（名词位换动词/形容词位）；此时本条的适用前提不再成立，入空的就不再是「listener」这一路，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9 04→RC-1.1→并列角色子类；与 R3.1"定语取词"共用一个前提：先定词性，再取原文。与 A6「词性/同义转换」冲突：把「speaker」当同义替换，会改掉原文用词；优先级：A1 原文唯一对应 ＞ A6。本空特有依据：上文"working with others"诱人填 partner（已给）。
10 自检：①与哪几项并列？②原文是单数还是复数？③我填的是角色名词吗？

**37｜secrets**
1 表"…learn something useful from their experiences and ____37____ of success"；原文"great leaders experience a lot and have their secrets of success"。空与 experiences 并列，复数名词位。
2 候选：secrets（原文）；keys；ways；stories。
3 secrets→their secrets of success 逐字对应，复数，成立。keys→"keys of success"搭配应为 key to，且原文 secrets。ways→"ways of success"可通，原文 secrets。stories→无据。
4 干扰：of success 前最顺的是 key/secret/way，三个都常见；上句 experiences 诱人再填一个复数抽象名词。
5 意图：考"of 短语取原文中心词"，分"按 of success 习惯搭配填 key"者。
6 边界：若空位是"the ____ to success"填 key（key to）；of 决定用 secrets/ways。
7 错：key（介词搭配应为 to）；secret（单数，与 experiences 并列不称）；success（与 of 重复）。
8 R13.2 若空位与已给复数名词并列且后接 of + 抽象名词，填原文该 of 短语的中心名词，保持复数；注意 key to / secret of 的介词分工。适用条件：空位与已给复数名词并列且后接 of + 抽象名词。例外（本空错误路径）：填 key（介词搭配应为 to）；错在把「空位与已给复数名词并列且后接 of + 抽象名词」当成唯一情形，一旦空位的词性/位置换成key to，答案就要从「secrets」换成「key」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9 04→RC-1.1→of 短语子类；与 R11.2"the ___ of 填名词"同族，方向相反（这里空在 of 前）。与 A6「词性/同义转换」冲突：把「keys」当同义替换会改掉原文用词；优先级：A1 原文唯一对应 ＞ A6。本空特有依据：of success 前最顺的是 key/secret/way，三个都常见。
10 自检：①of 前要名词吗？②原文用 secrets 还是 keys？③复数对吗？

**38｜excellent**
1 表"Learn from ____38____ student leaders who often organize activities for others."；原文"Take an active part in the activities organized by the excellent leaders and learn from them"。空在 Learn from 与 student leaders 之间，形容词位。
2 候选：excellent（原文）；school（上文 in your school）；popular（另一句）；young。
3 excellent→"excellent student leaders"与 the excellent leaders 对应，且 who often organize activities 正是 excellent 的证据，成立。school→"school student leaders"可通，但下文已限定 student leaders，且原文形容词是 excellent。popular→原文"those leaders who are popular with students"是另一句的定语，指"受欢迎"，与本句"组织活动"不完全对应。young→无据。
4 干扰：小标题"Learn from student leaders in your school"里的 school 最近，诱人填 school；"popular with students"也诱人填 popular。
5 意图：考"同一段两个形容词（popular/excellent）的分工"，分"就近取 school"者。
6 边界：若空位是"student leaders in your ____"填 school；若"leaders who are ____ with students"填 popular。空位后的名词与介词决定取哪句的定语。
7 错：school（取地点而非评价）；popular（取另一句定语）；excellently（副词）。
8 R13.3 若空位是名词前的形容词位，回原文找"修饰同一名词"的形容词；同段若有多个形容词，按空后名词的搭配与从句证据（who organize activities）选。适用条件：空位是名词前的形容词位。例外（本空错误路径）：填 school（取地点而非评价）；错在把「空位是名词前的形容词位」当成唯一情形，换到那种位置时，入空的恰恰是「school」，而不是「excellent」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9 04→RC-1.1→定语子类；与 R3.1 同族，多了一层"同段多定语"的辨析。与 A6「词性/同义转换」冲突：按「school」改写，词性/搭配与原位不合；优先级：A1 原文唯一对应 ＞ A6。本空特有依据：小标题"Learn from student leaders in your school"里的 school 最近，诱人填 school。
10 自检：①空后名词是什么？②原文修饰它的形容词是哪个？③是否取了另一句的形容词？

**39｜trust**
1 表"…You will get ____39____ easily like those popular leaders if you set up a club…"；原文"As time passes by, you will get more trust like those popular leaders."。空在 get 之后、easily 之前，名词位。
2 候选：trust（原文）；respect；support；praise。
3 trust→get trust＝获得信任，与 get more trust 对应，成立。respect→"get respect"可通，原文 trust。support→可通，原文无。praise→可通，义偏"表扬"。
4 干扰：get 后可接 trust/respect/support/praise 一串抽象名词；"like those popular leaders"又诱人填 praise（受欢迎＝被表扬）。
5 意图：考"get＋抽象名词取原文"，分"按语义任选一个"者。
6 边界：若空位是"win ____"填 trust/respect 均可，仍须原文；若"get more ____"（有 more）填不可数名词。
7 错：respect（同义替换）；trusted（动词/分词）；trusts（不可数）。
8 R13.4 若空位紧跟 get/win/gain 且后接抽象名词，回原文取该动词所带的唯一名词；同义抽象名词不作候选。适用条件：空位紧跟 get/win/gain 且后接抽象名词。例外（本空错误路径）：填 respect（同义替换）；错在把「空位紧跟 get/win/gain 且后接抽象名词」当成唯一情形，一旦空位的词性/位置换成有 more，答案就要从「trust」换成「不可数名词」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9 04→RC-1.1→动宾抽象名词；与 R7.4"develop 后抽象名词"同族。与 A6「词性/同义转换」冲突：换「respect」更顺口，但原文用的是另一个词；优先级：A1 原文唯一对应 ＞ A6。本空特有依据：get 后可接 trust/respect/support/praise 一串抽象名词。
10 自检：①动词是 get 吗？②原文带的是哪个名词？③可数吗？

**40｜trying**
1 表"conclusion｜You will become a successful leader as long as you stick to ____40____."；原文"In short, being a good leader needs courage and practice… As long as keep trying, you will develop your leadership skills soon."。空在 stick to 之后，V-ing/名词位。
2 候选：trying（原文 keep trying）；practice（同段 needs courage and practice）；learning；efforts。
3 trying→stick to trying＝坚持尝试，与 As long as keep trying 对应，stick to 后接 -ing，成立。practice→"stick to practice"语法亦通，且同段有 needs practice，但原文的条件句是 keep trying。learning→"stick to learning"可通，原文无。efforts→"stick to efforts"搭配弱。
4 干扰：同段"needs courage and practice"就在前一句，practice 是最强诱饵；stick to 后又常接名词（stick to the plan），诱人填名词。
5 意图：考"条件句取原文＋stick to 后 -ing"，分"取上句 practice"者。
6 边界：若空位是"needs courage and ____"填 practice；若"as long as you keep ____"填 trying。空位前的动词（stick to / keep）决定取哪句。
7 错：practice（取上句，非条件句）；try（stick to 后须 -ing）；to try（重复 to）。
8 R13.5 若空位在 stick to / keep 之后，填原文条件句中的动作；stick to 后接名词或 -ing，不接原形。同段若有多个"做法"名词，以空位所在句的原文为准。适用条件：空位在 stick to / keep 之后。例外（本空错误路径）：填 practice（取上句，非条件句）；错在把「空位在 stick to / keep 之后」当成唯一情形，换到那种位置时，入空的恰恰是「practice」，而不是「trying」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9 04→RC-1.2→条件句取词；与 R4.5"名词后 to"区分：此处 to 是介词。与 A1「原词直取」冲突：直取「practice」保留原形态，与空前结构不合；优先级：A3 空位词性/形态 ＞ A1。本空特有依据：同段"needs courage and practice"就在前一句，practice 是最强诱饵。
10 自检：①stick to 后接什么形式？②原文条件句是哪个动作？③是否取了上句的 practice？

**组小结（G13 · 小蓝书预测卷二十一）**
- 考点分布：并列角色单数化 1（36）；of 短语中心词 1（37）；同段多定语辨析 1（38）；get＋抽象名词 1（39）；stick to 后 -ing 1（40）。
- 规则：R13.1 并列角色清单取原文并单数化（36）；R13.2 of 前取中心名词、复数（37）；R13.3 同段多定语按空后名词与从句证据选（38）；R13.4 get 后取原文唯一抽象名词（39）；R13.5 stick to 后接 -ing、取条件句动作（40）。
- 冲突与优先级：R13.3 与"就近取词"冲突，空后名词优先；R13.5 与"上句词优先"冲突，空位所在句优先。
- 未归类：无。
- 覆盖检查：本篇补上"同段多定语"与"介词 to 后 -ing"，是词形类规则的收口。

### 2024湖北专升本小蓝书预测卷二十三 · Passage 5

Experts say that the ability of dogs to smell is one million times stronger than that of humans. With so much sniff (闻) power, it's hardly surprising that they stick their heads out of car windows. They don't care about the scenery. What they're after are the smells.

When dogs put their faces into the wind, their upper lips and noses are sniffing, their eyes partly closed, and their ears folded back. It looks as if they're enjoying themselves very much, but mainly they're concentrating. It's as though they're closing down all the rest of their senses to concentrate on this one. Every dog loves to hang his head out of the window, and all dogs have an excellent sense of smell. This ability is increased when they are moving quickly, which is one reason that they take advantage of open car windows. It also probably makes them excellent hunters.

Smell is so important to dogs that they have two systems for it. One is in the nose, which consists of a huge amount of tissue (组织) with scent receptors (嗅觉感受器). This area takes up about 1/2 square inch in humans, but up to 20 square inches in some dogs. As air moves over the tissue, odor molecules (气味分子) reach the millions of scent receptors. The more air flowing there is, the better dogs smell. So they love to put their faces into the wind.

Dogs have a second system in their mouths, where a tiny pipe leads to Jacobson's organ (犁鼻器). Its task is to catch different types of smells. Dogs depend on it to find the sources of smells.

| **Title** | **Dogs' Sense of Smell** | |
| --- | --- | --- |
| The most powerful sense dogs have is that of smell. | a. All dogs love to hang their heads out of car windows and put their faces into the wind. It seems as if they're just enjoying themselves, but actually they're ______36______ on the smells.<br>b. All dogs have an excellent sense of smell, and this ability is ______37______ when they are moving quickly. Maybe this is why they are good ______38______. | |
| The two smelling systems in dogs | The one in their ______39______ | Their noses have a huge amount of tissue with ______40______ of scent receptors. It covers as much as 20 square inches in some dogs. Odor molecules reach the receptors as air moves over the tissue. |



|  | The one in their mouths | A tiny pipe in the mouth leads to Jacobson's organ, which dogs depend on to determine types of smells, and find their sources of smells. |

**〔G14〕小蓝书预测卷二十三 · Passage 5（36—40）十层研究**

**36｜concentrating**
1 表"…It seems as if they’re just enjoying themselves, but actually they’re ____36____ on the smells."；原文"but mainly they’re concentrating… to concentrate on this one"。空在 be 与 on 之间，V-ing 位。
2 候选：concentrating（原文）；focusing（同义）；interested（搭配错）；keen（搭配对但无据）。
3 concentrating→are concentrating on 与原文一致，进行时成立。focusing→"focusing on"同义，原文 concentrating。interested→"interested on"搭配错（应为 in）。keen→"keen on"搭配对，但原文无。
4 干扰：seems as if…but actually 的转折诱人填 enjoying（已给）；on 前最顺的是 focus/concentrate，两者都成立。
5 意图：考"be+on 结构取原文 V-ing"，分"按 on 的常见搭配填 focusing"者。
6 边界：若空位是"____ on the smells"（无 be）填 Concentrating（句首）或原形；be 决定 -ing。若"are ____ in the smells"填 interested。
7 错：focusing（同义替换）；interested（介词错）；concentrate（be 后缺 -ing）。
8 R14.1 若空位在 be 与 on 之间，填 V-ing；回原文取该介词所带的动词，不按 on 的高频搭配换词。适用条件：空位在 be 与 on 之间。例外（本空错误路径）：填 focusing（同义替换）；错在把「空位在 be 与 on 之间」当成唯一情形，一旦空位的词性/位置换成无 be，答案就要从「concentrating」换成「Concentrating」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9 04→RC-1.1→V-ing 子类；与 R2.1"be+副词后 V-ing"同族，此处由 on 定搭配。与 A1「原词直取」冲突：按「focusing」的形态填，to/介词位不成立；优先级：A3 空位词性/形态 ＞ A1。本空特有依据：seems as if…but actually 的转折诱人填 enjoying（已给）。
10 自检：①be 后要 -ing 吗？②原文动词是哪个？③介词是 on 还是 in？

**37｜increased**
1 表"…this ability is ____37____ when they are moving quickly."；原文"This ability is increased when they are moving quickly."。空在 is 与 when 之间，过去分词位。
2 候选：increased（原文）；improved（同义）；stronger（形容词）；developed（无据）。
3 increased→is increased 被动/系表，与原文一致。improved→"is improved"可通，原文 increased。stronger→"is stronger"语法通，但原文强调"被增强"这一变化。developed→无据。
4 干扰：中文"嗅觉更强"诱人填 stronger；improve 与 increase 近义，诱人替换。
5 意图：考"被动/系表取原文分词"，分"按中文'更强'填 stronger"者。
6 边界：若空位是"this ability is much ____"填 stronger（形容词，无 by/when 变化义）；有 when 从句表变化时用 increased。
7 错：stronger（形容词，丢"变化"义）；increasing（语态错）；improve（原形）。
8 R14.2 若空位在 is 与 when/because 从句之间表"被增强"，填过去分词；形容词只用于静态比较。适用条件：空位在 is 与 when/because 从句之间表"被增强"。例外（本空错误路径）：填 stronger（形容词，丢"变化"义）；错在把「空位在 is 与 when/because 从句之间表"被增强"」当成唯一情形，换到那种位置时，入空的恰恰是「stronger」，而不是「increased」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9 04→RC-1.2→语态/分词子类；与 R7.3"be+to 人→过去分词"同族。与 A1「原文唯一对应词优先」冲突：按「improved」的语态填，主谓关系不成立；优先级：A18 被动↔主动 ＞ A1 直取。本空特有依据：中文"嗅觉更强"诱人填 stronger。
10 自检：①is 后是分词还是形容词？②原文用 increase 还是 strong？③有无"变化"义？

**38｜hunters**
1 表"Maybe this is why they are good ____38____."；原文"It also probably makes them excellent hunters."。空在 good 之后，复数名词位。
2 候选：hunters（原文）；trackers；helpers；smellers。
3 hunters→good hunters 与 excellent hunters 对应，复数，成立。trackers→"good trackers"可通，原文 hunters。helpers→无据。smellers→生造词。
4 干扰：全篇讲"闻气味"，诱人填 smellers/trackers；good 后接 -er 名词的惯性也强。
5 意图：考"评价句取原文名词"，分"按主题自造 -er 词"者。
6 边界：若空位是"good at ____"填 hunting/tracking（-ing）；有 at 用 -ing，无 at 用名词。
7 错：trackers（同义替换）；hunter（单数，与 they 不称）；hunting（good at hunting 才用）。
8 R14.3 若空位是"be good ____"且无 at，填复数名词（人/动物类）；有 at 则填 -ing。适用条件：空位是"be good ____"且无 at。例外（本空错误路径）：填 trackers（同义替换）；错在把「空位是"be good ____"且无 at」当成唯一情形，一旦空位的词性/位置换成-ing，答案就要从「hunters」换成「hunting/tracking」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9 04→RC-1.1→评价句名词；与 R13.1"并列角色"同族。与 A1「原文唯一对应词优先」冲突：直取「trackers」沿用原文单复数，与栏头/并列不合；优先级：A5 单复数独立判定 ＞ A1。
10 自检：①good 后有没有 at？②原文名词是哪个？③复数吗？

**39｜noses**
1 表"The two smelling systems in dogs｜The one in their ____39____"；原文"One is in the nose… Dogs have a second system in their mouths"。空在 their 之后，复数名词位，与下一行"the one in their mouths"对举。
2 候选：noses（原文 nose 的复数）；nose（单数）；mouths（第二系统）；heads。
3 noses→the one in their noses，与 in the nose 对应，their 后复数，且与 mouths 对举，成立。nose→"in their nose"数不一致。mouths→是第二系统，下一行已用。heads→无据。
4 干扰：下一行 mouths 就在表内，诱人错位；原文用单数 the nose，诱人填 nose。
5 意图：考"单复数随 their 调整＋两系统不串位"，分"照抄单数 nose"者。
6 边界：若空位是"in the ____"（无 their）填 nose（单数）；有 their 用复数。
7 错：nose（数不一致）；mouths（串位）；nose’s（所有格）。
8 R14.4 若原文用"in the + 单数"，表格改为"in their + ___"，名词须变复数；表格并列的两行不得互换所指。适用条件：原文用"in the + 单数"。例外（本空错误路径）：填 nose（数不一致）；错在把「原文用"in the + 单数"」当成唯一情形，一旦空位的词性/位置换成无 their，答案就要从「noses」换成「nose」，本条不成立，改按 A5（单复数独立判定）。
9 04→RC-1.1→数的一致；与 R2.2"be 动词定单复数"同族，此处由物主代词定。与 A6「词性/同义转换」冲突：把「mouths」当同义替换会改掉原文用词；优先级：A1 原文唯一对应 ＞ A6。本空特有依据：下一行 mouths 就在表内，诱人错位。
10 自检：①前面是 their 吗？②原文单数还是复数？③是否与下一行串位？

**40｜millions**
1 表"Their noses have a huge amount of tissue with ____40____ of scent receptors."；原文"reach the millions of scent receptors"。空在 with 与 of 之间，数量词位。
2 候选：millions（原文）；thousands；amounts；numbers。
3 millions→the millions of scent receptors 逐字对应，成立。thousands→无据（数量级不符）。amounts→"amounts of scent receptors"可通，但受体可数，且原文 millions。numbers→"numbers of"可通，原文 millions。
4 干扰：a huge amount of tissue 就在前半句，诱人重复 amount；of 前可填 lots/numbers/millions，只有原文那个对。
5 意图：考"数量级取原文＋amount/number 的可数性"，分"抄前半句 amount"者。
6 边界：若空位是"a huge ____ of tissue"填 amount（不可数）；此处 of 后是 receptors（可数），不能用 amount。
7 错：amounts（可数性错）；thousands（数量级错）；million（单数，与 of 搭配需 millions of）。
8 R14.5 若空位是"___ of + 可数复数"，用 millions/thousands/numbers，不用 amount；数量级以原文为准。适用条件：空位是"___ of + 可数复数"。例外（本空错误路径）：填 amounts（可数性错）；错在把「空位是"___ of + 可数复数"」当成唯一情形，一旦空位的词性/位置换成不可数，答案就要从「millions」换成「amount」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9 04→RC-1.4→数量词；与 R6.4"amount/number"同族，可数性优先。与 A6「词性/同义转换」冲突：取「thousands」属于同义转写，不是原文对应；优先级：A1 原文唯一对应 ＞ A6。本空特有依据：a huge amount of tissue 就在前半句，诱人重复 amount。
10 自检：①of 后名词可数吗？②原文数量级是多少？③millions of 的 s 有吗？

**组小结（G14 · 小蓝书预测卷二十三）**
- 考点分布：be+on 取 V-ing 1（36）；is+when 取过去分词 1（37）；good 后复数名词 1（38）；their 后复数、两行不串位 1（39）；数量级与可数性 1（40）。
- 规则：R14.1 be 与 on 之间填 V-ing（36）；R14.2 表"变化"用过去分词（37）；R14.3 good 无 at 填名词（38）；R14.4 their 后名词复数（39）；R14.5 ___ of 可数复数用 millions（40）。
- 冲突与优先级：R14.2 与"中文比较级"冲突，变化义优先；R14.4 与"照抄单数"冲突，物主代词优先；R14.5 与"前半句 amount"冲突，of 后名词可数性优先。
- 未归类：无。
- 覆盖检查：本篇把"数/态"两条线（单复数、分词）各补一例，并首次考"两行对举不得串位"。

### 2024湖北专升本小蓝书预测卷二十二 · Passage 5

I love parties. I love planning for them, making the invitations, planning the games and decorating my own cake. The only thing that I don't like is deciding on the guest list. When I was in grade school my mum would tell me the maximum number of friends that I could invite and it was always a number too small for my list. I also liked all different kinds of people. I never quite fit into one of the many little groups at my school, so I just kind of floated around, accumulating friends from various backgrounds. There was an "in" crowd made up of the cool kids who had the power to rule the school — if a cool kid wore two different coloured socks to school one day, everyone thought it was great. But if an uncool kid did the same thing, the other kids would turn away in disgust (厌恶). It made getting dressed in the morning a very scary thing.

Shannon was one of my friends who seemed to unknowingly break the rules of uncoolness all the time. I don't know why she was often targeted, but people found it acceptable to make fun of her. Shannon was a nice girl and wore pretty clothes, but was somewhat overweight and didn't talk much. She was picked on a lot. Whenever there was an strange noise or smell in the classroom the kids would giggle (咯咯笑) and point at her. Shannon never said anything, but it made me feel sick inside. Shannon was innocent, I thought.

One year to celebrate my birthday, my mum told me I could have a party at our house. I struggled for days deciding on which girls to invite. Once I had chosen all the names, I made



the invitations and handed them out to my friends at school.

"Why did you invite Shannon?" asked one of my cool friends when it was discovered that I had invited Shannon. She pressured me to tell her not to come. If I invited Shannon, maybe I would be targeted by children's ridicule. There were other people I could add in her place who would be more acceptable.

阅读短文完成下面的表格

| **5W** | **Details** |
| --- | --- |
| Who | Shannon was one of my friends, but she was often ______36______, and they made fun of her. |
| When | One year to ______37______ my birthday, I wanted to invite Shannon. |
| Where | Shannon was invited to attend my ______38______. |
| What | One of my cool friends suggested that I should not invite ______39______. I was at a loss. |
| Why | I was pressured. If I invited Shannon, maybe I would be targeted by the children's ______40______. |

**〔G15〕小蓝书预测卷二十二 · Passage 5（36—40）十层研究**

**36｜targeted（picked on 亦可，见边界）**
1｜表"Shannon was one of my friends, but she was often ____36____, and they made fun of her."；原文"I don’t know why she was often targeted, but people found it acceptable to make fun of her."。空＝过去分词位。
2｜候选：targeted / picked on / laughed at / ignored。
3｜targeted 与原文"was often targeted"逐字合，被动+often 成立；picked on 亦见于原文"She was picked on a lot"，但与本句"and they made fun of her"的衔接不如 targeted；laughed at 义近无据；ignored 义反。
4｜干扰：下一句"the kids would giggle and point at her"诱人填 laughed at；"didn’t talk much"诱人填 ignored。
5｜意图：考"同义两处（targeted/picked on）取与本句衔接者"，分"见 make fun 就填 laughed at"者。
6｜边界：若空后是 by other kids（有施事），picked on/laugh at 更顺；无施事时 targeted 更稳。本题两答案在原文均有，属半开放。
7｜错：laughing at（缺 was+ed）；ignored（义反）；target（原形）。
8｜R15.1 若原文同一人物有两种近义被动表述，取与表格后半句（and they made fun of her）衔接的那一个；被动语态须补 -ed。适用条件：原文同一人物有两种近义被动表述。例外：若空位是主动谓语位（空前无 be）；此时本条的适用前提不再成立，入空的就不再是「targeted」这一路，本条不成立，改按 A18（被动↔主动）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→被动同义子类；与 R8.5"be+过去分词"同族。与 A1「原文唯一对应词优先」冲突：直取「picked on」保留原文语态，与空位的 be/主动结构不合；优先级：A18 被动↔主动 ＞ A1 直取。本空特有依据：下一句"the kids would giggle and point at her"诱人填 laughed at。
10｜自检：空后有无 by？原文两句哪句更贴"and they…"？-ed 补了吗？

**37｜celebrate**
1｜表"When｜One year to ____37____ my birthday, I wanted to invite Shannon."；原文"One year to celebrate my birthday, my mum told me I could have a party"。空在 to 后，动词原形位。
2｜候选：celebrate / enjoy / have / mark。
3｜填「celebrate」→「When｜One year to celebrate my birthday, I wanted to invite Shannon.」：与原文逐字合，to celebrate my birthday 成立；填「enjoy」→「When｜One year to enjoy my birthday, I wanted to invite Shannon.」：可通但原文 celebrate，不成立；填「have」→「When｜One year to have my birthday, I wanted to invite Shannon.」：搭配不地道，不成立；填「mark」→「When｜One year to mark my birthday, I wanted to invite Shannon.」：可通无据，不成立。回读：空位是题面空位，只有「celebrate」同时满足形态与原文对应。
4｜干扰：下句"have a party"诱人填 have；"my birthday"诱人填 enjoy。
5｜意图：考"to 后原形取原文动词"，分"按中文'过生日'填 have"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"____ a party"填 have（have a party 固定）；表内已给 my birthday，须用 celebrate。
7｜错：celebrating（to 后误 -ing）；have（与 birthday 搭配错）；celebration（名词）。
8｜R15.2 若空位在 to 后且后接 my birthday，填 celebrate（原形）；have 只与 party 搭配。适用条件：空位在 to 后且后接 my birthday。例外（本空错误路径）：填 celebrating（to 后误 -ing）；错在把「空位在 to 后且后接 my birthday」当成唯一情形，换到那种位置时，入空的恰恰是「have」，而不是「celebrate」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→to 后原形；与 R4.5 同族。与 A1「原词直取」冲突：「enjoy」的形态与本空要求差一层（-ing/原形/过去式）；优先级：A3 空位词性/形态 ＞ A1。本空特有依据：下句"have a party"诱人填 have。
10｜自检：to 后原形？原文动词？与 birthday 搭配吗？

**38｜party**
1｜表"Where｜Shannon was invited to attend my ____38____."；原文"my mum told me I could have a party at our house"，且"I had invited Shannon"。空在 my 后，名词位。
2｜候选：party / birthday party / house / celebration。
3｜party 与 have a party 对应，attend my party 成立；birthday party 亦可但原文只说 party；house 是地点，"attend my house"不成立；celebration 义近无据。
4｜干扰：上一空刚考 birthday，诱人重复填 birthday party；"at our house"诱人填 house。
5｜意图：考"Where 行取事件而非地点"，分"把 house 当地点填"者。
6｜边界：若行标题是"Where"，答案应是地点（at our house）；本行问"被邀请参加什么"，故填 party。行标题与空格句的主语共同决定。
7｜错：house（地点≠事件）；birthday（单填不成立）；parties（单数语境）。
8｜R15.3 若行标题与空格句不一致（Where 行却问"参加什么"），以空格句的动词（attend）定答案类型：attend+事件名词。适用条件：行标题与空格句不一致（Where 行却问"参加什么"）。例外（本空错误路径）：填 house（地点≠事件）；错在把「行标题与空格句不一致（Where 行却问"参加什么"）」当成唯一情形，本条的适用前提不再成立，入空的就不再是「party」这一路，本条不成立，改按 A8（栏头/标题概括）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→5W 表格子类；与 R15.4"专名"并列。与 A1「原文唯一对应词优先」冲突：原文没有可直接抄的上位词，「house」只是下位词或干扰；优先级：A8 栏头/标题概括 ＞ A1。本空特有依据：上一空刚考 birthday，诱人重复填 birthday party。
10｜自检：空格句动词是什么？行标题与它一致吗？要事件还是地点？

**39｜Shannon**
1｜表"What｜One of my cool friends suggested that I should not invite ____39____."；原文""Why did you invite Shannon?" asked one of my cool friends… She pressured me to tell her not to come."。空在 invite 后，专名位。
2｜候选：Shannon / her / my cool friend / anyone。
3｜Shannon 与原文 not to come 的对象一致，专名大写，成立；her 指代不明（前文有 cool friend 与 Shannon 两个女性）；anyone 改变语义；my cool friend 是被邀请者之外的施压者。
4｜干扰：前句出现 her（She pressured me to tell her not to come），诱人填 her；表内 Who 行已写 Shannon，诱人以为不能重复。
5｜意图：考"专名回填、代词不代专名"，分"用 her 避重复"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"tell ____ not to come"填 her（宾语位，代词可）；invite 后作答案须用专名以便判卷。
7｜错：her（指代歧义）；shannon（未大写）；Shannon’s（所有格）。
8｜R15.4 表格答案涉及人名时用专名并大写，不用代词；即使同表已出现该名，也照填。适用条件：空位是专名位，且原文以同一书写形式出现。例外（本空错误路径）：填 her（指代歧义）；错在把「空位是专名位，且原文以同一书写形式出现」当成唯一情形，一旦空位的词性/位置换成宾语位，代词可，答案就要从「Shannon」换成「her」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.4→专名提取；与 R5.1"专名保大写"同族。与 A2「整短语不截断」冲突：写「her」会改变原文的书写形式（全称/缩写/连字符）；优先级：A12 专名按原文形式 ＞ A2。本空特有依据：前句出现 her（She pressured me to tell her not to come），诱人填 her。
10｜自检：这个词指谁？大写了吗？代词会不会歧义？

**40｜ridicule**
1｜表"Why｜…maybe I would be targeted by the children’s ____40____."；原文"maybe I would be targeted by children’s ridicule"。空在 children’s 后，名词位。
2｜候选：ridicule / laughter / jokes / bullying。
3｜ridicule 与原文逐字合，children’s ridicule 成立；laughter 可通但原文 ridicule；jokes 可通，义偏；bullying 义近，原文无。
4｜干扰：上文"the kids would giggle"诱人填 laughter；"made fun of"诱人填 jokes。
5｜意图：考"所有格后取原文名词"，分"按上下文动词填 laughter"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"children would ____ at me"填 laugh（动词）；所有格后必名词。
7｜错：laugh（动词/缺 -ter）；ridiculed（动词）；ridiculous（形容词）。
8｜R15.5 若空位在所有格之后，填名词；回原文取该所有格所带的名词，不由上下文动词反推。适用条件：空位在所有格之后。例外（本空错误路径）：填 laugh（动词/缺 -ter）；错在把「空位在所有格之后」当成唯一情形，一旦空位的词性/位置换成动词，答案就要从「ridicule」换成「laugh」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→所有格名词；与 R6.5"所有格后中心名词"同族。与 A6「词性/同义转换」冲突：把「laughter」当同义替换，会改掉原文用词；优先级：A1 原文唯一对应 ＞ A6。本空特有依据：上文"the kids would giggle"诱人填 laughter。
10｜自检：所有格后要名词吗？原文名词？词形对吗？

**组小结（G15 · 小蓝书预测卷二十二）**
- 考点分布：被动同义二选 1（36）；to 后原形 1（37）；5W 行取事件 1（38）；专名回填 1（39）；所有格后名词 1（40）。
- 规则：R15.1 两种被动表述取衔接者（36）；R15.2 to 后原形、与 birthday 搭配用 celebrate（37）；R15.3 行标题与空格句冲突时以空格句动词定类型（38）；R15.4 人名用专名大写（39）；R15.5 所有格后取原文名词（40）。
- 冲突与优先级：R15.1 与"唯一答案"冲突，本题半开放；R15.3 与"按行标题作答"冲突，空格句优先；R15.4 与"避免重复"冲突，专名优先。
- 未归类：36 半开放，答案二选一。
- 覆盖检查：本篇首次出现"行标题与空格句冲突"，是 5W 表格的专属考点。

### 2024湖北专升本小蓝书预测卷二十五 · Passage 5

**The Sky Railway**

Taking years to complete, the Qinghai-Tibet Railway is a record of all of our efforts to overcome the most difficult engineering challenges. How to protect the delicate ecosystem was among the top concerns.

The first landmark to catch my eye is the splendid Qingshuihe Bridge. Thirty-three passages have been built under the railway to allow the animals to move safely and freely in their natural habitat. Wild animals such as these Tibetan antelopes have now been using these passages for years.

To prevent damage to wetlands and grasslands, 675 bridges with a total length of about 160 kilometres were built between Golmud and Lhasa. We even moved 140,000 square metres of wetland to a new area in order to protect its distinct ecosystem.

Located at over 5,000 metres above sea level, Tanggula Station is the highest railway station in the world. In locations such as this, the thin air, changeable weather presented perhaps the greatest challenge for railway workers. To make sure we stayed healthy, several oxygen-making(制氧) stations were constructed. We were also able to enjoy regular breaks in lower areas.

As we pass Cuona Lake, I feel a sense of pride and achievement. Using thousands and thousands of sandbags, we built a twenty-kilometre wall along the lake to protect it from construction waste.

The Qinghai-Tibet Plateau has been attracting people's admiration for centuries. I am proud that we built our "impossible" railway, and did so with the care that the environment deserves. It truly is an extraordinary "Sky Railway".

| **Challenges** | **Solutions** |
| --- | --- |
| To protect wild animals | ●A total length of about ______36______ have been built under the railway to allow the animals to move safety and freely in their natural habitat. |
| To ______37______ wetlands and grasslands | ●675 bridges with ______38______ were built between Golmud and Lhasa.<br>●140.000 square meters of wetland were moved to a new area in |



|  | order to protect its distinct ecosystem |
| --- | --- |
| To make sure the workers stayed healthy | ●Several oxygen-making stations were constructed.<br>●The workers also enjoyed ______39______ in lower areas. |
| To ______40______ | ●Thousands and thousands of sandbags were used to build a twenty-kilometre wall along the lake to protect it from construction waste. |

**〔G16〕小蓝书预测卷二十五 · Passage 5（36—40）十层研究**

> **【3 词豁免】** 本组 Directions 未限 3 词，故不压缩：38 空 7 词（"a total length of about 160 kilometres"）按原文整短语入空。

> 【复原】表行"To protect wild animals｜A total length of about ____36____ have been built under the railway…"把"33 条通道"与"675 座桥、总长约 160 公里"混在一格；原文"Thirty-three passages have been built under the railway"与"675 bridges with a total length of about 160 kilometres"分属两句。此处按原文复原：36 问通道数，38 问桥的总长。

**36｜33 passages（Thirty-three passages）**
1｜表"…A total length of about ____36____ have been built under the railway to allow the animals to move safety and freely"；原文"Thirty-three passages have been built under the railway to allow the animals to move safely and freely in their natural habitat."。空＝数量+名词，谓语 have 为复数。
2｜候选：33 passages / 33 / thirty-three / 675 bridges。
3｜填「33 passages」→「…A total length of about 33 passages have been built under the railway to allow the animals to move safety and freely」：与原文逐字合，have been built 复数一致，成立；填「thirty-three」→「…A total length of about thirty-three have been built under the railway to allow the animals to move safety and freely」：原文无此形式，不成立；填「675 bridges」→「…A total length of about 675 bridges have been built under the railway to allow the animals to move safety and freely」：属另一句（桥，不是动物通道），不成立；填「33」→「…A total length of about 33 have been built under the railway to allow the animals to move safety and freely」：单写缺名词，且 have 无主语，不成立。回读：空位是数量+名词，谓语 have 为复数，只有「33 passages」同时满足形态与原文对应。
4｜干扰：同段"675 bridges with a total length of about 160 kilometres"就在下一句，数字 675 与"total length"最诱人。
5｜意图：考"两个数字分属两种设施"，分"见 total length 就填 160"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"____ bridges with a total length of 160 km"填 675；若"A total length of about ____"单独出现，填 160 kilometres。名词是判别键。
7｜错：675（设施错位）；160 kilometres（长度≠通道）；passages（缺数词）。
8｜R16.1 表格一格内含 two 组原文数据时，先看空后/空前的名词（passages/bridges）再取数字；数字必须带其计量单位或中心名词。适用条件：空位是数量+名词，谓语 have 为复数，须按原文的数字与格式整体取词。例外（本空错误路径）：填 675（设施错位）；错在把「空位是数量+名词，谓语 have 为复数，须按原文的数字与格式整体取词」当成唯一情形，换到那种位置时，入空的恰恰是「675」，而不是「33 passages」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.4→数字提取；与 R16.2"介词后原形"并列。与 A7「行内/段内取词」冲突：就近取「thirty-three」会与栏头/单位错配；优先级：A11 数字按单位/栏头配对 ＞ A7 就近。本空特有依据：同段"675 bridges with a total length of about 160 kilometres"就在下一句，数字 675 与"total length"最诱人。
10｜自检：空修饰的是 passages 还是 bridges？谓语单复数？数字带名词了吗？

**37｜prevent**
1｜表"To ____37____ wetlands and grasslands"；原文"To prevent damage to wetlands and grasslands, 675 bridges…"。空在 To 后，动词原形位。
2｜候选：prevent / protect / stop / avoid。
3｜prevent 与原文逐字合，prevent damage to 成立；protect 也常与 wetlands 搭配，但原文是 prevent damage to，且 protect 后一般不接 damage；stop 可通但原文 prevent；avoid 搭配不完整。
4｜干扰：下一格"To protect wild animals"就在上方，诱人填 protect；"protect it from construction waste"又在末段出现。
5｜意图：考"同一文档两处目的状语（prevent/protect）的分工"，分"见 wetlands 就填 protect"者。
6｜边界：若空后直接是 wetlands（无 damage to），protect 更顺；有 damage to 则必 prevent。
7｜错：protect（与 damage to 搭配冲突）；preventing（To 后原形）；stop（同义替换）。
8｜R16.2 若空位在 To 后且后接 damage to，填 prevent；protect 只接被保护对象，不接 damage。适用条件：空位在 To 后且后接 damage to。例外：若空位所在结构换成另一形态位（to 后→原形、介词后→-ing）；此时本条的适用前提不再成立，入空的就不再是「prevent」这一路，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→目的状语；与 R3.1"定语取词"共用"看空后搭配"。与 A1「原词直取」冲突：直取「protect」保留原形态，与空前结构不合；优先级：A3 空位词性/形态 ＞ A1。本空特有依据：下一格"To protect wild animals"就在上方，诱人填 protect。
10｜自检：空后是 damage to 还是直接名词？原文用哪个动词？原形吗？

**38｜a total length of about 160 kilometres**
1｜表"●675 bridges with ____38____ were built between Golmud and Lhasa."；原文"675 bridges with a total length of about 160 kilometres were built between Golmud and Lhasa."。空在 with 后，名词短语位。
2｜候选：a total length of about 160 kilometres / 160 kilometres / a total length / about 160 km long。
3｜a total length of about 160 kilometres 与原文逐字合，with+名词短语成立；160 kilometres 缺中心词 total length，且 with 160 kilometres 语义不完整；about 160 km long 是形容词短语，与 with 不搭。
4｜干扰：上一空刚用数字，诱人只填 160 kilometres； kilometres 的拼写又易错。
5｜意图：考"with 后补全整个名词短语"，分"只抄数字"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"were ____ long"填 160 kilometres long；with 后须名词短语。
7｜错：160 kilometres（缺 total length）；160 kilometre（单数）；about 160 kms（缩写）。
8｜R16.3 若空位在 with/without 后，补全整个名词短语（含中心词与数量），不截取数字部分。适用条件：空位在 with/without 后。例外（本空错误路径）：填 160 kilometres（缺 total length）；错在把「空位在 with/without 后」当成唯一情形，换到那种位置时，入空的恰恰是「160 kilometres long」，而不是「a total length of about 160 kilometres」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→介词后短语；与 R7.1"限定词后整短语"同族。与 A1「原词直取」冲突：冲突对象是 A1 直取的「about 160 km long」：它是形容词短语，与 with 不搭；本空的具体信号是「空位在 with/without 后」，入空的应是整短语；优先级：A2 整短语 ＞ A1（本空「a total length of about 160 kilometres」，7 词）。本空特有依据：上一空刚用数字，诱人只填 160 kilometres。
10｜自检：with 后要名词短语吗？中心词是哪个？数字+单位齐吗？

**39｜regular breaks**
1｜表"●The workers also enjoyed ____39____ in lower areas."；原文"We were also able to enjoy regular breaks in lower areas."。空在 enjoyed 后，名词短语位。
2｜候选：regular breaks / breaks / a good rest / holidays。
3｜填「regular breaks」→「●The workers also enjoyed regular breaks in lower areas.」：与原文逐字合，enjoy+名词成立；填「a good rest」→「●The workers also enjoyed a good rest in lower areas.」：义近无据，不成立；填「holidays」→「●The workers also enjoyed holidays in lower areas.」：义偏，不成立；填「breaks」→「●The workers also enjoyed breaks in lower areas.」：单写丢 regular，信息不全，不成立。回读：空位是题面空位，只有「regular breaks」同时满足形态与原文对应。
4｜干扰：上格"oxygen-making stations were constructed"是"保障健康"的措施，诱人填 oxygen；"lower areas"诱人填 holidays。
5｜意图：考"动宾短语整体取词"，分"只取中心名词 breaks"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"enjoy ____ breaks"填 regular（定语位）；空在 enjoy 后则取整短语。
7｜错：breaks（丢定语）；regular break（单复数）；relaxing（-ing 非名词短语）。
8｜R16.4 若原文是"形容词+名词"的宾语，表格空位在动词后时取整短语，不截取。适用条件：原文是"形容词+名词"的宾语。例外（本空错误路径）：填 breaks（丢定语）；错在把「原文是"形容词+名词"的宾语」当成唯一情形，一旦空位的词性/位置换成定语位，答案就要从「regular breaks」换成「regular」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→动宾短语；与 R16.3 同族。与 A1「原词直取」冲突：本空的具体信号是「原文是"形容词+名词"的宾语」，A1 却只取「a good rest」（义近无据，不成立）；优先级：A2 整短语 ＞ A1，本空以「regular breaks」（2 词）整体入空。本空特有依据：上格"oxygen-making stations were constructed"是"保障健康"的措施，诱人填 oxygen。
10｜自检：空位前是动词吗？原文这个宾语有几个词？单复数？

**40｜protect the lake**
1｜表"To ____40____｜●Thousands and thousands of sandbags were used to build a twenty-kilometre wall along the lake to protect it from construction waste."；空在 To 后，与前三行"To protect wild animals / To prevent… / To make sure…"并列，动词短语位。
2｜候选：protect the lake / protect it / protect Cuona Lake / stop construction waste。
3｜protect the lake 与"protect it from construction waste"对应（it＝Cuona Lake），与前 three 行同为 To+动词短语，成立；protect it 代词无先行词；protect Cuona Lake 更具体，亦可；stop construction waste 方向反（是防垃圾，不是止施工）。
4｜干扰：前三行都以 protect/prevent/make sure 开头，诱人只填 protect（缺宾语）；"construction waste"诱人填 stop。
5｜意图：考"目的栏取动词+宾语的整体"，分"只填动词"者。
6｜边界：若空位后已有宾语（"To ____ it from waste"），只填 protect；此处空后无宾语，须补 the lake。
7｜错：protect（缺宾语）；protecting（To 后原形）；stop the waste（方向反）。
8｜R16.5 若空位在 To 后且空后无宾语，补"动词+宾语"整体；前列并行项的结构即模板。适用条件：空位在 To 后且空后无宾语。例外：若题面把「protect the lake」拆成两空，此空只剩半截；此时本条的适用前提不再成立，入空的就不再是「protect the lake」这一路，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→目的栏；与 R16.2 同族（To 后原形）。与 A1「原词直取」冲突：A1 的「protect it」代词无先行词，而本空的具体信号是「空位在 To 后且空后无宾语」；优先级：A2 整短语 ＞ A1，答案「protect the lake」（3 词），A19 不启动。本空特有依据：前三行都以 protect/prevent/make sure 开头，诱人只填 protect（缺宾语）。
10｜自检：To 后原形吗？空后有没有宾语？与前几行结构一致吗？

**组小结（G16 · 小蓝书预测卷二十五）**
- 考点分布：数字与设施对应 1（36，含【复原】）；To 后 prevent/protect 分工 1（37）；with 后整短语 1（38）；动宾整短语 1（39）；目的栏补宾语 1（40）。
- 规则：R16.1 一格两数据时按中心名词取数字（36）；R16.2 To 后接 damage 用 prevent（37）；R16.3 介词后补全名词短语（38）；R16.4 动词后取整宾语短语（39）；R16.5 To 后补动词+宾语（40）。
- 冲突与优先级：R16.1 与"数字就近"冲突，中心名词优先；R16.2 与"同文档复现词（protect）"冲突，空后搭配优先。
- 未归类：无（36 已按原文复原）。
- 覆盖检查：本篇是"整短语 vs 截断"最集中的一篇，与 R7.1、R16.3、R16.4 构成完整规则组。

### 2024湖北专升本小蓝书预测卷二十四 · Passage 5

Poor Bobby was getting cold sitting out in the backyard in the snow. With only one dime (10 美分硬币) in hand, he could not come up with an idea for his mother's birthday gift. Ever since his father had passed away, the family of five had struggled. Although the family was short of money, they had love and family unity(团结).

Bobby had three sisters, all of whom had already made beautiful gifts for their mother. Wiping tears from his eyes, Bobby kicked the snow and started to walk down to the street where the stores opened. Anyway, he wanted to try his luck.

Bringing together all his courage, he walked into the first store he saw. His heart quickly turned cold when salesperson after salesperson told him that he could not buy anything with only a dime. Finally he saw a flower shop and went inside to wait in line. When the shop owner asked if he could help him, Bobby presented the dime and asked if he could buy one flower for his mother. The shop owner looked at Bobby and his dime. Then he put his hand on Bobby's shoulder gently and said, "You just wait here and I'll see what I can do for you." Then the shop owner left. All alone in the shop, Bobby began to feel alone and afraid. After a little while, the shop owner came out with a bunch of red roses, with leaves of green and tiny white flowers all tied together with a big silver bow.

| Bobby was thinking hard about the ______36______ for his mother. | ⇨ | His family was poor but full of love. |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ⇩ | | ⇩ |



| Bobby's sisters all made gifts, but Bobby just had only one dime and didn't know what to do. | ⇨ | Bobby felt ______37______ and helpless. |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ⇩ | | ⇩ |
| In hope of trying his luck, Bobby gathered all his ______38______ and walked to the store. | ⇨ | Bobby became very ______39______ when he was told only one dime could buy nothing. |
| ⇩ | | ⇩ |
| Bobby went to a flower shop and asked the owner if he could buy a flower with one dime. | ⇨ | Instead of ______40______ Bobby, the shop owner left and then came out with a bunch of red roses. |

**〔G17〕小蓝书预测卷二十四 · Passage 5（36—40）十层研究**

**36｜gift（birthday gift 亦可）**
1｜表"Bobby was thinking hard about the ____36____ for his mother."；原文"he could not come up with an idea for his mother’s birthday gift"。空＝名词。
2｜候选：gift / birthday gift / idea / party。
3｜gift 与 birthday gift 的中心词合，for his mother 成立；birthday gift 更全；idea 与 thinking hard about an idea 重复且丢"礼物"义；party 无据。
4｜干扰：本句动词 thinking hard about an idea 诱人填 idea。
5｜意图：考"取中心名词而非同句抽象词"，分"抄 idea"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"an ____ for his mother’s birthday"填 idea（原文 come up with an idea）；有 gift 则取 gift。
7｜错：idea（同句抽象词）；gifts（单数语境）；present（同义替换）。
8｜R17.1 若同句既有抽象词（idea）又有具体名词（gift），空位由后置定语（for his mother）定：给母亲的物→gift。适用条件：同句既有抽象词（idea）又有具体名词（gift）。例外（本空错误路径）：填 idea（同句抽象词）；错在把「同句既有抽象词（idea）又有具体名词（gift）」当成唯一情形，换到那种位置时，入空的恰恰是「idea」，而不是「gift」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1；与 R10.2"行内取词"并用。与 A6「词性/同义转换」冲突：按「idea」改写，词性/搭配与原位不合；优先级：A1 原文唯一对应 ＞ A6。本空特有依据：本句动词 thinking hard about an idea 诱人填 idea。
10｜自检：后置定语指向什么？原文名词？单数？

**37｜sad（alone 亦可，半开放）**
1｜表"Bobby felt ____37____ and helpless."；原文"Wiping tears from his eyes"与"Bobby began to feel alone and afraid"。空＝形容词，与 helpless 并列。
2｜候选：sad / alone / afraid / cold。
3｜sad 与"抹眼泪"合，sad and helpless 搭配成立；alone 见于原文（alone and afraid），与 helpless 亦搭，属可接受；afraid 是原文另一词，但与 helpless 并列时义偏"害怕"；cold 指身体冷。
4｜干扰：原文 alone and afraid 就成对出现，诱人整对搬用。
5｜意图：考"情感词按语境选，原文给的是线索不是答案"，分"整对搬 alone and afraid"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"felt alone and ____"填 afraid（原文原对）；本题已给 helpless，须换词。
7｜错：afraid（与 helpless 义叠）；cold（生理非情感）；sadly（副词）。
8｜R17.2 情感填空若表格已给一个情感词（helpless），另一空须与它不重复且贴合场景（抹泪→sad）；原文成对词只在表格原样保留时才照搬。适用条件：空位是形容词，与 helpless 并列，且原文有唯一对应词。例外（本空错误路径）：填 afraid（与 helpless 义叠）；错在把「空位是形容词，与 helpless 并列，且原文有唯一对应词」当成唯一情形，一旦空位的词性/位置换成原文原对，答案就要从「sad」换成「afraid」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.3→情感概括；与 R10.2 并用。与 A6「词性/同义转换」冲突：换「alone」更顺口，但原文用的是另一个词；优先级：A1 原文唯一对应 ＞ A6。本空特有依据：原文 alone and afraid 就成对出现，诱人整对搬用。
10｜自检：已给哪个情感词？场景是什么？重复吗？

**38｜courage**
1｜表"In hope of trying his luck, Bobby gathered all his ____38____ and walked to the store."；原文"Bringing together all his courage, he walked into the first store he saw."。空＝名词。
2｜候选：courage / luck / money / strength。
3｜courage 与 Bringing together all his courage 逐字对应；luck 已在表中（trying his luck），重复；money 只有一 dime；strength 义偏体力。
4｜干扰：同句"trying his luck"诱人填 luck；"walked to the store"诱人填 strength。
5｜意图：考"gather all his+名词"取原文，分"抄 luck"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"in hope of trying his ____"填 luck；gather 的宾语是 courage。
7｜错：luck（重复）；courages（不可数）；brave（形容词）。
8｜R17.3 若空位是"gather/collect all his ____"，取原文该动词的宾语；同句其他名词不作候选。适用条件：空位是"gather/collect all his ____"。例外（本空错误路径）：填 luck（重复）；错在把「空位是"gather/collect all his ____"」当成唯一情形，换到那种位置时，入空的恰恰是「luck」，而不是「courage」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→动宾；与 R13.4 同族。与 A6「词性/同义转换」冲突：取「luck」属于同义转写，不是原文对应；优先级：A1 原文唯一对应 ＞ A6。本空特有依据：同句"trying his luck"诱人填 luck。
10｜自检：动词是哪个？宾语？可数吗？

**39｜disappointed（cold 为原文直译，半开放）**
1｜表"Bobby became very ____39____ when he was told only one dime could buy nothing."；原文"His heart quickly turned cold when salesperson after salesperson told him that he could not buy anything with only one dime."。空＝形容词。
2｜候选：disappointed / cold / sad / angry。
3｜disappointed 与"心凉"的情节合，became+adj 成立；cold 是原文直译（his heart turned cold），但"Bobby became very cold"字面指体温；sad 与 37 重复；angry 无据。
4｜干扰：原文 turned cold 就在眼前，诱人直填 cold。
5｜意图：考"原文隐喻须还原为情感词"，分"直译隐喻"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"his heart turned ____"填 cold（原文）；主语是 Bobby 时用情感形容词。
7｜错：cold（隐喻直译）；disappointing（-ing 修饰物）；disappointment（名词）。
8｜R17.4 若原文用"his heart turned cold"一类隐喻，表格主语是人时，还原为情感形容词（disappointed），不照搬隐喻词。适用条件：原文用"his heart turned cold"一类隐喻。例外（本空错误路径）：填 cold（隐喻直译）；错在把「原文用"his heart turned cold"一类隐喻」当成唯一情形，一旦空位的词性/位置换成原文，答案就要从「disappointed」换成「cold」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.3→隐喻还原；与 R10.4"原文优先"冲突：R10.4 适用于搭配，本条适用于隐喻；优先级：隐喻优先还原 ＞ R10.4。
10｜自检：主语是人还是心？原文是隐喻吗？与 37 重复吗？

**40｜refusing**
1｜表"Instead of ____40____ Bobby, the shop owner left and then came out with a bunch of red roses."；原文"He said, 'You just wait here and I’ll see what I can do for you.' Then the shop owner left."。空＝V-ing（介词 of 后）。
2｜候选：refusing / leaving / ignoring / charging。
3｜refusing 与"没有拒绝、反而想办法"对应，instead of+V-ing 成立；leaving 与原文 the shop owner left 冲突（他确实离开了）；ignoring 可通但无据；charging 无据（故事重点是没拒绝）。
4｜干扰：原文 the shop owner left 就在本句后半，诱人填 leaving；"left"与"instead of"的转折关系易被误读。
5｜意图：考"instead of 前后语义对立"，分"填本句已有的动作"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"the shop owner ____ and then came out"填 left（原形，陈述事实）；instead of 后必须是与事实相反的动作。
7｜错：leaving（与事实同）；refuse（of 后缺 -ing）；to refuse（of 后不用 to）。
8｜R17.5 instead of 后填与事实相反的动作，用 V-ing；不得填本句已陈述的事实动词。适用条件：空位是V-ing（介词 of 后），形态由空前结构定。例外（本空错误路径）：填 leaving（与事实同）；错在把「空位是V-ing（介词 of 后），形态由空前结构定」当成唯一情形，一旦空位的词性/位置换成原形，陈述事实，答案就要从「refusing」换成「left」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.2→instead of 结构；与 R3.3"介词后 V-ing"并用。与 A1「原词直取」冲突：按「leaving」的形态填，to/介词位不成立；优先级：A3 空位词性/形态 ＞ A1。本空特有依据：原文 the shop owner left 就在本句后半，诱人填 leaving。
10｜自检：of 后 -ing？这个动作是事实还是被否定的？与后半句矛盾吗？

**组小结（G17 · 小蓝书预测卷二十四）**
- 考点分布：中心名词 vs 抽象词 1（36）；情感词不重复 1（37，半开放）；动宾取词 1（38）；隐喻还原 1（39，半开放）；instead of 反事实 1（40）。
- 规则：R17.1 后置定语定名词（36）；R17.2 情感空与已给情感词不重复（37）；R17.3 gather 后取原文宾语（38）；R17.4 隐喻还原为情感词（39）；R17.5 instead of 后用 V-ing 且反事实（40）。
- 冲突与优先级：R17.4 与 R10.4"原文优先"冲突，隐喻场景下还原优先（已在层 9 说明）。
- 未归类：37、39 半开放，各有两个可接受答案。
- 覆盖检查：本篇补上"隐喻还原"与"instead of 反事实"，两者都是前 16 组未出现的子类。

### 2024湖北专升本小蓝书预测卷五 · Passage 5

In the modern world, people have to remember passwords (密码) for everything from their email, to online banking and internet shopping.

So it's easy to understand that many of us would pick (选择) something and easy to remember.

A leaked (泄露的) Yahoo database (数据库) showed that the most popular passwords are still "123456" and "password". Easy ones like "qwerty" and "abc123" are also among the top 10, reported the *Daily Mail*. Other top passwords include "welcome", "sunshine" and



"ninja". Many more used simple combinations (组合) of their name, age, or birthday.

These people are trying to make life easy for themselves. But they are also making it easy for hackers (黑客) to break into their accounts, experts warned.

Researchers from UK's Lancaster University as well as Peking and Fujian Normal universities in China, did a test on passwords. They tried to guess passwords based on people's different personal information.

They guessed passwords for more than 73 percent of ordinary users' accounts. Hackers could even break a third of the harder passwords in 100 guesses, reported the *Daily Mail*.

So how can you make your passwords safer? Researchers from Bloom berg Business week have some tips for you.

Upper-casing (大写) your password is a good start. Adding numbers and/or symbols to your password can also be of great help. Choose a nine-letter password that includes numbers and/or symbols. This would take a hackers computer hundreds of years to break! Never use personal information as a password. Use different passwords for different accounts (账户). Experts suggest that a user should change their password every 90 days to keep hackers guessing.

**Password Safety**

| The reason to pick simple passwords | It's ______36______ for people to remember. |
| --- | --- |
| Most popular passwords to use | \*123456 \*password \*qwerty \*abc123 \*welcome \*sunshine \*ninja |
| The study on passwords | Researchers from the UK and China tried to guess passwords.<br>\*They guessed passwords for over 73 ______37______ of ordinary users' accounts. |
| Ways to make passwords safer | \*Upper-case your password;<br>\*Add numbers and/or symbols to passwords;<br>\*Choose a nine-letter password which has numbers and/or symbols;<br>\*Don't use ______38______ information; |



| | \*Use ______39______ passwords for different accounts;<br>\*Change the password every ______40______ days. |
| --- | --- |

**〔G18〕小蓝书预测卷五 · Passage 5（36—40）十层研究**

> 【复原】原文"So it’s easy to understand that many of us would pick (选择) something and easy to remember."中"something and easy"疑为"something easy"的脱字；不影响 36 的答案。

**36｜easy**
1｜表"It’s ____36____ for people to remember."；原文"…would pick something easy to remember"。空＝形容词（It’s+adj+for sb to do）。
2｜候选：easy / hard / common / necessary。
3｜easy 与 easy to remember 合，It’s easy for sb to do 成立；hard 反义；common 义偏"普遍"；necessary 与"图省事"的因果不合。
4｜干扰：全段讲"图方便"，hard 是反义陷阱；remember 诱人填 important。
5｜意图：考"It’s+adj+for sb to do"取原文形容词，分"按常识填"者。
6｜边界：若"It’s ____ to remember"（无 for people）仍填 easy；若"it is ____ that…"可填 clear/true。
7｜错：hard（反义）；easily（副词）；easiness（名词）。
8｜R18.1 若空位在 It’s 与 for sb to do 之间，填形容词，取原文该不定式的评价词。适用条件：空位在 It’s 与 for sb to do 之间。例外（本空错误路径）：填 hard（反义）；错在把「空位在 It’s 与 for sb to do 之间」当成唯一情形，换到那种位置时，入空的恰恰是「clear/true」，而不是「easy」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→形式主语；与 R4.3"系动词后形容词"同族。与 A6「词性/同义转换」冲突：把「hard」当同义替换，会改掉原文用词；优先级：A1 原文唯一对应 ＞ A6。本空特有依据：全段讲"图方便"，hard 是反义陷阱。
10｜自检：要形容词吗？原文评价词？反义了吗？

**37｜percent**
1｜表"They guessed passwords for over 73 ____37____ of ordinary users’ accounts."；原文"more than 73 percent of ordinary users’ accounts"。空＝量词。
2｜候选：percent / percents / people / accounts。
3｜填「percent」→「They guessed passwords for over 73 percent of ordinary users’ accounts.」：与 73 percent of 合，percent 无复数，成立；填「percents」→「They guessed passwords for over 73 percents of ordinary users’ accounts.」：不成立；填「accounts」→「They guessed passwords for over 73 accounts of ordinary users’ accounts.」：与句末重复，不成立；填「people」→「They guessed passwords for over 73 people of ordinary users’ accounts.」：与 of accounts 冲突，不成立。回读：空位是量词，只有「percent」同时满足形态与原文对应。
4｜干扰：73 后最顺是 percent，但常误写 percents；accounts 在句末诱人重复。
5｜意图：考"百分数表达"，分"写 percents"者。
6｜边界：若"73 ____ of them"仍 percent；若空位是"a ____ of"（比例）填 percentage。
7｜错：percents（不可数）；percentage（须 a percentage of）。
8｜R18.2 百分数后填 percent（不加 s），后接 of+名词。适用条件：空位是量词，且原文有唯一对应词。例外：若空位改由另一词性承担（名词位换动词/形容词位）；此时本条的适用前提不再成立，入空的就不再是「percent」这一路，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.4→百分数；与 R6.4 同族。与 A6「词性/同义转换」冲突：按「people」改写，词性/搭配与原位不合；优先级：A1 原文唯一对应 ＞ A6。本空特有依据：73 后最顺是 percent，但常误写 percents。
10｜自检：加 s 了吗？后接 of 吗？重复了吗？

**38｜personal**
1｜表"*Don’t use ____38____ information;"；原文"Never use personal information as a password."。空＝形容词。
2｜候选：personal / private / same / simple。
3｜personal 与原文逐字合，personal information 高频搭配；private 义近非原文；same 与"每账户不同密码"冲突；simple 是反面（简单密码危险）。
4｜干扰：上文"simple combinations of their name, age, or birthday"诱人填 simple；private 与 personal 近义。
5｜意图：考"高频搭配取原文"，分"近义替换"者。
6｜边界：若原文讲 privacy，可用 private；本条原文明确 personal。
7｜错：private（同义替换）；person（名词）；personally（副词）。
8｜R18.3 名词前形容词位若构成高频搭配，取原文词，不用近义形容词。适用条件：空位是形容词，且原文有唯一对应词。例外（本空错误路径）：填 private（同义替换）；错在把「空位是形容词，且原文有唯一对应词」当成唯一情形，本条的适用前提不再成立，入空的就不再是「personal」这一路，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→搭配；与 R3.5 同族。与 A6「词性/同义转换」冲突：换「private」更顺口，但原文用的是另一个词；优先级：A1 原文唯一对应 ＞ A6。本空特有依据：上文"simple combinations of their name, age, or birthday"诱人填 simple。
10｜自检：搭配是哪个？原文用词？词性？

**39｜different**
1｜表"*Use ____39____ passwords for different accounts;"；原文"Use different passwords for different accounts."。空＝形容词。
2｜候选：different / same / safe / strong。
3｜填「different」→「*Use different passwords for different accounts;」：与原文逐字合，与后半 different accounts 呼应，成立；填「strong」→「*Use strong passwords for different accounts;」：是目的不是做法，不成立；填「same」→「*Use same passwords for different accounts;」：与建议相反，不成立；填「safe」→「*Use safe passwords for different accounts;」：是目的不是做法，不成立。回读：空位是形容词，只有「different」同时满足形态与原文对应。
4｜干扰：本段"make passwords safer"里的 safer 诱人填 safe/strong。
5｜意图：考"重复结构取原文"，分"按'更安全'填"者。
6｜边界：若"make your passwords ____"填 safer（比较级）。
7｜错：same（反义）；safely（副词）；difference（名词）。
8｜R18.4 若表格保留原文重复结构（different ____ for different accounts），取原文同一形容词。适用条件：表格保留原文重复结构（different ____ for different accounts）。例外（本空错误路径）：填 same（反义）；错在把「表格保留原文重复结构（different ____ for different accounts）」当成唯一情形，一旦空位的词性/位置换成比较级，答案就要从「different」换成「safer」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→重复结构；与 R10.3 同族。与 A6「词性/同义转换」冲突：取「same」属于同义转写，不是原文对应；优先级：A1 原文唯一对应 ＞ A6。本空特有依据：本段"make passwords safer"里的 safer 诱人填 safe/strong。
10｜自检：与后半哪个词呼应？原文词？反义了吗？

**40｜90**
1｜表"*Change the password every ____40____ days."；原文"change their password every 90 days"。空＝数词。
2｜候选：90 / 30 / 60 / 100。
3｜填「90」→「*Change the password every 90 days.」：与原文逐字合，成立；填「100」→「*Change the password every 100 days.」：与"100 guesses"混，不成立；填「30」→「*Change the password every 30 days.」：无据，不成立；填「60」→「*Change the password every 60 days.」：无据，不成立。回读：空位是数词，只有「90」同时满足形态与原文对应。
4｜干扰：上段"break…in 100 guesses"的 100 最近。
5｜意图：考"数字按单位配对"，分"就近取 100"者。
6｜边界：若"in ____ guesses"填 100；every ____ days 对应 90。
7｜错：100（邻近数字）；90th（序数）。
8｜R18.5 数字空按其单位（days/guesses/percent）回原文配对。适用条件：空位是数词，须按原文的数字与格式整体取词。例外（本空错误路径）：填 100（邻近数字）；错在把「空位是数词，须按原文的数字与格式整体取词」当成唯一情形，换到那种位置时，入空的恰恰是「100」，而不是「90」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.4→数字+单位；与 R16.1 同族。与 A7「行内/段内取词」冲突：同段数字多，按「30」配对会串栏；优先级：A11 数字按单位/栏头配对 ＞ A7 就近。本空特有依据：上段"break…in 100 guesses"的 100 最近。
10｜自检：单位是什么？原文对应数字？邻近干扰？

**组小结（G18 · 小蓝书预测卷五）**
- 考点分布：形式主语形容词 1（36）；百分数量词 1（37）；搭配形容词 1（38）；重复结构 1（39）；数字+单位 1（40）。
- 规则：R18.1 It’s+adj+for sb to do（36）；R18.2 percent 不加 s（37）；R18.3 高频搭配取原文（38）；R18.4 重复结构取同一形容词（39）；R18.5 数字按单位配对（40）。
- 冲突与优先级：R18.3 与近义替换冲突，原文优先；R18.5 与就近数字冲突，单位优先。
- 未归类：无。
- 覆盖检查：本篇是小词类（量词/数字/搭配）合集，补足 RC-1.4 数字分支。

### 2024湖北专升本小蓝书预测卷八 · Passage 5

Why should students play sports? There are some common reasons. Some people think that students play sports in order to get exercise. Others think it can help students have fun. But that is not enough. If students play sports, they can get more than healthy bodies. Why? Read the following for other reasons.

Students who play sports do better in school. Some people think that doing exercise will take up students' study time. But a recent study has shown that students who play sports get



better scores in school than those who don't. Exercise gets students to learn, remember things and reflect better.

Students who play sports develop their teamwork spirits and learn problem-solving skills. When they are working together with others to win games and reach goals, they're learning how to succeed in practice. And these skills will be useful to them for their study or their future work.

Playing sports can also improve confidence. Students who play sports feel better about themselves. When they know they can improve and reach their goals by practicing, it builds their confidence. And sports can also help them have communication with others and make new friends.

Now, how about going out to play sports with your friends every day?

| | |
|---|---|
| **Why should students play sports?** | |
| Common reasons | Students play sports to get exercise or have fun. |
| Other ____36____ | Playing sports helps students get better ____37____ in school. |
|  | It also makes them learn, remember things and reflect better. |
|  | Students develop teamwork spirits and learn problem-solving skills. |
|  | Students who play sports can learn how to succeed in ____38____. |
|  | Playing sports can improve confidence. Sports can help students ____39____ with others and make new friends. |
| Advice | Students should ____40____ out to play sports with friends every day. |

**〔G19〕小蓝书预测卷八 · Passage 5（36—40）十层研究**

**36｜reasons**
1｜表"Other ____36____｜Playing sports helps students get better ____37____ in school."；原文"Why should students play sports? There are some common reasons.… Read the following for other reasons."。空＝复数名词（栏头）。
2｜候选：reasons / ways / benefits / advice。
3｜reasons 与 other reasons 逐字合，与上文 common reasons 对举，复数；ways 义偏"方法"；benefits 义近但原文 reasons；advice 不可数，不能配 other（复数）。
4｜干扰：全篇讲"好处"，诱人填 benefits；上句 common reasons 与 other 对举，学生若不回看易填 ways。
5｜意图：考"other X 对举取原文 X"，分"按主题填 benefits"者。
6｜边界：若栏头是"Other ____ of playing sports"填 benefits（of 搭配）；reasons 与 other 直接搭配。
7｜错：benefit（单数）；advice（不可数）；reason（单数）。
8｜R19.1 若表格出现 other ____，回原文找同结构的 common/other + 名词，取同一名词并保持复数。适用条件：表格出现 other ____。例外（本空错误路径）：填 benefit（单数）；错在把「表格出现 other ____」当成唯一情形，一旦空位的词性/位置换成of 搭配，答案就要从「reasons」换成「benefits」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.3→对举栏头；与 R8.2"topic sentence 取词"同族。与 A1「原文唯一对应词优先」冲突：抄「ways」会概括不住下辖各行；优先级：A8 栏头/标题概括 ＞ A1。本空特有依据：全篇讲"好处"，诱人填 benefits。
10｜自检：与哪个词对举？可数吗？单复数？

**37｜scores**
1｜表"Playing sports helps students get better ____37____ in school."；原文"students who play sports get better scores in school than those who don’t"。空＝复数名词。
2｜候选：scores / grades / results / marks。
3｜填「scores」→「Playing sports helps students get better scores in school.」：与原文逐字合，better scores 成立；填「results」→「Playing sports helps students get better results in school.」：义近非原文，不成立；填「grades」→「Playing sports helps students get better grades in school.」：义近非原文，不成立；填「marks」→「Playing sports helps students get better marks in school.」：英式，原文 scores，不成立。回读：空位是复数名词，只有「scores」同时满足形态与原文对应。
4｜干扰：in school 诱人填 grades（美式同义）；下一句"learn, remember things and reflect better"诱人填 skills。
5｜意图：考"better+名词取原文"，分"按同义词填 grades"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"get better ____ than those who don’t"仍 scores；若"get better at ____"填 sports/study。
7｜错：grade（单数/同义）；score（单数）；scoring（-ing）。
8｜R19.2 若空位紧跟 better 且后接 in school，取原文该比较句的中心名词，复数。适用条件：空位紧跟 better 且后接 in school。例外（本空错误路径）：填 grade（单数/同义）；错在把「空位紧跟 better 且后接 in school」当成唯一情形，换到那种位置时，入空的恰恰是「sports/study」，而不是「scores」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→比较句取词；与 R2.2 同族。与 A6「词性/同义转换」冲突：把「grades」当同义替换会改掉原文用词；优先级：A1 原文唯一对应 ＞ A6。本空特有依据：in school 诱人填 grades（美式同义）。
10｜自检：原文比较句名词？单复数？同义替换了吗？

**38｜practice**
1｜表"Students who play sports can learn how to succeed in ____38____."；原文"they’re learning how to succeed in practice"。空＝名词。
2｜候选：practice / life / school / games。
3｜填「practice」→「Students who play sports can learn how to succeed in practice.」：与原文逐字合，succeed in practice 成立；填「school」→「Students who play sports can learn how to succeed in school.」：与"in school"重复，不成立；填「games」→「Students who play sports can learn how to succeed in games.」：义偏，不成立；填「life」→「Students who play sports can learn how to succeed in life.」：义泛，不成立。回读：空位是名词，只有「practice」同时满足形态与原文对应。
4｜干扰：上一行"better scores in school"诱人填 school；"win games"诱人填 games。
5｜意图：考"in+抽象名词取原文"，分"就近取 school"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"learn how to ____ in practice"填 succeed（动词）；in 后须名词。
7｜错：practise（动词，英式拼写）；practices（复数）；practical（形容词）。
8｜R19.3 若空位在 succeed in 之后，填抽象名词（practice），不与上一行重复。适用条件：空位在 succeed in 之后。例外（本空错误路径）：填 practise（动词，英式拼写）；错在把「空位在 succeed in 之后」当成唯一情形，一旦空位的词性/位置换成动词，答案就要从「practice」换成「succeed」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→介词宾语；与 R4.4 同族。与 A6「词性/同义转换」冲突：把「life」当同义替换，会改掉原文用词；优先级：A1 原文唯一对应 ＞ A6。本空特有依据：上一行"better scores in school"诱人填 school。
10｜自检：in 后要名词吗？原文词？与上行重复吗？

**39｜communicate**
1｜表"Sports can help students ____39____ with others and make new friends."；原文"sports can also help them have communication with others and make new friends"。空＝动词原形（help sb do）。
2｜候选：communicate / talk / work / play。
