3｜communicate 与 have communication 对应，help sb communicate with 成立；talk 义近非原文；work 与"交友"义不合；play 与 sports 重复。
4｜干扰：with others 诱人填 talk/play；help them 后接原形的规则易被忽略而填 communication。
5｜意图：考"名词转动词（communication→communicate）"，分"直搬名词"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"have ____ with others"填 communication（名词）；help sb 后须原形。
7｜错：communication（名词）；communicating（help sb 后原形）；to communicate（不加 to）。
8｜R19.4 若空位在 help sb 之后且后接 with others，填动词原形，名词 communication 转为 communicate。适用条件：空位在 help sb 之后且后接 with others。例外（本空错误路径）：填 communication（名词）；错在把「空位在 help sb 之后且后接 with others」当成唯一情形，一旦空位的词性/位置换成名词，答案就要从「communicate」换成「communication」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.2→名词转动词；与 R9.5 同族。与 A1「原词直取」冲突：「talk」的形态与本空要求差一层（-ing/原形/过去式）；优先级：A3 空位词性/形态 ＞ A1。本空特有依据：with others 诱人填 talk/play。
10｜自检：help sb 后形态？原文名词？介词 with？

**40｜go**
1｜表"Advice｜Students should ____40____ out to play sports with friends every day."；原文"how about going out to play sports with your friends every day?"。空＝动词原形（should 后）。
2｜候选：go / come / take / hang。
3｜填「go」→「Advice｜Students should go out to play sports with friends every day.」：与 going out to play 对应，should go out 成立；填「come」→「Advice｜Students should come out to play sports with friends every day.」：方向反，不成立；填「take」→「Advice｜Students should take out to play sports with friends every day.」：缺宾语，不成立；填「hang」→「Advice｜Students should hang out to play sports with friends every day.」：与 out 搭配义为"闲逛"，义偏，不成立。回读：空位是动词原形（should 后），只有「go」同时满足形态与原文对应。
4｜干扰：out 前最顺的是 go/hang，hang out 是高频短语，诱人。
5｜意图：考"建议句取原文动词＋should 后原形"，分"按 hang out 填"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"how about ____ out"填 going（介词 about 后 -ing）；should 后用原形。
7｜错：going（should 后原形）；hang（同义但非原文）；to go（不加 to）。
8｜R19.5 若空位在 should 后且后接 out to do，取原文动词原形；介词 about 后则 -ing。适用条件：空位在 should 后且后接 out to do。例外：若空位所在结构换成另一形态位（to 后→原形、介词后→-ing）；此时本条的适用前提不再成立，入空的就不再是「go」这一路，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→情态动词后；与 R2.5 同族。与 A1「原词直取」冲突：直取「come」保留原形态，与空前结构不合；优先级：A3 空位词性/形态 ＞ A1。本空特有依据：out 前最顺的是 go/hang，hang out 是高频短语，诱人。
10｜自检：should 后形态？原文动词？out 搭配？

**组小结（G19 · 小蓝书预测卷八）**
- 考点分布：对举栏头 1（36）；比较句名词 1（37）；介词宾语 1（38）；名词转动词 1（39）；情态动词后原形 1（40）。
- 规则：R19.1 other X 对举取原文 X（36）；R19.2 better+名词取原文（37）；R19.3 succeed in 后抽象名词（38）；R19.4 help sb 后名词转动词（39）；R19.5 should 后原形（40）。
- 冲突与优先级：R19.2/R19.4 与同义替换冲突，原文优先；R19.5 与"高频短语 hang out"冲突，原文动词优先。
- 未归类：无。
- 覆盖检查：本篇复现并加固了 R9.5、R2.5 两条，说明同一规则在不同卷中反复被考。

### 2024湖北专升本小蓝书预测卷六 · Passage 5

It is said that the eyes are the windows of our soul. Beautiful and bright eyes not only show a good health but also make you look very attractive (有魅力的). To have healthy eyes, you have to look after them properly.

Pay more attention to your diet. First, eat food that is good for your eyes. They are fresh fruits and vegetables as well as fish, eggs, and milk. Besides, a regular amount of vitamins A and B2 should also be taken.

A regular and good sleep is necessary. It can help to relax your eyes. Usually, you must



sleep for at least eight hours every day. Loss of sleep can give you dark circles under the eyes.

You must exercise your eyes every day. Blink (眨眼) your eyes at regular periods, which makes the eyeballs watery, and gives them a good wash.

We use our eyes in many wrong ways. For example, if a small thing goes into our eye, we start to rub (揉) it, which is wrong. Rubbing can do harm to our eyes. Don't read or write in too bright sunlight. Reading, writing, watching television or working on the computer for too long without a rest can also cause eye tiredness.

Remember to always look after God's most beautiful gift to you. Your life will be very colourless without your eyes.

| How to look after your eyes | ______36______ | ·Eat food good for your eyes, such as fresh fruits, ______37______ and food high in protein (蛋白质).<br>·Take a proper amount of Vitamins A and B2 regularly. |
| --- | --- | --- |
| | Sleep | Have a regular and ______38______ sleep. |
| | Eye exercising | Exercise your eyes every day |
| | Eye using | ·Don't ______39______ your eye when something goes in.<br>·Don't read or write in too ______40______ sunlight.<br>·Take a rest after reading, writing, watching TV or working on the computer for too long. |

**〔G20〕小蓝书预测卷六 · Passage 5（36—40）十层研究**

**36｜Diet**
1｜表"How to look after your eyes｜____36____｜·Eat food good for your eyes…·Take a proper amount of Vitamins A and B2"。空＝名词（栏目名），与 Sleep / Eye exercising / Eye using 并列。
2｜候选：Diet / Food / Eating / Nutrition。
3｜Diet 与原文小标题"Pay more attention to your diet."对应，与其余栏目同为单词名词；Food 义窄（只指食物，不含"饮食结构"）；Eating 是 -ing；Nutrition 义近非原文。
4｜干扰：栏内两句都以 Eat/Take 开头，诱人填 Food/Eating；Sleep 一栏是单词，说明其余也应是单词。
5｜意图：考"栏目名取原文小标题名词"，分"按栏内动词自造"者。
6｜边界：若栏目名是"____ habits"填 eating（eating habits）；单独一栏用名词 Diet。
7｜错：Food（义窄）；Eating（-ing 与并列不一致）；Diets（复数，栏目名用单数）。
8｜R20.1 表格并列栏目的空位取原文小标题的名词（Diet/Sleep），单数，与其余栏目形态一致。适用条件：空位是栏头/标题的概括位，且须统摄下辖各行。例外（本空错误路径）：填 Food（义窄）；错在把「空位是栏头/标题的概括位，且须统摄下辖各行」当成唯一情形，换到那种位置时，入空的恰恰是「eating」，而不是「Diet」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.3→栏目名；与 R8.2 同族，来源是小标题而非 topic sentence。与 A1「原文唯一对应词优先」冲突：原文没有可直接抄的上位词，「Food」只是下位词或干扰；优先级：A8 栏头/标题概括 ＞ A1。本空特有依据：栏内两句都以 Eat/Take 开头，诱人填 Food/Eating。
10｜自检：并列栏目是什么形态？原文小标题？单数吗？

**37｜vegetables**
1｜表"·Eat food good for your eyes, such as fresh fruits, ____37____ and food high in protein"。原文"They are fresh fruits and vegetables as well as fish, eggs, and milk."。空＝复数名词，与 fruits 并列。
2｜候选：vegetables / meat / fish / milk。
3｜vegetables 与原文 fresh fruits and vegetables 逐字合，与 fruits 并列；fish 属后文 protein 一类；meat 原文未提；milk 同属蛋白类。
4｜干扰：原文 fish, eggs, and milk 紧跟其后，诱人填 fish；high in protein 又概括了鱼蛋奶，学生易混。
5｜意图：考"such as 列举取原文前两项"，分"取后文蛋白类"者。
6｜边界：若空位在"food high in protein, such as fish, eggs and ____"填 milk；列举范围由 such as 管辖。
7｜错：fish（属 protein 组）；vegetable（单数）；fruit（与 fruits 重复）。
8｜R20.2 such as 列举中，空位取原文同一列举序列的对应项；不同列举组（fruits/vegetables 与 fish/eggs/milk）不得混。适用条件：空位是复数名词，与 fruits 并列，且栏头/并列项定数。例外（本空错误路径）：填 fish（属 protein 组）；错在把「空位是复数名词，与 fruits 并列，且栏头/并列项定数」当成唯一情形，换到那种位置时，入空的恰恰是「milk」，而不是「vegetables」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→列举；与 R1.2 同族。与 A6「词性/同义转换」冲突：把「meat」当同义替换会改掉原文用词；优先级：A1 原文唯一对应 ＞ A6。本空特有依据：原文 fish, eggs, and milk 紧跟其后，诱人填 fish。
10｜自检：与谁并列？原文列举顺序？单复数？

**38｜good**
1｜表"Sleep｜Have a regular and ____38____ sleep."；原文"A regular and good sleep is necessary."。空＝形容词。
2｜候选：good / enough / sound / deep。
3｜填「good」→「Sleep｜Have a regular and good sleep.」：与原文逐字合，regular and good 并列，成立；填「enough」→「Sleep｜Have a regular and enough sleep.」：位置应在名词前/后但原文无，不成立；填「sound」→「Sleep｜Have a regular and sound sleep.」：是常见搭配但原文 good，不成立；填「deep」→「Sleep｜Have a regular and deep sleep.」：是常见搭配但原文 good，不成立。回读：空位是形容词，只有「good」同时满足形态与原文对应。
4｜干扰：中文"睡得好"诱人填 sound/deep；下一句"at least eight hours"诱人填 enough。
5｜意图：考"并列形容词取原文"，分"按搭配填 sound"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"sleep for at least eight ____"填 hours；形容词位取原文 good。
7｜错：well（副词）；sound（同义替换）；better（比较级无据）。
8｜R20.3 若空位与 regular 并列修饰同一名词，取原文该并列的另一个形容词。适用条件：空位与 regular 并列修饰同一名词。例外（本空错误路径）：填 well（副词）；错在把「空位与 regular 并列修饰同一名词」当成唯一情形，换到那种位置时，入空的恰恰是「hours」，而不是「good」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→并列定语；与 R1.2 同族。与 A6「词性/同义转换」冲突：按「enough」改写，词性/搭配与原位不合；优先级：A1 原文唯一对应 ＞ A6。本空特有依据：中文"睡得好"诱人填 sound/deep。
10｜自检：与谁并列？原文形容词？比较级吗？

**39｜rub**
1｜表"Eye using｜·Don’t ____39____ your eye when something goes in."；原文"if a small thing goes into our eye, we start to rub (揉) it, which is wrong"。空＝动词原形（Don’t 后）。
2｜候选：rub / touch / close / wash。
3｜填「rub」→「Eye using｜·Don’t rub your eye when something goes in.」：与原文逐字合，Don’t rub your eye 成立；填「touch」→「Eye using｜·Don’t touch your eye when something goes in.」：义轻，不成立；填「close」→「Eye using｜·Don’t close your eye when something goes in.」：义偏，不成立；填「wash」→「Eye using｜·Don’t wash your eye when something goes in.」：是正确做法（文中未说），不成立。回读：空位是动词原形（Don’t 后），只有「rub」同时满足形态与原文对应。
4｜干扰：中文"揉眼睛"直译 rub 反而对；但"something goes in"会诱人填 wash（冲洗，实际是正确做法）。
5｜意图：考"取原文动词＋否定祈使句原形"，分"按常识填 wash"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"we start to ____ it"填 rub（to 后原形）；Don’t 后同样原形。
7｜错：rubbing（Don’t 后原形）；rubber（名词）；wash（常识替换）。
8｜R20.4 否定祈使句 Don’t 后填动词原形，取原文该动作的动词；"正确做法"不能作答案（本条问的是错误做法）。适用条件：空位是动词原形（Don’t 后），形态由空前结构定。例外：若空位所在结构换成另一形态位（to 后→原形、介词后→-ing）；此时本条的适用前提不再成立，入空的就不再是「rub」这一路，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→祈使句；与 R2.5"否定助动词后原形"同族。与 A1「原词直取」冲突：按「touch」的形态填，to/介词位不成立；优先级：A3 空位词性/形态 ＞ A1。本空特有依据：中文"揉眼睛"直译 rub 反而对。
10｜自检：Don’t 后形态？原文动词？这是错误做法吗？

**40｜bright**
1｜表"·Don’t read or write in too ____40____ sunlight."；原文"Don’t read or write in too bright sunlight."。空＝形容词。
2｜候选：bright / strong / much / dark。
3｜bright 与原文逐字合，too bright sunlight 成立；strong 可修饰 light 但原文 bright；much 不能修饰 sunlight（不可数但此处是形容词位）；dark 反义（暗处也伤眼，但原文说太亮）。
4｜干扰：too 后常接 much/many，诱人填 much；"伤眼"常识诱人填 dark。
5｜意图：考"too+形容词+名词"结构，分"按 too much 惯性填"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"too ____ sunlight"（不可数）不能用 many；若"too much ____"填 sunlight（名词）。
7｜错：much（too much 后不接形容词）；brightly（副词）；strong（同义替换）。
8｜R20.5 too 后若已有名词，空位是形容词；too much/many 只在空位后直接跟名词时用。适用条件：空位是形容词，且原文有唯一对应词。例外（本空错误路径）：填 much（too much 后不接形容词）；错在把「空位是形容词，且原文有唯一对应词」当成唯一情形，本条的适用前提不再成立，入空的就不再是「bright」这一路，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→形容词位；与 R3.1 同族。与 A6「词性/同义转换」冲突：换「strong」更顺口，但原文用的是另一个词；优先级：A1 原文唯一对应 ＞ A6。本空特有依据：too 后常接 much/many，诱人填 much。
10｜自检：too 后是形容词还是量词？原文词？反义了吗？

**组小结（G20 · 小蓝书预测卷六）**
- 考点分布：栏目名取小标题 1（36）；such as 列举 1（37）；并列定语 1（38）；否定祈使原形 1（39）；too+形容词 1（40）。
- 规则：R20.1 栏目名取原文小标题名词、单数（36）；R20.2 列举分组不混（37）；R20.3 并列定语取原文（38）；R20.4 否定祈使取原文动词、正确做法不作答案（39）；R20.5 too 后形容词（40）。
- 冲突与优先级：R20.4 与"常识正确做法"冲突，原文错误做法优先；R20.5 与"too much 惯性"冲突，空后有无名词优先。
- 未归类：无。
- 覆盖检查：本篇补上"栏目名取小标题"与"列举分组"，与 G19 的对举栏头合成"栏头三型"。

### 2024湖北专升本小蓝书预测卷十 · Passage 5

There are many methods you can use to improve your learning ability. Here are some of them.

Take notes by hand. Research shows it can help you learn more effectively(有效地). Write down important information in your own words. Write in phrases (短语) instead of complete sentences so you can still pay attention and won't waste time on every little word.

Repeat what you want to remember out loud. It can help information stick in your mind. As you review or study information, use your own speech to add an active element to a word or phrase. Say something you really want to remember out loud. It can help it become clearer in your long-term memory.

Test yourself as often as you can. It's a great way to find out what you need to learn. Look through the information you're trying to learn and ask yourself questions about the material. If you answer correctly, great! If not, find the right answer and try to remember it.

Practice a task in different ways. Changing the way that you practice can help you improve. If you're trying to learn a task or skill, practice is one of the most useful ways to get better. But don't just practice in the same way over and over. Instead, try practicing in a different way each time, which can help make you learn more quickly.

| How to improve your learning ability | |
|---|---|
| Take notes by hand. | *It can help you learn more effectively. |
|  | *When taking notes, you should write in phrases rather than ____36____. |
| Repeat what you want to remember out loud. | *It can help you remember something for a long time. |
|  | *It can help something you really want to remember become ____37____ in your long-term memory. |
| Test yourself as often as you can. | *You can find out what you ____38____ to learn by doing this. |
|  | *Ask yourself questions when you ____39____ the information you are learning. |
| Practice a task in different ways. | When you learn a task or skill, practicing in different ways can help you become better and learn ____40____. |

**〔G21〕小蓝书预测卷十 · Passage 5（36—40）十层研究**

**36｜complete sentences**
1｜表"*When taking notes, you should write in phrases rather than ____36____."；原文"Write in phrases (短语) instead of complete sentences"。空＝名词短语，rather than 后与 phrases 对举。
2｜候选：complete sentences / sentences / words / long phrases。
3｜complete sentences 与原文逐字合，rather than 对举成立；sentences 丢 complete，信息不全；words 与 phrases 不对举；long phrases 与 phrases 重复。
4｜干扰：上句"won’t waste time on every little word"里的 word 诱人填 words。
5｜意图：考"rather than 对举取原文整短语"，分"只取中心词 sentences"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"instead of ____ sentences"填 complete（定语位）；rather than 后取整短语。
7｜错：words（不对举）；sentence（单数）；completing（-ing）。
8｜R21.1 rather than/instead of 对举时，取原文对应的整个短语（含定语），不截断。适用条件：空位是名词短语，rather than 后与 phrases 对举，且题面词数容纳该短语。例外（本空错误路径）：填 words（不对举）；错在把「空位是名词短语，rather than 后与 phrases 对举，且题面词数容纳该短语」当成唯一情形，一旦空位的词性/位置换成定语位，答案就要从「complete sentences」换成「complete」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→对举短语；与 R7.1 同族。与 A1「原词直取」冲突：A1 直取的「words」与 phrases 不对举；本空的具体信号是「空位是名词短语，rather than 后与 phrases 对举，且题面词数容纳该短语」；优先级：A2 整短语 ＞ A1，本空取「complete sentences」（2 词），题面未限 1 词，A19 不启动。本空特有依据：上句"won’t waste time on every little word"里的 word 诱人填 words。
10｜自检：与谁对举？原文整短语？单复数？

**37｜clearer**
1｜表"*It can help something you really want to remember become ____37____ in your long-term memory."；原文"Say something you really want to remember out loud. It can help it become clearer in your long-term memory."。空＝比较级形容词（become 后）。
2｜候选：clearer / clear / more clear / clearly。
3｜填「clearer」→「*It can help something you really want to remember become clearer in your long-term memory.」：与原文逐字合，become clearer 成立；填「more clear」→「*It can help something you really want to remember become more clear in your long-term memory.」：不合习惯，不成立；填「clearly」→「*It can help something you really want to remember become clearly in your long-term memory.」：是副词，不成立；填「clear」→「*It can help something you really want to remember become clear in your long-term memory.」：丢比较义，不成立。回读：空位是比较级形容词（become 后），只有「clearer」同时满足形态与原文对应。
4｜干扰：become 后常填原级 clear，诱人忽略原文的 -er。
5｜意图：考"become+比较级取原文"，分"填原级"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"become ____ in your memory"且原文无 -er，填 clear；有比较语境则 -er。
7｜错：clear（丢比较）；clearly（副词）；clearest（最高级无据）。
8｜R21.2 若原文用比较级（clearer）且表格保留 become，照填比较级，不降为原级。适用条件：原文用比较级（clearer）且表格保留 become。例外：若空位改由另一词性承担（名词位换动词/形容词位）；此时本条的适用前提不再成立，入空的就不再是「clearer」这一路，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.2→比较级；与 R11.5 同族。与 A6「词性/同义转换」冲突：取「more clear」属于同义转写，不是原文对应；优先级：A1 原文唯一对应 ＞ A6。本空特有依据：become 后常填原级 clear，诱人忽略原文的 -er。
10｜自检：原文有 -er 吗？become 后词性？比较级形式？

**38｜need**
1｜表"*You can find out what you ____38____ to learn by doing this."；原文"It’s a great way to find out what you need to learn."。空＝动词（what 从句谓语）。
2｜候选：need / want / have / plan。
3｜填「need」→「*You can find out what you need to learn by doing this.」：与原文逐字合，what you need to learn 成立；填「want」→「*You can find out what you want to learn by doing this.」：义近非原文，不成立；填「have」→「*You can find out what you have to learn by doing this.」：to learn 义"必须"，与"查漏"语境不合，不成立；填「plan」→「*You can find out what you plan to learn by doing this.」：无据，不成立。回读：空位是动词（what 从句谓语），只有「need」同时满足形态与原文对应。
4｜干扰：want 与 need 近义，且"想学的"更口语；have to 结构也顺。
5｜意图：考"what 从句谓语取原文"，分"按近义填 want"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"what you ____ learn"（无 to）填 must/should；有 to 用 need/want。
7｜错：want（同义替换）；needn’t（否定）；needing（-ing）。
8｜R21.3 what 从句中缺谓语时，取原文该从句的动词；近义情态动词不作替换。适用条件：空位是动词（what 从句谓语），且原文有唯一对应词。例外（本空错误路径）：填 want（同义替换）；错在把「空位是动词（what 从句谓语），且原文有唯一对应词」当成唯一情形，一旦空位的词性/位置换成无 to，答案就要从「need」换成「must/should」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→从句谓语；与 R1.3 同族。与 A6「词性/同义转换」冲突：把「want」当同义替换，会改掉原文用词；优先级：A1 原文唯一对应 ＞ A6。本空特有依据：want 与 need 近义，且"想学的"更口语。
10｜自检：从句缺什么？原文动词？后面有 to 吗？

**39｜look through**
1｜表"*Ask yourself questions when you ____39____ the information you are learning."；原文"Look through the information you’re trying to learn and ask yourself questions about the material."。空＝动词短语（when 从句谓语）。
2｜候选：look through / review / read / study。
3｜look through 与原文逐字合，"浏览"义与"提问"衔接；review 见于另一段（Repeat 段"As you review or study"），属跨段取词；read 义偏；study 同上跨段。
4｜干扰：另一段的 review/study 就在前文，诱人跨段取词；"the information"又诱人填 read。
5｜意图：考"when 从句取本段原文"，分"跨段取同义词"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"As you ____ information"（Repeat 段）填 review；本段（Test yourself）用 look through。
7｜错：review（跨段）；look（缺 through）；looking through（when 从句需谓语原形/时态一致）。
8｜R21.4 when 从句谓语取本段原文动词短语，不跨段取同义短语；短语动词须整体填。适用条件：空位是动词短语（when 从句谓语），且题面词数容纳该短语。例外（本空错误路径）：填 review（跨段）；错在把「空位是动词短语（when 从句谓语），且题面词数容纳该短语」当成唯一情形，一旦空位的词性/位置换成Repeat 段，答案就要从「look through」换成「review」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→从句谓语；与 R10.2"行内取词"同族。与 A1「原词直取」冲突：A1 的「review」见于另一段（Repeat 段"As you review or study"），属跨段取词，而本空的具体信号是「空位是动词短语（when 从句谓语），且题面词数容纳该短语」；优先级：A2 整短语 ＞ A1，答案「look through」（2 词），A19 不启动。本空特有依据：另一段的 review/study 就在前文，诱人跨段取词。
10｜自检：本段还是邻段？短语完整吗？时态？

**40｜more quickly**
1｜表"practicing in different ways can help you become better and learn ____40____."；原文"which can help make you learn more quickly"。空＝比较级副词。
2｜候选：more quickly / quickly / faster / better。
3｜more quickly 与原文逐字合，learn more quickly 成立；quickly 丢比较；faster 同义非原文；better 与前半 become better 重复。
4｜干扰：前半 become better 诱人再填 better；quickly 与 more quickly 只差一词。
5｜意图：考"比较级副词取原文"，分"填原级"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"learn ____"且原文无比较，填 quickly；有 more 语境则 more quickly。
7｜错：quickly（丢比较）；quicker（learn 用副词）；fast（同义替换）。
8｜R21.5 若原文用比较级副词（more quickly），表格保留比较语境时照填，不降级、不换同义词。适用条件：原文用比较级副词（more quickly）。例外：若「more quickly」的前半已给、只留后半截空；此时本条的适用前提不再成立，入空的就不再是「more quickly」这一路，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.2→比较级副词；与 R21.2 同族。与 A1「原词直取」冲突：A1 直取的「faster」同义非原文；本空的具体信号是「原文用比较级副词（more quickly）」；优先级：A2 整短语 ＞ A1，本空取「more quickly」（2 词），题面未限 1 词，A19 不启动。本空特有依据：前半 become better 诱人再填 better。
10｜自检：原文有 more 吗？副词还是比较级？与 better 重复吗？

**组小结（G21 · 小蓝书预测卷十）**
- 考点分布：对举整短语 1（36）；become+比较级 1（37）；what 从句谓语 1（38）；when 从句短语动词 1（39）；比较级副词 1（40）。
- 规则：R21.1 对举取整短语（36）；R21.2 比较级不降级（37）；R21.3 从句谓语取原文（38）；R21.4 本段取词不跨段（39）；R21.5 比较级副词照填（40）。
- 冲突与优先级：R21.3/R21.4 与"近义替换"冲突，原文与段界优先；R21.2/R21.5 与"原级更自然"冲突，原文级数优先。
- 未归类：无。
- 覆盖检查：本篇首次系统考"级数（比较级）不得降级"，与 R11.5 合成"比较三态"的第二例。

### 2024湖北专升本小蓝书预测卷十一 · Passage 5

As is known to all, exercise is good for your health. However, you need to do it in a scientific way. Otherwise, you can not only achieve the bad effect but also may hurt yourself. The following can help you exercise well.

Warm up. It is important to warm up before you exercise. You need to stretch your legs, arms, back and neck before you do any difficult activities like running.

Find good places to exercise. You do not need to spend a lot of money exercising. You can run or play sports in a park or in your school gym. You don't need to buy exercise equipment for your home. Just get your body moving!

Don't work too hard. Choose a sport that you are interested in and then your exercise will be fun instead of work. Also, you only need to exercise a few times each week. If you exercise for 30 minutes each time, you will stay healthy.

Drink enough water. It is important to drink water when you exercise. Your body needs the water for energy and for keeping cool.

Be careful! If you hurt yourself while you are exercising, rest until you feel better. Exercising when you are hurt is bad for your health.

Make exercise plans. Exercising can be difficult because it is tiring. You may sweat a lot and not feel like moving at all after exercising. Therefore, a good and effective plan is very important for you to control your speed. You can take care of your own body better in this way,

| How to exercise well. | |
|---|---|
| The problem | You need to exercise in a ____36____ way. |
| The ways of exercising well | Warm up: stretch your body before any ____37____ activities. |
|  | Find good places to exercise: you do not need to spend ____38____ exercising. |
|  | You can run or play sports in a park or in your school gym |



|  | Don't work too hard: exercise for spending ____39____ each time; do it a few times a week |
|  | Drink enough water: drink water during your exercise |
|  | Be careful: if you hurt yourself while you are exercising, rest until you ____40____. |
|  | Make exercise plans: you can take care of your own body better. |

**〔G22〕小蓝书预测卷十一 · Passage 5（36—40）十层研究**

> **【3 词豁免】** 本组 Directions 未限 3 词，故不压缩：38 空 4 词（"a lot of money"）按原文整短语入空。

**36｜scientific**
1｜表"The problem｜You need to exercise in a ____36____ way."；原文"you need to do it in a scientific way"。空＝形容词。
2｜候选：scientific / right / proper / healthy。
3｜填「scientific」→「The problem｜You need to exercise in a scientific way.」：与原文逐字合，in a scientific way 成立；填「healthy」→「The problem｜You need to exercise in a healthy way.」：与"运动有益健康"重复，不成立；填「proper」→「The problem｜You need to exercise in a proper way.」：义泛非原文，不成立；填「right」→「The problem｜You need to exercise in a right way.」：义泛非原文，不成立。回读：空位是形容词，只有「scientific」同时满足形态与原文对应。
4｜干扰：首句"exercise is good for your health"诱人填 healthy；"Otherwise…hurt yourself"诱人填 safe。
5｜意图：考"in a+adj+way 取原文形容词"，分"按常识填 healthy/safe"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"exercise ____"（副词位）填 scientifically；in a ____ way 用形容词。
7｜错：healthy（主题词替换）；scientifically（副词入形容词位）；science（名词）。
8｜R22.1 若空位是 in a ____ way，填形容词，取原文该短语的形容词。适用条件：空位是 in a ____ way。例外：若空位改由另一词性承担（名词位换动词/形容词位）；此时本条的适用前提不再成立，入空的就不再是「scientific」这一路，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→方式状语；与 R3.1 同族。与 A6「词性/同义转换」冲突：按「right」改写，词性/搭配与原位不合；优先级：A1 原文唯一对应 ＞ A6。本空特有依据：首句"exercise is good for your health"诱人填 healthy。
10｜自检：a 与 way 之间要什么词性？原文形容词？主题词干扰？

**37｜difficult**
1｜表"Warm up: stretch your body before any ____37____ activities."；原文"before you do any difficult activities like running"。空＝形容词。
2｜候选：difficult / sports / outdoor / tiring。
3｜填「difficult」→「Warm up: stretch your body before any difficult activities.」：与原文逐字合，成立；填「outdoor」→「Warm up: stretch your body before any outdoor activities.」：无据，不成立；填「sports」→「Warm up: stretch your body before any sports activities.」：义偏（activities 已含运动），不成立；填「tiring」→「Warm up: stretch your body before any tiring activities.」：是结果非分类，不成立。回读：空位是形容词，只有「difficult」同时满足形态与原文对应。
4｜干扰：like running 诱人填 sports/running；"warm up"的常识诱人填 tiring。
5｜意图：考"any+adj+名词取原文"，分"按举例反推类别"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"any ____ like running"（名词位）填 sports；形容词位取 difficult。
7｜错：sport（单数/词性）；difficulty（名词）；hardly（副词）。
8｜R22.2 若空位是 any 与名词之间的形容词位，取原文该名词的定语，不由 like 后的举例反推类别词。适用条件：空位是 any 与名词之间的形容词位。例外（本空错误路径）：填 sport（单数/词性）；错在把「空位是 any 与名词之间的形容词位」当成唯一情形，一旦空位的词性/位置换成名词位，答案就要从「difficult」换成「sports」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→定语；与 R13.3"同段多定语"同族。与 A6「词性/同义转换」冲突：换「sports」更顺口，但原文用的是另一个词；优先级：A1 原文唯一对应 ＞ A6。本空特有依据：like running 诱人填 sports/running。
10｜自检：any 后是形容词还是名词？原文定语？举例干扰？

**38｜a lot of money**
1｜表"Find good places to exercise: you do not need to spend ____38____ exercising."；原文"You do not need to spend a lot of money exercising."。空＝spend 的宾语。
2｜候选：a lot of money / much / money / time。
3｜a lot of money 与原文逐字合，spend+宾语+doing 成立；much 单独不成立（须 much money）；money 丢 a lot of；time 与下句"a few times"混。
4｜干扰：spend 后最顺的是 time（spend time doing 高频），诱人；下一句"30 minutes each time"又强化 time。
5｜意图：考"spend 的宾语按原文而非搭配惯性"，分"见 spend 填 time"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"spend ____ exercising"且原文谈时间，填 time；本段谈"不必花钱"，故 money。
7｜错：time（搭配惯性）；much（缺名词）；moneys（不可数）。
8｜R22.3 spend 后宾语由本段主题定（钱/时间），取原文宾语短语，不按 spend time doing 的惯性填。适用条件：空位是spend 的宾语，且题面词数容纳该短语。例外（本空错误路径）：填 time（搭配惯性）；错在把「空位是spend 的宾语，且题面词数容纳该短语」当成唯一情形，换到那种位置时，入空的恰恰是「time」，而不是「a lot of money」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→动宾；与 R13.4 同族。与 A1「原词直取」冲突：A1 的「much」单独不成立（须 much money），而本空的具体信号是「空位是spend 的宾语，且题面词数容纳该短语」；优先级：A2 整短语 ＞ A1，答案「a lot of money」（4 词），A19 不启动。本空特有依据：spend 后最顺的是 time（spend time doing 高频），诱人。
10｜自检：本段讲钱还是时间？原文宾语？短语完整吗？

**39｜30 minutes**
1｜表"Don't work too hard: exercise for spending ____39____ each time; do it a few times a week"；原文"If you exercise for 30 minutes each time, you will stay healthy."。空＝时间量。
2｜候选：30 minutes / a few times / 30 / hours。
3｜30 minutes 与原文逐字合，for 30 minutes each time 成立；a few times 已是后半信息；30 缺单位；hours 与"don't work too hard"相反。
4｜干扰：前半"only need to exercise a few times each week"的 a few times 最近，诱人；30 与 minutes 分离易只填数字。
5｜意图：考"数字+单位整体取词"，分"只填 30"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"exercise for ____ minutes"（minutes 已给）只填 30；空后无单位须补 minutes。
7｜错：30（缺单位）；minutes（缺数字）；30 minute（单复数）。
8｜R22.4 时间量空须"数字+单位"整体，除非表格已给单位。适用条件：空位是时间量，须按原文的数字与格式整体取词。例外：若同段另有「a few times」且栏头/单位指向它；此时本条的适用前提不再成立，入空的就不再是「30 minutes」这一路，本条不成立，改按 A11（数字按单位配对）。
9｜04→RC-1.4→数字+单位；与 R18.5 同族。与 A7「行内/段内取词」冲突：看到「a few times」就填，会忽略本栏的单位；优先级：A11 数字按单位/栏头配对 ＞ A7 就近。本空特有依据：前半"only need to exercise a few times each week"的 a few times 最近，诱人。
10｜自检：单位给了吗？原文数值？与后半重复吗？

**40｜feel better**
1｜表"Be careful: if you hurt yourself while you are exercising, rest until you ____40____."；原文"rest until you feel better"。空＝从句谓语。
2｜候选：feel better / feel good / recover / stop hurting。
3｜feel better 与原文逐字合，until you feel better 成立；feel good 时态义偏（"感觉好"非"好转"）；recover 义近非原文；stop hurting 义近但原文 feel better。
4｜干扰：hurt yourself 诱人填 recover/stop hurting；until 从句又常填 feel well。
5｜意图：考"until 从句取原文谓语"，分"按'康复'义填 recover"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"until you ____ well"填 feel；本处须补 better。
7｜错：recover（同义替换）；feel good（义偏）；feeling better（从句需谓语）。
8｜R22.5 until 从句缺谓语时，取原文该从句的动词短语，近义动词不作替换。适用条件：空位是从句谓语，且题面词数容纳该短语。例外：若空位只要求「feel better」中的一个词；此时本条的适用前提不再成立，入空的就不再是「feel better」这一路，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→从句谓语；与 R21.3 同族。与 A1「原词直取」冲突：冲突对象是 A1 直取的「feel good」：它时态义偏（"感觉好"非"好转"）；本空的具体信号是「空位是从句谓语，且题面词数容纳该短语」，入空的应是整短语；优先级：A2 整短语 ＞ A1（本空「feel better」，2 词）。本空特有依据：hurt yourself 诱人填 recover/stop hurting。
10｜自检：从句缺什么？原文短语？比较级 better？

**组小结（G22 · 小蓝书预测卷十一）**
- 考点分布：in a ___ way 1（36）；any+adj+名词 1（37）；spend 宾语按主题 1（38）；数字+单位 1（39）；until 从句谓语 1（40）。
- 规则：R22.1 in a ___ way 填形容词（36）；R22.2 any 后形容词取原文（37）；R22.3 spend 宾语按本段主题（38）；R22.4 时间量整体（39）；R22.5 until 从句取原文（40）。
- 冲突与优先级：R22.3 与"spend time doing 惯性"冲突，段主题优先；R22.4 与"只填数字"冲突，单位优先。
- 未归类：无。
- 覆盖检查：本篇第二次考"数字+单位"，并首次考"spend 宾语的主题约束"。

### 2024湖北专升本小蓝书预测卷十七 · Passage 5

Too many people think that intelligence (智力) is a gift of nature and that there is little anyone can do to improve theirs. In fact, it's not. Just like swimming, cooking, dancing, and many other things, being smart is a skill that requires training. I discovered some of the most important tools to be smart. Now I will share them with you.

**Practice self-critical thinking**

Critical (批判性的) thinking is the act of questioning the arguments that are put before us. It is a valuable skill—without it, we will get influenced easily. When we use critical thinking to our own ideas, we use self-critical thinking. If you practice self-critical thinking, you can come up with a better idea.

**Be persistent (坚持不懈的)**

Smart people don't give up. They believe that they can do it, so they spend months, even years trying until they succeed finally. You might know that Thomas Edison failed thousands



of times before he succeeded. Actually, almost all scientists experience failure lots of times. So if you want to be smart, be persistent.

**Learn to express your ideas**

You may have your good ideas, but if you don't sell your ideas, no one will know their worth and consider you smart. So you need to learn how to express your ideas. Express your thoughts in a proper way, and most importantly, let your enthusiasm (热情) about your ideas shine through.

Finally, you must also believe in yourself. Those who appear smarter than you simply have used the right tools to exercise their minds. You are smarter than you think.

| Practice self-critical thinking◆With critical thinking, we won't get influenced easily. |  |
|---|---|
| ◆When we use self-critical thinking, we'll come up with a ______36______ |  |
| **Being smart:A skill that requires training** |  |
| **Be persistent**◆Smart people never give up until they ______37______<br>◆Almost all scientists ______38______ lots of times. | **Learn to express your idea**◆You need to sell your ideas to let others know ______39______<br>◆Express your thoughts in a ______40______ with enthusiasm. |

**〔G23〕小蓝书预测卷十七 · Passage 5（36—40）十层研究**

**36｜better idea**
1｜表"◆When we use self-critical thinking, we’ll come up with a ____36____"；原文"If you practice self-critical thinking, you can come up with a better idea."。空＝名词短语（a 后）。
2｜候选：better idea / good idea / idea / better thought。
3｜better idea 与原文逐字合，come up with a better idea 成立；good idea 丢比较义；idea 丢 better；better thought 同义非原文。
4｜干扰：段首"you can come up with a better idea"与"we’ll come up with a ____"人称改换，学生易只填 idea。
5｜意图：考"a+比较级+名词整体取词"，分"只取中心词"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"come up with a ____ idea"填 better（定语位）；空在 a 后则整短语入空。
7｜错：idea（丢比较）；good idea（降级）；better ideas（单数语境）。
8｜R23.1 若空位紧跟 a 且原文是"a+比较级+名词"，取整短语，不截取中心词、不降级。适用条件：空位紧跟 a 且原文是"a+比较级+名词"。例外（本空错误路径）：填 idea（丢比较）；错在把「空位紧跟 a 且原文是"a+比较级+名词"」当成唯一情形，一旦空位的词性/位置换成定语位，答案就要从「better idea」换成「better」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→整短语；与 R16.3 同族。与 A1「原词直取」冲突：本空的具体信号是「空位紧跟 a 且原文是"a+比较级+名词"」，A1 却只取「good idea」（丢比较义）；优先级：A2 整短语 ＞ A1，本空以「better idea」（2 词）整体入空。本空特有依据：段首"you can come up with a better idea"与"we’ll come up with a ____"人称改换，学生易只填 idea。
10｜自检：a 后是整短语吗？有比较级吗？单复数？

**37｜succeed**
1｜表"◆Smart people never give up until they ____37____"；原文"they spend months, even years trying until they succeed finally"。空＝从句谓语（原形/现在时）。
2｜候选：succeed / succeed finally / win / make it。
3｜succeed 与原文逐字合，until they succeed 成立；succeed finally 与表格已删 finally 重复且超词；win 义偏（赢比赛）；make it 口语且非原文。
4｜干扰：前半"never give up"诱人填 give up（反义）；"trying"诱人填 try。
5｜意图：考"until 从句取原文谓语"，分"抄 trying"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"until they ____ up"填 give（give up）；无 up 时用 succeed。
7｜错：give up（反义）；success（名词）；succeeding（从句需谓语）。
8｜R23.2 until 从句缺谓语时取原文动词；注意 never…until 的肯定结果义（succeed），不填 give up。适用条件：空位是从句谓语（原形/现在时），形态由空前结构定。例外（本空错误路径）：填 give up（反义）；错在把「空位是从句谓语（原形/现在时），形态由空前结构定」当成唯一情形，一旦空位的词性/位置换成give up，答案就要从「succeed」换成「give」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→从句谓语；与 R22.5 同族。与 A1「原词直取」冲突：「win」的形态与本空要求差一层（-ing/原形/过去式）；优先级：A3 空位词性/形态 ＞ A1。本空特有依据：前半"never give up"诱人填 give up（反义）。
10｜自检：从句缺谓语？原文动词？never…until 的语义？

**38｜experience failure**
1｜表"◆Almost all scientists ____38____ lots of times."；原文"Actually, almost all scientists experience failure lots of times."。空＝动宾。
2｜候选：experience failure / fail / have failure / experience failures。
3｜experience failure 与原文逐字合，动宾+lots of times 成立；fail 是动词，与 lots of times 搭配弱且丢宾语；have failure 搭配不成立；experience failures 与原文不可数不合。
4｜干扰：上句"Thomas Edison failed thousands of times"的 failed 诱人填 fail；中文"经历失败"诱人只填 experience。
5｜意图：考"动宾整体取词＋不可数"，分"只填动词"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"____ failure lots of times"填 experience（动词位）；空后无宾语则须补 failure。
7｜错：fail（缺宾语）；experience（缺宾语）；failures（不可数）。
8｜R23.3 若原文是"动词+不可数名词"的动宾结构，表格空位在主语后时取整动宾，名词不加复数。适用条件：原文是"动词+不可数名词"的动宾结构。例外（本空错误路径）：填 fail（缺宾语）；错在把「原文是"动词+不可数名词"的动宾结构」当成唯一情形，一旦空位的词性/位置换成动词位，答案就要从「experience failure」换成「experience」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→动宾；与 R16.4 同族。与 A1「原词直取」冲突：A1 直取的「have failure」搭配不成立；本空的具体信号是「原文是"动词+不可数名词"的动宾结构」；优先级：A2 整短语 ＞ A1，本空取「experience failure」（2 词），题面未限 1 词，A19 不启动。本空特有依据：上句"Thomas Edison failed thousands of times"的 failed 诱人填 fail。
10｜自检：空后有无宾语？名词可数吗？原文动宾？

**39｜their worth**
1｜表"◆You need to sell your ideas to let others know ____39____"；原文"if you don’t sell your ideas, no one will know their worth and consider you smart"。空＝宾语（their worth）。
2｜候选：their worth / your worth / the worth / their value。
3｜their worth 与原文逐字合，know their worth 成立；your worth 人称错（sell your ideas 后指 ideas 的价值）；the worth 缺限定；their value 同义非原文。
4｜干扰：前半"your ideas"诱人填 your worth；中文"价值"诱人填 value。
5｜意图：考"人称指代＋取原文名词"，分"人称弄错"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"know the ____ of your ideas"填 worth/value（the 后）；无 of 时 their worth 整体。
7｜错：your worth（人称错）；worth（缺限定）；worthy（形容词）。
8｜R23.4 代词回指须与原文一致（their＝ideas 的）；worth 在此为名词，不作形容词。适用条件：空位是宾语（their worth），且题面词数容纳该短语。例外（本空错误路径）：填 your worth（人称错）；错在把「空位是宾语（their worth），且题面词数容纳该短语」当成唯一情形，一旦空位的词性/位置换成the 后，答案就要从「their worth」换成「worth/value」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→代词回指；与 R23.5 并列。与 A1「原词直取」冲突：冲突对象是 A1 直取的「your worth」：它人称错（sell your ideas 后指 ideas 的价值）；本空的具体信号是「空位是宾语（their worth），且题面词数容纳该短语」，入空的应是整短语；优先级：A2 整短语 ＞ A1（本空「their worth」，2 词）。本空特有依据：前半"your ideas"诱人填 your worth。
10｜自检：their 指谁？worth 是名词吗？原文短语？

**40｜proper way**
1｜表"◆Express your thoughts in a ____40____ with enthusiasm."；原文"Express your thoughts in a proper way, and most importantly, let your enthusiasm (热情) about your ideas shine through."。空＝名词短语（a 后，与 with enthusiasm 并列）。
2｜候选：proper way / good way / way / proper manner。
3｜填「proper way」→「◆Express your thoughts in a proper way with enthusiasm.」：与原文逐字合，in a proper way 成立；填「proper manner」→「◆Express your thoughts in a proper manner with enthusiasm.」：同义非原文，不成立；填「good way」→「◆Express your thoughts in a good way with enthusiasm.」：同义降级，不成立；填「way」→「◆Express your thoughts in a way with enthusiasm.」：丢 proper，不成立。回读：空位是名词短语，只有「proper way」同时满足形态与原文对应。
4｜干扰：with enthusiasm 是介词短语，学生易把空填成形容词 proper 而漏 way（in a proper with…不成立）。
5｜意图：考"a+形容词+名词整体＋介词搭配"，分"只填形容词"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"in a ____ way with enthusiasm"（way 已给）填 proper；空在 a 后且 way 未给则整短语。
7｜错：proper（缺名词）；properly（副词）；proper ways（单数语境）。
8｜R23.5 若空位在 a 与介词短语之间，须补"形容词+名词"整体，保证 in a … way 结构完整。适用条件：空位在 a 与介词短语之间。例外：若同栏另给一空分担「proper way」的一半；此时本条的适用前提不再成立，入空的就不再是「proper way」这一路，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→整短语；与 R23.1 同族。与 A1「原词直取」冲突：本空的具体信号是「空位在 a 与介词短语之间」，A1 却只取「good way」（同义降级，不成立）；优先级：A2 整短语 ＞ A1，本空以「proper way」（2 词）整体入空。本空特有依据：with enthusiasm 是介词短语，学生易把空填成形容词 proper 而漏 way（in a proper with…不成立）。
10｜自检：a 后缺名词吗？原文短语？介词搭配完整吗？

**组小结（G23 · 小蓝书预测卷十七）**
- 考点分布：a+比较级+名词 1（36）；until 从句谓语 1（37）；动宾不可数 1（38）；代词回指 1（39）；a+adj+名词 1（40）。
- 规则：R23.1 a 后取整短语不降级（36）；R23.2 until 从句取原文、never…until 表肯定结果（37）；R23.3 动宾不可数不加 s（38）；R23.4 代词回指按原文（39）；R23.5 a 与介词之间补整短语（40）。
- 冲突与优先级：R23.1/R23.5 与"只取中心词"冲突，短语完整性优先；R23.2 与"never 的否定直觉"冲突，结果义优先。
- 未归类：无。
- 覆盖检查：本篇把"整短语"规则推到 4 例（36、38、39、40），可与 R7.1、R16.3 合并为一条总规则。

### 2024湖北专升本小蓝书预测卷十三 · Passage 5

Research has shown that when we volunteer — where we devote ourselves to helping others — we actually end up benefiting too. Our happiness grows. We feel more satisfied with life. We even have more energy and creativity. Basically, we have more to give.

I've noticed a pattern with my friends. Sometimes, they get into the habit of gossiping about others. They will constantly complain about their bosses or coworkers. It seems as if they get caught in this loop(闭环) of complaining, and their frustration distracts them in other parts of their life. When this happens, they become uninterested in new events or become annoyed by their colleagues or things that happen at work.

Meanwhile, I've also seen other friends go out of their way to help someone on their team or to help out on a project that have very little — or even nothing — to do with their main work. Although it seems like they won't receive anything in return, my friends benefit from these interactions. My friends have told me about the stronger relationships they've built at work and the fun they had working on new and interesting projects. They've told me about how the people they helped before have returned the favor later on. Not only that, but they have even talked about times where their boss recognized them for their work or when they were informed in advance about upcoming projects.

We often think that when we help someone else, we aren't doing anything for ourselves.



But we actually often end up getting back just as much as we give. When we step in to help someone, we often turn out to be helping ourselves as well.

| Research has shown that ____36____ we help others, we end up ____37____ too. | Our happiness grows. | We have more to give. |
|---|---|---|
|  | We feel more satisfied. |  |
|  | We have more ____38____. | We often turn out to be ____40____. |
|  | We build ____39____. |  |

**〔G24〕小蓝书预测卷十三 · Passage 5（36—40）十层研究**

**36｜when**
1｜表"Research has shown that ____36____ we help others, we end up ____37____ too."；原文"Research has shown that when we volunteer… we actually end up benefiting too."。空＝连词（引导时间/条件状语从句）。
2｜候选：when / if / while / as。
3｜填「when」→「Research has shown that when we help others, we end up ____37____ too.」：与原文逐字合，成立；填「while」→「Research has shown that while we help others, we end up ____37____ too.」：强调同时，不成立；填「if」→「Research has shown that if we help others, we end up ____37____ too.」：表条件，弱化"每次帮助都会"的规律义，不成立；填「as」→「Research has shown that as we help others, we end up ____37____ too.」：亦可但原文 when，不成立。回读：空位是连词（引导时间/条件状语从句），只有「when」同时满足形态与原文对应。
4｜干扰：中文"当我们帮助别人时"可对应 when/while/as 三个，只有原文 when。
5｜意图：考"从句连词取原文"，分"按中文对应任选"者。
6｜边界：若从句用进行时（we were helping）用 while；一般现在时用 when。
7｜错：while（同时性过强）；if（条件化）；that（不引导状语从句）。
8｜R24.1 状语从句连词取原文；when/while/as 的区别在时态与"规律 vs 同时"。适用条件：空位是连词（引导时间/条件状语从句），且原文有唯一对应词。例外（本空错误路径）：填 while（同时性过强）；错在把「空位是连词（引导时间/条件状语从句），且原文有唯一对应词」当成唯一情形，本条的适用前提不再成立，入空的就不再是「when」这一路，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-2 提纲填空→连词；与 R1.1 同属"框架词取原文"。与 A6「词性/同义转换」冲突：取「if」属于同义转写，不是原文对应；优先级：A1 原文唯一对应 ＞ A6。本空特有依据：中文"当我们帮助别人时"可对应 when/while/as 三个，只有原文 when。
10｜自检：从句时态？原文连词？语义是规律还是同时？

**37｜benefiting**
1｜表"…we end up ____37____ too."；原文"we actually end up benefiting too"。空＝V-ing（end up 后）。
2｜候选：benefiting / benefited / to benefit / benefit。
3｜填「benefiting」→「…we end up benefiting too.」：与原文逐字合，end up doing 固定结构，成立；填「to benefit」→「…we end up to benefit too.」：不成立；填「benefited」→「…we end up benefited too.」：不合 end up 搭配，不成立；填「benefit」→「…we end up benefit too.」：原形不成立。回读：空位是V-ing（end up 后），只有「benefiting」同时满足形态与原文对应。
4｜干扰：end up 后常被误接原形或 to do；"benefit"作名词也常见。
5｜意图：考"end up+V-ing"，分"填原形"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"we ____ from it"填 benefit（谓语）；end up 后必 -ing。
7｜错：benefit（原形）；to benefit（不定式）；benefitted（拼写/时态）。
8｜R24.2 end up / finish / enjoy 等后接 V-ing；取原文 -ing 形式。适用条件：空位是V-ing（end up 后），形态由空前结构定。例外（本空错误路径）：填 benefit（原形）；错在把「空位是V-ing（end up 后），形态由空前结构定」当成唯一情形，一旦空位的词性/位置换成谓语，答案就要从「benefiting」换成「benefit」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→V-ing；与 R3.3 同族。与 A1「原词直取」冲突：直取「benefited」保留原形态，与空前结构不合；优先级：A3 空位词性/形态 ＞ A1。本空特有依据：end up 后常被误接原形或 to do。
10｜自检：end up 后形态？原文 -ing？拼写？

**38｜energy and creativity**
1｜表"We have more ____38____."；原文"We even have more energy and creativity."。空＝并列名词短语。
2｜候选：energy and creativity / energy / creativity / energy, creativity。
3｜填「energy and creativity」→「We have more energy and creativity.」：与原文逐字合，成立；填「energy, creativity」→「We have more energy, creativity.」：原文无此形式，不成立；填「creativity」→「We have more creativity.」：丢一半，不成立；填「energy」→「We have more energy.」：丢一半，不成立。回读：空位是并列名词短语，只有「energy and creativity」同时满足形态与原文对应。
4｜干扰：中文常只说"精力"，诱人只填 energy。
5｜意图：考"并列两项整体取词"，分"取一半"者。
6｜边界：若表格分成两行（We have more energy / We have more ____）则各填一项。
7｜错：energy（漏项）；energies（不可数）；creative（形容词）。
8｜R24.3 原文并列两项而表格只有一空时，两项全填（用 and），不取一半。适用条件：空位是并列名词短语，且题面词数容纳该短语。例外（本空错误路径）：填 energy（漏项）；错在把「空位是并列名词短语，且题面词数容纳该短语」当成唯一情形，换到那种位置时，入空的恰恰是「一项」，而不是「energy and creativity」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→并列整体；与 R1.2 同族。与 A1「原词直取」冲突：冲突对象是 A1 直取的「energy」：它丢一半，不成立；本空的具体信号是「空位是并列名词短语，且题面词数容纳该短语」，入空的应是整短语；优先级：A2 整短语 ＞ A1（本空「energy and creativity」，3 词）。本空特有依据：中文常只说"精力"，诱人只填 energy。
10｜自检：原文并列几项？表格几空？用 and 吗？

**39｜stronger relationships**
1｜表"We build ____39____."；原文"the stronger relationships they’ve built at work"。空＝名词短语（含比较级）。
2｜候选：stronger relationships / relationships / strong relationships / friendship。
3｜stronger relationships 与原文逐字合，build 的宾语；relationships 丢 stronger；strong relationships 降级；friendship 义偏（工作关系≠友谊）。
4｜干扰：下文"the people they helped before have returned the favor"诱人填 friendship。
5｜意图：考"比较级定语+名词整体取词"，分"降级为 strong"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"build ____ relationships"（relationships 已给）填 stronger。
7｜错：relationships（丢比较）；strong relationships（降级）；relationship（单数）。
8｜R24.4 原文含比较级定语时，整短语入空且保留比较级；不降级、不换同义词。适用条件：空位是名词短语（含比较级），且题面词数容纳该短语。例外：若空前已给「stronger relationships」的中心词、只剩修饰语位；此时本条的适用前提不再成立，入空的就不再是「stronger relationships」这一路，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→整短语；与 R23.1 同族。与 A1「原词直取」冲突：本空的具体信号是「空位是名词短语（含比较级），且题面词数容纳该短语」，A1 却只取「strong relationships」（降级）；优先级：A2 整短语 ＞ A1，本空以「stronger relationships」（2 词）整体入空。本空特有依据：下文"the people they helped before have returned the favor"诱人填 friendship。
10｜自检：有比较级吗？单复数？同义替换了吗？

**40｜helping ourselves**
1｜表"We often turn out to be ____40____."；原文"When we step in to help someone, we often turn out to be helping ourselves as well."。空＝V-ing 短语（be 后）。
2｜候选：helping ourselves / helped / helping others / selfish。
3｜helping ourselves 与原文逐字合，turn out to be doing 成立；helped 被动/过去不合；helping others 方向反；selfish 义偏。
4｜干扰：全文讲"帮助他人"，诱人填 helping others；中文"其实是帮自己"诱人填 selfish。
5｜意图：考"turn out to be+V-ing 取原文＋反身代词"，分"填 helping others"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"we often turn out to ____ ourselves"填 help（to be 已给时不适用）；be 后接 -ing 表进行。
7｜错：helping others（方向反）；helped（时态/语态）；help ourselves（缺 be 后 -ing）。
8｜R24.5 turn out to be 后接 V-ing 时取原文短语；反身代词 ourselves 随主语 we。适用条件：空位是V-ing 短语（be 后），且题面词数容纳该短语。例外（本空错误路径）：填 helping others（方向反）；错在把「空位是V-ing 短语（be 后），且题面词数容纳该短语」当成唯一情形，换到那种位置时，入空的恰恰是「help」，而不是「helping ourselves」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→V-ing 短语；与 R24.2 同族。与 A1「原词直取」冲突：A1 的「helped」被动/过去不合，而本空的具体信号是「空位是V-ing 短语（be 后），且题面词数容纳该短语」；优先级：A2 整短语 ＞ A1，答案「helping ourselves」（2 词），A19 不启动。本空特有依据：全文讲"帮助他人"，诱人填 helping others。
10｜自检：be 后形态？方向（自己/他人）？代词？

**组小结（G24 · 小蓝书预测卷十三）**
- 考点分布：状语从句连词 1（36）；end up+V-ing 1（37）；并列两项整体 1（38）；比较级定语整体 1（39）；turn out to be+V-ing 1（40）。
- 规则：R24.1 连词取原文（36）；R24.2 end up 后 -ing（37）；R24.3 并列两项全填（38）；R24.4 比较级定语不降级（39）；R24.5 be 后 -ing 与反身代词（40）。
- 冲突与优先级：R24.3/R24.4 与"取一半/降级"冲突，完整性优先；R24.5 与"主题词（helping others）"冲突，原文方向优先。
- 未归类：无。
- 覆盖检查：本篇是"整体取词"的第三例（G16、G23、G24），该规则支撑数已达 10+，可独立成条。

### 2024湖北专升本小蓝书预测卷十九 · Passage 5

Washington D.C. is the capital of the United States. It's the center of national government. It's a busy, working city with a large population. But what is more, Washington is also a city of history and culture.



Washington was created in 1790, when the Congress (the highest lawmaking body of the U.S.A.) decided to place the nation's new capital in the east of the country. It was built half-way between the northern and southern states of America. And of course it was named after George Washington, the first President of the United States.

There are many tourist sights in Washington. Perhaps the most important tourist sight is the Capital Building. This house is where is the Congress.

Another world-famous building in Washington is the White House. This is the home and office of the President of the United States. Most visitors are, however, surprised by how small the White House actually is.

Washington is also a great culture city. The Library of Congress contains one of the largest library collections in the world. The Smithsonian Museum holds the nation's largest collection of cultural materials. And the John F. Kennedy Center is a famous center of art and culture. It has many great musical. and theatre performances throughout the year.

**Now fill in the table with some information about Washington D. C. (注意：每空不要超过 3 个单词)**

**Washington D. C.**

| History and location | Created in ______36______ when the Congress decided that, and named after ______37______ | Lies halfway between ______38______ and southern states of America |
|---|---|---|
| A capital city | Center of the ______39______ government |  |
| A ______40______ city | The Smithsonian Museum has the nation's largest collection of cultural materials | John F. Kennedy Center is a center famous for art and culture |

**〔G25〕小蓝书预测卷十九 · Passage 5（36—40）十层研究**

> 本篇表前注明"每空不要超过 3 个单词"，与真题"每空 1 词"不同，属 3 词约束题型。

**36｜1790**
1｜表"History and location｜Created in ____36____ when the Congress decided that…"；原文"Washington was created in 1790, when the Congress…"。空＝年份。
2｜候选：1790 / 1970 / the 1790s / 1790 year。
3｜填「1790」→「History and location｜Created in 1790 when the Congress decided that…」：与原文逐字合，in 1790 成立；填「the 1790s」→「History and location｜Created in the 1790s when the Congress decided that…」：是十年，语义变，不成立；填「1790 year」→「History and location｜Created in 1790 year when the Congress decided that…」：结构错，不成立；填「1970」→「History and location｜Created in 1970 when the Congress decided that…」：无据，不成立。回读：空位是年份，只有「1790」同时满足形态与原文对应。
4｜干扰：同段"the first President"与"1790"数字相近的年份易混；in 后学生常加 the。
5｜意图：考"年份直取、不加冠词"，分"写 in the 1790"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"in the ____"（有 the）填 1790s 才通；无 the 时用具体年份。
7｜错：the 1790（多冠词）；1790s（十年）；1970（邻数）。
8｜R25.1 年份空直接取原文数字，in 后不加 the；只有"in the + 复数年份"才用 -s。适用条件：空位是年份，须按原文的数字与格式整体取词。例外：若同段另有「1970」且栏头/单位指向它；此时本条的适用前提不再成立，入空的就不再是「1790」这一路，本条不成立，改按 A11（数字按单位配对）。
9｜04→RC-1.4→年份；与 R18.5 同族。与 A7「行内/段内取词」冲突：就近取「1970」会与栏头/单位错配；优先级：A11 数字按单位/栏头配对 ＞ A7 就近。本空特有依据：同段"the first President"与"1790"数字相近的年份易混。
10｜自检：有 the 吗？四位数年份？原文数字？

**37｜George Washington**
1｜表"…and named after ____37____"；原文"And of course it was named after George Washington, the first President of the United States."。空＝专名。
2｜候选：George Washington / Washington / the first President / George。
3｜George Washington 与原文逐字合，named after+人名；Washington 与地名同形，歧义；the first President 是同位语不是名字；George 只是名。
4｜干扰：城市名 Washington 与总统名同形，诱人只填 Washington；同位语 the first President 紧随其后。
5｜意图：考"named after 取全名、避地名同形"，分"填 Washington"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"named after the first ____"填 President；named after 后接全名。
7｜错：Washington（地名同形）；President（职位）；george washington（未大写）。
8｜R25.2 named after 后填被命名者的全名；与地名同形时须补全名以消歧；专名首字母大写。适用条件：空位是专名，且原文以同一书写形式出现。例外（本空错误路径）：填 Washington（地名同形）；错在把「空位是专名，且原文以同一书写形式出现」当成唯一情形，换到那种位置时，入空的恰恰是「President」，而不是「George Washington」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.4→专名；与 R5.1 同族。与 A2「整短语不截断」冲突：按「the first President」改写，专名形式与原文不合；优先级：A12 专名按原文形式 ＞ A2。本空特有依据：城市名 Washington 与总统名同形，诱人只填 Washington。
10｜自检：全名还是地名？大写？限定 3 词内吗？

**38｜the northern states**
1｜表"Lies halfway between ____38____ and southern states of America"；原文"It was built half-way between the northern and southern states of America."。空＝名词短语（与 southern states 并列）。
2｜候选：the northern states / northern states / the north / the northern。
3｜the northern states 与原文 northern…states 对应，与 southern states 并列，3 词内；northern states 缺 the；the north 义偏（地区）；the northern 缺中心词。
4｜干扰：中文"北方"诱人填 the north；并列结构诱人只填 northern。
5｜意图：考"并列短语补齐＋3 词约束"，分"只填形容词"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"between the ____ and southern states"（states 已给）填 northern；空后已有 states 时不重复。
7｜错：the north（缺 states）；northern（缺 the 与中心词）；Northern States（大小写，非专名）。
8｜R25.3 并列短语若一方已给（southern states），另一方补全"限定词+形容词+中心词"，3 词内；已给的成分不重复。适用条件：空位是名词短语（与 southern states 并列），且题面词数容纳该短语。例外：若空位只覆盖「northern states」这半截（前半已给）；此时本条的适用前提不再成立，入空的就不再是「the northern states」这一路，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→并列短语；与 R1.2 同族。与 A1「原词直取」冲突：本空的具体信号是「空位是名词短语（与 southern states 并列），且题面词数容纳该短语」，A1 却只取「northern states」（缺 the）；优先级：A2 整短语 ＞ A1，本空以「the northern states」（3 词）整体入空。本空特有依据：中文"北方"诱人填 the north。
10｜自检：并列的另一方给了什么？缺哪部分？超 3 词吗？

**39｜national**
1｜表"A capital city｜Center of the ____39____ government"；原文"It’s the center of national government."。空＝形容词。
2｜候选：national / federal / American / central。
3｜填「national」→「A capital city｜Center of the national government」：与原文逐字合，成立；填「American」→「A capital city｜Center of the American government」：义偏，不成立；填「federal」→「A capital city｜Center of the federal government」：义近（联邦），但原文 national，不成立；填「central」→「A capital city｜Center of the central government」：与 center 重复，不成立。回读：空位是形容词，只有「national」同时满足形态与原文对应。
4｜干扰：美国语境下 federal 更常见，诱人；center 与 central 同根，诱人重复。
5｜意图：考"取原文形容词、避同根重复"，分"按国情填 federal"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"the ____ government"且原文用 federal，填 federal；本条原文 national。
7｜错：federal（同义替换）；nation（名词）；nationally（副词）。
8｜R25.4 形容词空取原文词；避免与句中其他词同根（center→central）。适用条件：空位是形容词，且原文有唯一对应词。例外（本空错误路径）：填 federal（同义替换）；错在把「空位是形容词，且原文有唯一对应词」当成唯一情形，换到那种位置时，入空的恰恰是「federal」，而不是「national」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→定语；与 R3.1 同族。与 A6「词性/同义转换」冲突：把「federal」当同义替换，会改掉原文用词；优先级：A1 原文唯一对应 ＞ A6。本空特有依据：美国语境下 federal 更常见，诱人。
10｜自检：原文形容词？同根重复？国情替换？

**40｜culture**
1｜表"A ____40____ city｜The Smithsonian Museum…John F. Kennedy Center is a center famous for art and culture"；原文"Washington is also a great culture city."。空＝名词定语（A ___ city）。
2｜候选：culture / cultural / art / history。
3｜culture 与原文逐字合，a culture city（原文搭配，虽不地道）；cultural 更合语法但原文用 culture；art 是举例内容；history 属上一栏。
4｜干扰：cultural 是"正确"的形容词，学生易改；下一栏"art and culture"又诱人填 art。
5｜意图：考"原文搭配优先于语法直觉（同 G10 的 a clear thinking）"，分"改成 cultural"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"a ____ center"填 cultural（该处原文用 cultural？原文是"culture city"，故仍 culture）；以原文为准。
7｜错：cultural（改写原文）；cultured（义偏"有教养的"）；cultures（复数）。
8｜R25.5 原文搭配即使不合语法直觉（a culture city），仍照原文填；与 R10.4 同一条原则的第二次出现。适用条件：空位是名词定语（A ___ city），且原文有唯一对应词。例外：若空位改由另一词性承担（名词位换动词/形容词位）；此时本条的适用前提不再成立，入空的就不再是「culture」这一路，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→原文优先；与 R10.4 合并。与 A6「词性/同义转换」冲突：按「cultural」改写，词性/搭配与原位不合；优先级：A1 原文唯一对应 ＞ A6。本空特有依据：cultural 是"正确"的形容词，学生易改。
10｜自检：原文用 culture 还是 cultural？我改了吗？同栏其他词干扰？

**组小结（G25 · 小蓝书预测卷十九）**
- 考点分布：年份直取 1（36）；专名全名 1（37）；并列短语补齐（3 词）1（38）；形容词取原文 1（39）；原文搭配优先 1（40）。
- 规则：R25.1 年份不加 the（36）；R25.2 named after 用全名（37）；R25.3 并列短语补全、3 词内（38）；R25.4 定语取原文（39）；R25.5 原文搭配优先（40）。
- 冲突与优先级：R25.2 与"地名同形"冲突，全名优先；R25.5 与"语法直觉"冲突，原文优先（同 R10.4）。
- 未归类：无。
- 覆盖检查：本篇首次出现"3 词约束"，与真题"1 词"形成对照；R25.5 是 R10.4 的复用，支撑数 +1。

### 2024湖北专升本小蓝书预测卷十二 · Passage Five

Jenny was the only child in her home. She had a quarrel with her mother that afternoon and she ran out of the house with anger. She was sorrowful when she thought of the scolding from her mother. Wandering aimlessly in the street for hours, she felt a little hungry and wished for something to eat, but it was not possible for her, since she had nothing with her. She stood beside a stand for a while, watching the middle-aged seller busy doing his business. However, with no money in hand, she sighed and had to leave.

The seller behind the stand noticed the young girl and asked, "Hey, girl, you want to have the noodles?"

"Oh, yes... but I don't have money on me..." she replied.

"That's nothing. I'll treat you today," said the man. "Come in."

The seller brought her a bowl of noodles, whose smell was so attractive. As she was eating, Jenny cried silently.

"What is it?" asked the man kindly.

"Nothing, actually I was just touched by your kindness!" said Jenny as she wiped her tears. "Even a stranger on the street will give me a bowl of noodles, while my mother drove me out of the house. She showed no care for me. She is so merciless compared to a stranger!"

|  |  |  |
|---|---|---|
| kind   treat   mercy   hunger   expect   move   anger   wander   strict   complain   sorrowful   possible |  |  |
| One afternoon Jenny quarreled with her mother and left home with ____36____. | ⇒ | She felt ____37____. |
| ⇓ |  | ⇓ |
| ____38____ in the street for hours, Jenny felt hungry. | ⇒ | Jenny expected something to eat, but it was impossible for her. |
| ⇓ |  | ⇓ |
| A seller had a small talk with Jenny and treated her to a bowl of noodles. | ⇒ | She was moved and cried silently because of the seller's ____39____. |



| ⇓ |  | ⇓ |
| Jenny thanked the seller and complained that her mother showed no care for her. | ⇒ | She thought her mother was so ____40____ compared to a stranger. |

**〔G26〕小蓝书预测卷十二 · Passage Five（36—40）十层研究**

> 本篇为**词库选词＋词形变化**题：表前词库 kind / treat / mercy / hunger / expect / move / anger / wander / strict / complain / sorrowful / possible 共 12 词，5 空须选词并变形（kindness、merciless、Wandering）。这是本文件唯一的"词库型"，规则与"原文取词型"不同。

**36｜anger**
1｜表"One afternoon Jenny quarreled with her mother and left home with ____36____."；原文"she ran out of the house with anger"。空＝with 后名词。
2｜候选：anger / sorrowful / complain / strict。
3｜anger 与原文逐字合，with anger 成立，且 quarrel→anger 的因果一致；sorrowful 是形容词，with 后不能接；complain 是动词；strict 形容词。
4｜干扰：下一句"She was sorrowful"紧接，诱人填 sorrowful；词库里形容词占多数，易误判词性。
5｜意图：考"with+抽象名词"与词性筛选，分"见情绪就填形容词"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"she left home ____"（副词位）可填 angrily（词库无）；with 后必名词。
7｜错：sorrowful（词性错）；angry（形容词）；anger 写成 angriness。
8｜R26.1 词库题先按空位定词性（with 后名词），再从词库筛同词性项；词库给原形，必要时变形。适用条件：空位是with 后名词，形态由空前结构定。例外（本空错误路径）：填 sorrowful（词性错）；错在把「空位是with 后名词，形态由空前结构定」当成唯一情形，一旦空位的词性/位置换成副词位，答案就要从「anger」换成「angrily」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-4 词库选词→词性筛选；与 R27.1 并列为本文件唯一两条词库规则。与 A1「原词直取」冲突：按「sorrowful」的形态填，to/介词位不成立；优先级：A3 空位词性/形态 ＞ A1。本空特有依据：下一句"She was sorrowful"紧接，诱人填 sorrowful。
10｜自检：空位要什么词性？词库里有几个同词性？需要变形吗？

**37｜sorrowful**
1｜表"She felt ____37____."；原文"She was sorrowful when she thought of the scolding from her mother."。空＝形容词（feel 后）。
2｜候选：sorrowful / anger / strict / possible。
3｜填「sorrowful」→「She felt sorrowful.」：与原文逐字合，felt sorrowful 成立；填「possible」→「She felt possible.」：语义不合，不成立；填「strict」→「She felt strict.」：可作表语但语义不符（母亲严厉≠她难过），不成立；填「anger」→「She felt anger.」：名词，不成立。回读：空位是形容词（feel 后），只有「sorrowful」同时满足形态与原文对应。
4｜干扰：anger 刚用过，学生易重复；strict 与"母亲责骂"有关联，义近而误。
5｜意图：考"feel+形容词"与语义匹配，分"按'被骂'填 strict"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"her mother was ____"填 strict（母亲严厉）；主语是 Jenny 时用 sorrowful。
7｜错：anger（词性错）；strict（主语错位）；sorrow（名词）。
8｜R26.2 feel 后填形容词；主语决定语义（Jenny→sorrowful，mother→strict）。适用条件：空位是形容词（feel 后），且原文有唯一对应词。例外（本空错误路径）：填 anger（词性错）；错在把「空位是形容词（feel 后），且原文有唯一对应词」当成唯一情形，一旦空位的词性/位置换成母亲严厉，答案就要从「sorrowful」换成「strict」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-4→形容词筛选；与 R17.2"情感词不重复"并用。与 A6「词性/同义转换」冲突：换「anger」更顺口，但原文用的是另一个词；优先级：A1 原文唯一对应 ＞ A6。本空特有依据：anger 刚用过，学生易重复。
10｜自检：主语是谁？feel 后词性？词库同词性项哪个贴主语？

**38｜Wandering**
1｜表"____38____ in the street for hours, Jenny felt hungry."；原文"Wandering aimlessly in the street for hours, she felt a little hungry"。空＝现在分词（句首状语，首字母大写）。
2｜候选：Wandering / Wander / To wander / Wondering。
3｜Wandering 与原文逐字合，分词作状语表伴随，首字母大写；Wander 原形不能作状语；To wander 表目的，与"几小时"不合；Wondering 形近义异（想知道）。
4｜干扰：wander/wonder 形近；句首又诱人用原形（祈使句错觉）。
5｜意图：考"分词状语＋首字母大写＋形近词辨析"，分"用原形"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"Jenny ____ in the street"（谓语位）填 wandered（过去式）；句首无主语时用 -ing。
7｜错：Wander（原形）；Wondering（形近）；wandered（谓语式）。
8｜R26.3 句首无主语、后有逗号+主句时，填现在分词（V-ing）并大写首字母；wander≠wonder。适用条件：空位是现在分词（句首状语，首字母大写），形态由空前结构定。例外（本空错误路径）：填 Wander（原形）；错在把「空位是现在分词（句首状语，首字母大写），形态由空前结构定」当成唯一情形，一旦空位的词性/位置换成谓语位，答案就要从「Wandering」换成「wandered」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-4→分词状语；与 R20.4"祈使句"区分：祈使句无逗号+主句。与 A1「原词直取」冲突：「To wander」的形态与本空要求差一层（-ing/原形/过去式）；优先级：A3 空位词性/形态 ＞ A1。本空特有依据：wander/wonder 形近。
10｜自检：句首有无主语？逗号后是主句吗？wander 还是 wonder？

**39｜kindness**
1｜表"She was moved and cried silently because of the seller’s ____39____."；原文""Nothing, actually I was just touched by your kindness!""。空＝名词（所有格后），词库只给 kind。
2｜候选：kindness（kind 的名词化）；kind（形容词）；mercy（词库另一词）；treat（动词/名词）。
3｜kindness 与原文 your kindness 合，所有格后名词，成立；kind 是形容词，所有格后不能单独作中心词；mercy 义为"仁慈"，与"一碗面"的具体善意有偏差，且原文用 kindness；treat 义为"请客"，与"因为…而感动"的因果不符。
4｜干扰：词库给的是 kind（形容词），学生易直接填 kind；mercy 与 kindness 义近，是第二诱饵。
5｜意图：考"词库给原形→按空位变形"，分"照抄 kind"者。这是本篇最核心的一空。
6｜边界：若空位是"the seller was ____"填 kind（形容词）；所有格后必名词 kindness。
7｜错：kind（词性错）；mercy（同义替换）；kindly（副词）。
8｜R26.4 词库给原形而空位需另一词性时，做派生（kind→kindness，mercy→merciless）；先定词性，再定派生后缀。适用条件：空位是名词（所有格后），词库只给 kind，形态由空前结构定。例外（本空错误路径）：填 kind（词性错）；错在把「空位是名词（所有格后），词库只给 kind，形态由空前结构定」当成唯一情形，一旦空位的词性/位置换成形容词，答案就要从「kindness」换成「kind」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-4→词形变化；与 R26.1 同族，是本篇的"变形"双例之一（39、40）。与 A1「原词直取」冲突：直取「mercy」保留原形态，与空前结构不合；优先级：A3 空位词性/形态 ＞ A1。本空特有依据：词库给的是 kind（形容词），学生易直接填 kind。
10｜自检：空位要名词吗？词库原形是什么？后缀对吗？

**40｜merciless**
1｜表"She thought her mother was so ____40____ compared to a stranger."；原文"She is so merciless compared to a stranger!"。空＝形容词（so 后），词库只给 mercy。
2｜候选：merciless（mercy 的形容词，反义派生）；merciful（mercy 的形容词，本义）；mercy（名词）；strict（词库另一词）。
3｜merciless 与原文逐字合，so merciless 成立，且与"compared to a stranger"的对比一致（母亲无情）；merciful 义反（仁慈），与原句"抱怨母亲"矛盾；mercy 名词，so 后不能接；strict 语义偏"严厉"，不含"无情"。
4｜干扰：merciful 是 mercy 的常规形容词，最易误填；strict 与"母亲"关联强。
5｜意图：考"同一词根的正/反派生按语境选"，分"见 mercy 就加 -ful"者。
6｜边界：若前文说母亲宽容，用 merciful；本句是抱怨，须 merciless。
7｜错：merciful（反义）；mercy（词性错）；mercilessly（副词）。
8｜R26.5 词库给名词/形容词原形时，按语境决定加 -ful 还是 -less；-less 表"无"，与否定语境（抱怨、无情）匹配。适用条件：空位是形容词（so 后），词库只给 mercy，形态由空前结构定。例外（本空错误路径）：填 merciful（反义）；错在把「空位是形容词（so 后），词库只给 mercy，形态由空前结构定」当成唯一情形，本条的适用前提不再成立，入空的就不再是「merciless」这一路，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-4→反义派生；与 R26.4 同族。与 A1「原词直取」冲突：按「merciful」的形态填，to/介词位不成立；优先级：A3 空位词性/形态 ＞ A1。本空特有依据：merciful 是 mercy 的常规形容词，最易误填。
10｜自检：语境是褒还是贬？-ful 还是 -less？so 后词性？

**组小结（G26 · 小蓝书预测卷十二）**
- 考点分布：with+名词 1（36）；feel+形容词 1（37）；分词状语 1（38）；名词化 1（39）；反义派生 1（40）。
- 规则：R26.1 词库题先定词性再筛词（36）；R26.2 feel 后形容词、主语定语义（37）；R26.3 句首分词状语并大写（38）；R26.4 原形→名词派生（39）；R26.5 -ful/-less 按语境（40）。
- 冲突与优先级：R26.4/R26.5 与"照抄词库原形"冲突，词性与语境优先；R26.5 中 -ful 与 -less 冲突时，语境褒贬优先。
- 未归类：无。
- 覆盖检查：本篇是本文件唯一的词库型，新增 RC-4 节点与 5 条词库规则，与 RC-1（原文取词）并列。

### 2024湖北专升本小蓝书预测卷十五 · Passage Five

**Following My Dream**

I grew up in a poor family with six brothers, three sisters, my father and mother. We had little money and few goods, but plenty of love and attention. I was happy and energetic. I understood that no matter how poor a person was, he could still afford a dream.



My dream was athletics. By the time I was sixteen, I could throw a ninety-mile-per-hour fastball and hit anything moving on the football field. And I was so lucky that my high-school coach was Ollie Jarvis, who not only believed in me, but often told me "The dream is priceless." However, when I was about to get off the ground, one particular thing happened, which almost ruined (毁坏)my long-held dream.

It was the summer holiday in Senior Two and a friend recommended me for a summer job. I was offered $12.25 per hour. Compared with the average $3.5, that was a big sum. I was so excited, because this meant a chance for money in my pocket, certainly, money for a new bike and new clothes, and the start of savings for a house for my mother. The prospect (前景)of the job was inviting, and I wanted to jump at the opportunity.

Then I realized I would have to give up summer baseball training to handle the work schedule. I didn't dare to tell my coach. I thought of the advice my mother often gave me when I lacked courage, "If you make your bed, you have to lie in it." I decided to talk to my coach by myself. After all, my dream of buying my mom a house and having money in my pocket was worth facing his disappointment to me. I gathered my courage and knocked at his door…

|  | Following My Dream |  |
|---|---|---|
| I grew up in a poor family and we had ______36______ money and few goods. | ⇨ | I understood that no matter how poor a person was, he could still ______37______ a dream. |
| ⇩ |  | ⇩ |
| By the time I was sixteen, I could ______38______ a fast ball and hit anything moving on the football field. | ⇨ | I was so lucky that my coach was Ollie Jarvis, who often told me "The dream is priceless." |
| ⇩ |  | ⇩ |



| In the summer holiday I was ______39______ for a summer job. I was offered $12.25 per hour. | ⇨ | I was so excited about the big sum, because this meant a chance for money in my pocket, money for a new bike and so on. |
| ⇩ |  | ⇩ |
| If I wanted to continue to work, I would have to give up summer baseball training. | ⇨ | I didn't dare to tell my coach, but my dream of buying my mom a house and having money in my pocket was worth facing his ______40______ to me. |

**〔G27〕小蓝书预测卷十五 · Passage Five（36—40）十层研究**

**36｜little**
1｜表"I grew up in a poor family and we had ____36____ money and few goods."；原文"We had little money and few goods"。空＝限定词（修饰不可数 money，与 few goods 对举）。
2｜候选：little / few / a little / less。
3｜little 与原文逐字合，little money 不可数，与 few goods 对举；few 修饰可数，与 money 冲突；a little 表"一些"，与 poor 矛盾；less 是比较级，无比较对象。
4｜干扰：后半 few goods 就在旁边，诱人填 few；中文"很少钱"易写成 a little。
5｜意图：考"little/few 的可数性对立＋与 few 对举"，分"照抄 few"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"____ goods"填 few；money 用 little。a little 只在表"有一点"时用。
7｜错：few（可数性错）；a little（语义反）；less（比较级无据）。
8｜R27.1 little/few 由名词可数性定；与对举项（few goods）互为镜像，不可互换。适用条件：空位是限定词（修饰不可数 money，与 few goods 对举），且原文有唯一对应词。例外（本空错误路径）：填 few（可数性错）；错在把「空位是限定词（修饰不可数 money，与 few goods 对举），且原文有唯一对应词」当成唯一情形，换到那种位置时，入空的恰恰是「few」，而不是「little」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→数量限定；与 R3.4"fewer"同族。与 A6「词性/同义转换」冲突：取「few」属于同义转写，不是原文对应；优先级：A1 原文唯一对应 ＞ A6。本空特有依据：后半 few goods 就在旁边，诱人填 few。
10｜自检：名词可数吗？对举项是什么？a 有没有？

**37｜afford**
1｜表"…he could still ____37____ a dream."；原文"he could still afford a dream"。空＝动词原形（could 后）。
2｜候选：afford / have / make / achieve。
3｜afford 与原文逐字合，afford a dream 成立，且与"poor"形成"穷也负担得起梦想"的反差；have 语义平；make a dream 搭配弱；achieve a dream 义近非原文。
4｜干扰：中文"有梦想"诱人填 have；"实现梦想"诱人填 achieve。
5｜意图：考"could 后原形＋取原文动词（afford 的反差义）"，分"按中文填 have"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"____ a dream come true"填 make；afford 强调"负担得起"。
7｜错：have（语义弱化，丢掉反差）；affording（could 后原形）；affordable（形容词）。
8｜R27.2 情态动词后填原形，取原文动词；注意 afford 在此的"负担得起"义与 poor 的反差，不用泛义 have。适用条件：空位是动词原形（could 后），形态由空前结构定。例外（本空错误路径）：填 have（语义弱化，丢掉反差）；错在把「空位是动词原形（could 后），形态由空前结构定」当成唯一情形，换到那种位置时，入空的恰恰是「make」，而不是「afford」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→情态后原形；与 R19.5 同族。与 A1「原词直取」冲突：「have」的形态与本空要求差一层（-ing/原形/过去式）；优先级：A3 空位词性/形态 ＞ A1。本空特有依据：中文"有梦想"诱人填 have。
10｜自检：could 后形态？原文动词？泛义替换了吗？

**38｜throw**
1｜表"By the time I was sixteen, I could ____38____ a fast ball and hit anything moving…"；原文"I could throw a ninety-mile-per-hour fastball"。空＝动词原形。
2｜候选：throw / hit / catch / pitch。
3｜throw 与原文逐字合，throw a fastball 成立；hit 已在后半（and hit anything），重复；catch 义反；pitch 是棒球术语，原文用 throw。
4｜干扰：后半 hit 就在空后，诱人重复；pitch 是"投球"的专业词，义最近。
5｜意图：考"取原文动词、不与后半重复"，分"填 hit/pitch"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"____ anything moving"填 hit；throw 的宾语是 fastball。
7｜错：hit（与后半重复）；pitch（同义专业词）；throwing（could 后原形）。
8｜R27.3 情态动词后原形，取原文动词；同一句后半已出现的动词不得重复填。适用条件：空位是动词原形，形态由空前结构定。例外（本空错误路径）：填 hit（与后半重复）；错在把「空位是动词原形，形态由空前结构定」当成唯一情形，换到那种位置时，入空的恰恰是「hit」，而不是「throw」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→情态后原形；与 R27.2 同族。与 A1「原词直取」冲突：直取「hit」保留原形态，与空前结构不合；优先级：A3 空位词性/形态 ＞ A1。本空特有依据：后半 hit 就在空后，诱人重复。
10｜自检：could 后形态？原文动词？与后半重复吗？

**39｜recommended**
1｜表"In the summer holiday I was ____39____ for a summer job."；原文"a friend recommended me for a summer job"。空＝过去分词（was 后，被动）。
2｜候选：recommended / offered / hired / chosen。
3｜recommended 与原文 recommend sb for sth 的被动式一致；offered 虽在下一句（I was offered $12.25），但搭配是 offer sb sth，不带 for；hired 无据；chosen 义近非原文。
4｜干扰：下一句"I was offered $12.25 per hour"的 offered 最近，且同样是 was+ed，诱人。
5｜意图：考"主动转被动＋for 搭配"，分"取下一句 offered"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"I was ____ $12.25 per hour"填 offered（offer sb sth 的被动）；有 for 时用 recommended。
7｜错：offered（与 for 不搭）；recommend（缺 -ed）；recommending（语态错）。
8｜R27.4 若空位是 was+___+for，填过去分词且该动词须能接 for；offer 接双宾语，不接 for。适用条件：空位是 was+___+for。例外（本空错误路径）：填 offered（与 for 不搭）；错在把「空位是 was+___+for」当成唯一情形，换到那种位置时，入空的恰恰是「offered」，而不是「recommended」，本条不成立，改按 A18（被动↔主动）。
9｜04→RC-1.2→语态转换；与 R7.3 同族，多一层介词搭配校验。与 A1「原文唯一对应词优先」冲突：按「offered」的语态填，主谓关系不成立；优先级：A18 被动↔主动 ＞ A1 直取。本空特有依据：下一句"I was offered $12.25 per hour"的 offered 最近，且同样是 was+ed，诱人。
10｜自检：was 后分词？动词能接 for 吗？是下一句的词吗？

**40｜disappointment**
1｜表"…my dream of buying my mom a house and having money in my pocket was worth facing his ____40____ to me."；原文"worth facing his disappointment to me"。空＝名词（his 后）。
2｜候选：disappointment / disappoint / disappointed / anger。
3｜disappointment 与原文逐字合，his disappointment 成立；disappoint 动词；disappointed 形容词（his disappointed 不成立）；anger 义偏（教练的反应是失望）。
4｜干扰：中文"面对他的失望/生气"两可，诱人填 anger；disappoint 家族三词易混。
5｜意图：考"所有格后名词＋同根词辨析"，分"填形容词/动词"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"his ____ look"填 disappointed（形容词作定语）；his 后单独作宾语须名词。
7｜错：disappointed（形容词）；disappoint（动词）；disappointing（-ing 修饰物）。
8｜R27.5 所有格后填名词；同根词按空位句法角色选（-ment 名词 / -ed 人 / -ing 物）。适用条件：空位是名词（his 后），形态由空前结构定。例外（本空错误路径）：填 disappointed（形容词）；错在把「空位是名词（his 后），形态由空前结构定」当成唯一情形，一旦空位的词性/位置换成形容词作定语，答案就要从「disappointment」换成「disappointed」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→同根词辨析；与 R15.5"所有格后名词"同族。与 A1「原词直取」冲突：按「disappointed」的形态填，to/介词位不成立；优先级：A3 空位词性/形态 ＞ A1。本空特有依据：中文"面对他的失望/生气"两可，诱人填 anger。
10｜自检：所有格后词性？同根词哪个对？语境是失望还是生气？

**组小结（G27 · 小蓝书预测卷十五）**
- 考点分布：little/few 对举 1（36）；情态后原形 1（37）；同句不重复 1（38）；被动+介词搭配 1（39）；同根词辨析 1（40）。
- 规则：R27.1 little/few 按可数性（36）；R27.2 情态后原形取原文（37）；R27.3 同句动词不重复（38）；R27.4 was+___+for 须能接 for（39）；R27.5 所有格后名词、同根词按角色（40）。
- 冲突与优先级：R27.3 与"就近取词"冲突，原文与不重复优先；R27.4 与"邻句同形词"冲突，介词搭配优先。
- 未归类：无。
- 覆盖检查：本篇补上"同根词三态（-ment/-ed/-ing）"，是词形类最后一个缺口。

### 2024湖北专升本小蓝书预测卷十八 · Passage 5

British towns and cities have rich experience in providing accommodation (膳宿) for students and there are many cheap, comfortable and safe places to live.

You should always try to arrange your long-term accommodation before leaving home. Your school should be able to help you with it. When you are accepted by a school, you will usually receive a package of information including accommodation information. Complete the accommodation application form and return it before the deadline. If the school accommodation is not available, the accommodation advisory(顾问的) office of your school can help you find private accommodation.

If you're coming to the UK for the first time, school accommodation school might be the most suitable choice. This is a choice taken by more than half of the international students. There are plenty of advantages of living in accommodation provided by the school: you can live close to where you are studying; you will get to know other students easily; and most importantly, you will be living in a safer environment. This kind of accommodation is also affordable: a room in a self-catering(自己做饭的) student apartment usually costs from 180 to 360 pounds per month. To live in a place where meals are included you are expected to pay from 320 to 400 pounds per month.

If you choose to rent accommodation in private houses, the choices are lodging, private hostels (招待所) and bedsits(卧室兼起居室). In a lodging, your landlord / landlady lives in the same house, possibly with his / her family, and prepares meals for you. For hostel



accommodation and lodgings where meals are included, you can expect to pay from 300 to 400 pounds per month. For a bedsit, you would pay from 200 to 380 pounds per month.

| Accommodation in Britain |  |  |
|---|---|---|
| Application | Types of accommodation |  |
| You will usually receive a package of information including accommodation application form. Remember to complete the form and return it before the deadline. | School accommodation | Accommodation in ______38______ houses |
|  | It's the most ______36______ choice for international students. There are many ______37______ if living on campus. | You have many ______39______ like lodgings, private hostels and bedsits. For the first choice, you have to ______40______ in the same house with your landlord / landlady. |

**〔G28〕小蓝书预测卷十八 · Passage 5（36—40）十层研究**

**36｜suitable**
1｜表"School accommodation｜It’s the most ____36____ choice for international students."；原文"school accommodation school might be the most suitable choice"。空＝形容词（the most 后）。
2｜候选：suitable / popular / important / comfortable。
3｜suitable 与原文逐字合，the most suitable choice 成立；popular 与下文"taken by more than half"有关但原文 suitable；important 义泛；comfortable 与"cheap, comfortable and safe"有关但非本句。
4｜干扰：首段"cheap, comfortable and safe places"三个形容词诱人取一个；"more than half"诱人填 popular。
5｜意图：考"the most+adj+choice 取原文形容词"，分"取首段形容词"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"the most ____ place"原文用 comfortable 时可填 comfortable；choice 的搭配是 suitable。
7｜错：popular（由比例反推）；suit（动词）；suitably（副词）。
8｜R28.1 the most+___+名词 的空位取原文该名词的定语形容词；不由邻近数据反推。适用条件：空位是形容词（the most 后），且原文有唯一对应词。例外（本空错误路径）：填 popular（由比例反推）；错在把「空位是形容词（the most 后），且原文有唯一对应词」当成唯一情形，换到那种位置时，入空的恰恰是「comfortable」，而不是「suitable」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→定语；与 R3.1 同族。与 A6「词性/同义转换」冲突：把「popular」当同义替换，会改掉原文用词；优先级：A1 原文唯一对应 ＞ A6。本空特有依据：首段"cheap, comfortable and safe places"三个形容词诱人取一个。
10｜自检：the most 后词性？原文定语？邻近数据干扰？

**37｜advantages**
1｜表"…There are many ____37____ if living on campus."；原文"There are plenty of advantages of living in accommodation provided by the school"。空＝复数名词。
2｜候选：advantages / reasons / choices / benefits。
3｜advantages 与原文 plenty of advantages 对应，many+复数；reasons 义偏；choices 与下一栏 choices 重复；benefits 义近非原文。
4｜干扰：下文"you can live close…get to know…safer"像在列举原因，诱人填 reasons；下一栏"many choices"诱人填 choices。
5｜意图：考"plenty of X→many X 的替换取词"，分"按列举内容自造 reasons"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"There are many ____ of living on campus"仍 advantages；choices 属"私人住房"栏，不串栏。
7｜错：benefits（同义替换）；advantage（单数）；reasons（义偏）。
8｜R28.2 若表格把 plenty of 改为 many，名词不变；取原文该数量短语的中心名词，复数。适用条件：表格把 plenty of 改为 many。例外：若栏头或并列项改为单数；此时本条的适用前提不再成立，入空的就不再是「advantages」这一路，本条不成立，改按 A5（单复数独立判定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→数量短语；与 R19.1 同族。与 A1「原文唯一对应词优先」冲突：用「reasons」当标题，覆盖不了全部行；优先级：A8 栏头/标题概括 ＞ A1。本空特有依据：下文"you can live close…get to know…safer"像在列举原因，诱人填 reasons。
10｜自检：原文数量短语？名词复数？是否串栏？

**38｜private**
1｜表"Types of accommodation｜…｜Accommodation in ____38____ houses"；原文"If you choose to rent accommodation in private houses"。空＝形容词。
2｜候选：private / school / local / rented。
3｜private 与原文逐字合，private houses 成立；school 与"私人住房"对立（学校宿舍是上一栏）；local 无据；rented 是动词化，原文 private。
4｜干扰：左栏"School accommodation"就在同一行，诱人填 school（正好相反）；"rent"诱人填 rented。
5｜意图：考"两栏对立（学校/私人）"，分"被左栏词带走"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"____ accommodation"指学校那一栏，填 school；houses 与 private 搭配。
7｜错：school（与右栏语义相反）；rented（动词化）；privacy（名词）。
8｜R28.3 表格左右栏并列对比时，右栏空位取原文对应词，且不得与左栏已给词相同。适用条件：空位是形容词，且原文有唯一对应词。例外（本空错误路径）：填 school（与右栏语义相反）；错在把「空位是形容词，且原文有唯一对应词」当成唯一情形，换到那种位置时，入空的恰恰是「school」，而不是「private」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→对比栏；与 R10.2"行内取词"并用。与 A6「词性/同义转换」冲突：按「school」改写，词性/搭配与原位不合；优先级：A1 原文唯一对应 ＞ A6。本空特有依据：左栏"School accommodation"就在同一行，诱人填 school（正好相反）。
10｜自检：这是哪一栏？原文用词？与左栏重复吗？

**39｜choices**
1｜表"You have many ____39____ like lodgings, private hostels and bedsits."；原文"the choices are lodging, private hostels and bedsits"。空＝复数名词（many 后）。
2｜候选：choices / kinds / ways / advantages。
3｜填「choices」→「You have many choices like lodgings, private hostels and bedsits.」：与原文逐字合，many choices like… 成立；填「advantages」→「You have many advantages like lodgings, private hostels and bedsits.」：是上一栏，串栏，不成立；填「kinds」→「You have many kinds like lodgings, private hostels and bedsits.」：可通但原文 choices，不成立；填「ways」→「You have many ways like lodgings, private hostels and bedsits.」：义偏，不成立。回读：空位是复数名词（many 后），只有「choices」同时满足形态与原文对应。
4｜干扰：上一栏刚填 advantages（many advantages），同形结构诱人重复；like 列举诱人填 kinds。
5｜意图：考"同表两处 many+名词 的区分"，分"重复上一空"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"many ____ of accommodation"可用 kinds/types；like 后列举对应 choices。
7｜错：advantages（串栏重复）；choice（单数）；choose（动词）。
8｜R28.4 同表出现多处 many+名词 时，各空对应各自原文句，不重复、不串栏。适用条件：空位是复数名词（many 后），且栏头/并列项定数。例外（本空错误路径）：填 advantages（串栏重复）；错在把「空位是复数名词（many 后），且栏头/并列项定数」当成唯一情形，本条的适用前提不再成立，入空的就不再是「choices」这一路，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→数量短语；与 R28.2 同族。与 A1「原文唯一对应词优先」冲突：原文没有可直接抄的上位词，「kinds」只是下位词或干扰；优先级：A8 栏头/标题概括 ＞ A1。本空特有依据：上一栏刚填 advantages（many advantages），同形结构诱人重复。
10｜自检：本句原文？上一空填了什么？重复吗？

**40｜live**
1｜表"For the first choice, you have to ____40____ in the same house with your landlord / landlady."；原文"In a lodging, your landlord / landlady lives in the same house"。空＝动词原形（have to 后）。
2｜候选：live / stay / share / rent。
3｜live 与原文 lives in the same house 对应，have to live 成立；stay 义偏"暂住"；share 需 with 宾语且原文无；rent 与"住宿方式"义偏。
4｜干扰：本段首句"rent accommodation in private houses"的 rent 诱人；share…with 结构诱人填 share。
5｜意图：考"have to 后原形＋主语转换（landlord lives→you live）"，分"填 rent"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"you have to ____ the house with others"填 share；in the same house 用 live。
7｜错：living（have to 后原形）；stay（义偏）；share（缺 with）。
8｜R28.5 若原文主语是房东（lives），表格主语改为 you，动词不变（live），只换主语；have to 后原形。适用条件：原文主语是房东（lives）。例外（本空错误路径）：填 living（have to 后原形）；错在把「原文主语是房东（lives）」当成唯一情形，换到那种位置时，入空的恰恰是「share」，而不是「live」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→主语转换；与 R1.3"按主语还原谓语"相反：此处主语变了而动词不变，因动作相同。与 A1「原词直取」冲突：「stay」的形态与本空要求差一层（-ing/原形/过去式）；优先级：A3 空位词性/形态 ＞ A1。本空特有依据：本段首句"rent accommodation in private houses"的 rent 诱人。
10｜自检：主语是谁？原文动词？have to 后形态？

**组小结（G28 · 小蓝书预测卷十八）**
- 考点分布：the most+adj 1（36）；plenty of→many 1（37）；对比栏 1（38）；同表多空区分 1（39）；主语转换 1（40）。
- 规则：R28.1 the most 后取原文定语（36）；R28.2 plenty of 改 many 名词不变（37）；R28.3 对比栏不与左栏重复（38）；R28.4 多空各归其句（39）；R28.5 主语转换动词不变（40）。
- 冲突与优先级：R28.3/R28.4 与"就近/重复"冲突，栏位归属优先；R28.5 与 R1.3"按主语换谓语"冲突，动作相同则动词不变。
- 未归类：无。
- 覆盖检查：本篇补上"对比栏"与"同表多空"，是表格结构类规则的最后一块。

### 2024湖北专升本小蓝书预测卷十六 · Passage 5

Could you imagine being in a car driven by a computer, not a human? Recently, China



has made a big step toward self-driving cars.

Since August, driverless taxis can run on roads in Wuhan, Chongqing and Shenzhen. Self-driving cars bring us many benefits. They use computers to sense the things around them and make better use of space. This means fewer traffic jams and better parking of cars. For disabled or blind people, self-driving cars can also help them get around more easily.

According to US Centers for Disease Control and Prevention, more than 1.35 million people are killed on roads around the world every year. Many more are injured. Self-driving cars could reduce road accidents by 90 percent and save governments a lot of money, said the website TechDim. The main cause of car accidents is human mistakes. By using computers, self-driving cars don't make poor decisions or lose attention like humans do.

But self-driving cars aren't perfect. If something unexpected on the road happens, self-driving cars can't react as fast as a human driver could. Also, during weather events like heavy snow, self-driving car s would struggle (费力) to see the lines on the road. It might lead to an accident. The car's computer system could also get hacked (非法入侵) by someone, causing it to crash or change its destination (目的地).

It's clear that there is a lot of work to do to make self-driving cars a common and safe sight on our roads.And China is taking the lead in making this happen.

|  | ◆______38______ human, self-driving cars won't suffer from loss of attention or make poor decisions, so they can also reduce car accidents. |
| Imperfection(不完美) | ◆The cars can't react faster than a human driver if something unexpected on the road happens. |
|  | ◆The cars would ______39______ seeing the lines on the road to lead to an accident during the weather events. |
|  | ◆Getting the car's computer system hacked will lead to the |



|  | ◆Getting the car's computer system hacked will lead to the ______40______ of car crash and destination change. |

**〔G29〕小蓝书预测卷十六 · Passage 5（36—40）十层研究**

**36｜Self-driving cars（The future of driving 亦可，开放空）**
1｜表"Title ____36____"，下辖 Benefits 与 Imperfection 两栏，全文讲无人驾驶。空＝标题名词短语。
2｜候选：Self-driving cars / Driverless cars / The future of driving / Cars。
3｜Self-driving cars 与全文关键词一致（self-driving cars 反复出现），作标题成立；Driverless cars 义同，首段 driverless taxis 出现；The future of driving 概括"前景"，但两栏讲的是利弊不是前景；Cars 太泛。
4｜干扰：首句"a car driven by a computer"诱人填 Computers；"driverless taxis"诱人只写 Taxis。
5｜意图：考"标题取全文关键词"，分"取首句具体词（computer）"者。开放空，答案不唯一。
6｜边界：若下辖只有"benefits"一栏，标题可写 Advantages of self-driving cars；两栏并列时标题须中性（只写对象）。
7｜错：Computer（过窄）；Driverless（缺 cars）；Self-driving car（单数，与全文复数不称）。
8｜R29.1 标题空取全文反复出现的关键名词短语；下辖利弊两栏时标题须中性，只写对象不写评价。适用条件：空位是栏头/标题的概括位，且须统摄下辖各行。例外（本空错误路径）：填 Computer（过窄）；错在把「空位是栏头/标题的概括位，且须统摄下辖各行」当成唯一情形，本条的适用前提不再成立，入空的就不再是「Self-driving cars」这一路，本条不成立，改按 A8（栏头/标题概括）。
9｜04→RC-1.3→标题；与 R9.1 同族（自造上位），此处有原文关键词可抄。与 A1「原文唯一对应词优先」冲突：抄「Driverless cars」会概括不住下辖各行；优先级：A8 栏头/标题概括 ＞ A1。本空特有依据：首句"a car driven by a computer"诱人填 Computers。
10｜自检：全文关键词是什么？单复数？标题中性吗？

**37｜easier**
1｜表"◆The lives of the disabled will be ____37____"；原文"For disabled or blind people, self-driving cars can also help them get around more easily."。空＝形容词（be 后）。
2｜候选：easier / better / convenient / easier to live。
3｜easier 与 get around more easily 对应（副词→形容词），The lives…will be easier 成立；better 义泛；convenient 是"方便"非"更轻松"且无比较；easier to live 超词。
4｜干扰：中文"生活更方便"诱人填 convenient；more easily 的副词形式诱人直搬 easily（缺比较）。
5｜意图：考"副词比较级→形容词比较级＋主语转换（get around→lives）"，分"填 convenient"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"the disabled will get around ____"填 more easily（副词）；be 后用形容词。
7｜错：convenient（无比较、义偏）；easily（副词）；easy（丢比较）。
8｜R29.2 若原文用"help sb do sth more easily"，表格改为"sth will be ___"，须转为形容词比较级（easier）。适用条件：原文用"help sb do sth more easily"。例外（本空错误路径）：填 convenient（无比较、义偏）；错在把「原文用"help sb do sth more easily"」当成唯一情形，一旦空位的词性/位置换成副词，答案就要从「easier」换成「more easily」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.2→词性/级数转换；与 R21.2 同族。与 A6「词性/同义转换」冲突：换「better」更顺口，但原文用的是另一个词；优先级：A1 原文唯一对应 ＞ A6。本空特有依据：中文"生活更方便"诱人填 convenient。
10｜自检：be 后词性？原文是比较级吗？主语换了吗？

**38｜Unlike**
1｜表"◆____38____ human, self-driving cars won’t suffer from loss of attention or make poor decisions…"；原文"By using computers, self-driving cars don’t make poor decisions or lose attention like humans do."。空＝介词（句首，大写）。
2｜候选：Unlike / Like / Compared / Besides。
3｜Unlike 与"不像人类那样"对应，Unlike human(s), cars won't… 成立；Like 义反（像人类才会犯错）；Compared 需 with/to；Besides 义"此外"。
4｜干扰：原文 like humans do 就在句末，诱人填 Like；Compared 与后半 won't 的对比感也顺。
5｜意图：考"对比句首介词＋语义方向"，分"照抄 like"者。
6｜边界：若句子是"____ humans, cars also make mistakes"填 Like；本句是否定，须 Unlike。
7｜错：Like（方向反）；Compared（缺 with/to）；Unlikely（副词/形容词）。
8｜R29.3 句首对比空看主句是肯定还是否定：否定差异用 Unlike，肯定相似用 Like；Compared 须带 with/to。适用条件：空位是介词（句首，大写），且原文有唯一对应词。例外（本空错误路径）：填 Like（方向反）；错在把「空位是介词（句首，大写），且原文有唯一对应词」当成唯一情形，换到那种位置时，入空的恰恰是「Like」，而不是「Unlike」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-2 提纲填空→对比介词；与 R1.1 同族。与 A6「词性/同义转换」冲突：取「Like」属于同义转写，不是原文对应；优先级：A1 原文唯一对应 ＞ A6。本空特有依据：原文 like humans do 就在句末，诱人填 Like。
10｜自检：主句肯定还是否定？需要 with/to 吗？大写？

**39｜struggle**
1｜表"◆The cars would ____39____ seeing the lines on the road to lead to an accident during the weather events."；原文"self-driving cars would struggle (费力) to see the lines on the road"。空＝动词原形（would 后）。
2｜候选：struggle / fail / have difficulty / be hard。
3｜struggle 与原文逐字合，would struggle to see 成立；fail 义过重（完全不能）；have difficulty 须接 in；be hard 结构不合（cars would be hard to see）。
4｜干扰：中文"难以看清"诱人填 fail/have difficulty；括号注释"费力"提示 struggle，但学生常忽略。
5｜意图：考"would 后原形＋取原文动词（含注释提示）"，分"按中文填 fail"者。
6｜边界：若空位是"have ____ seeing"填 difficulty（名词）；would 后用原形。
7｜错：fail（义过重）；difficulty（名词）；struggling（would 后原形）。
8｜R29.4 情态动词后原形；原文括注的汉语释义往往就是该词的考点，回原文取词时优先核对括注词。适用条件：空位是动词原形（would 后），形态由空前结构定。例外（本空错误路径）：填 fail（义过重）；错在把「空位是动词原形（would 后），形态由空前结构定」当成唯一情形，一旦空位的词性/位置换成名词，答案就要从「struggle」换成「difficulty」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→情态后原形；与 R27.2 同族。与 A1「原词直取」冲突：直取「fail」保留原形态，与空前结构不合；优先级：A3 空位词性/形态 ＞ A1。本空特有依据：中文"难以看清"诱人填 fail/have difficulty。
10｜自检：would 后形态？原文动词？括注词是哪个？

**40｜possibility（risk 亦可，半开放）**
1｜表"◆Getting the car’s computer system hacked will lead to the ____40____ of car crash and destination change."；原文"The car’s computer system could also get hacked, causing it to crash or change its destination."。空＝名词（the…of）。
2｜候选：possibility / risk / danger / cause。
3｜possibility 与 could…causing 的"可能"义合，the possibility of car crash 成立；risk 亦可（风险），原文用 could 表可能；danger 义偏"危险"；cause 与 lead to the cause of 语义重复。
4｜干扰：lead to 诱人填 cause（导致…的原因）；crash 的严重性诱人填 danger。
5｜意图：考"could→possibility 的情态名词化"，分"填 cause"者。半开放。
6｜边界：若空位是"will ____ car crash"（动词位）填 cause；the…of 结构须名词。
7｜错：cause（与 lead to 重复）；possible（形容词）；possibilities（单数语境）。
8｜R29.5 could/can 表可能时，表格 the ___ of 结构填 possibility；lead to 后不填 cause（语义重复）。适用条件：空位是名词（the…of），且原文有唯一对应词。例外（本空错误路径）：填 cause（与 lead to 重复）；错在把「空位是名词（the…of），且原文有唯一对应词」当成唯一情形，一旦空位的词性/位置换成动词位，答案就要从「possibility」换成「cause」，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.2→情态名词化；与 R9.2 同族。与 A6「词性/同义转换」冲突：把「risk」当同义替换，会改掉原文用词；优先级：A1 原文唯一对应 ＞ A6。本空特有依据：lead to 诱人填 cause（导致…的原因）。
10｜自检：the…of 要名词吗？原文是 could 吗？与 lead to 重复吗？

**组小结（G29 · 小蓝书预测卷十六）**
- 考点分布：标题关键词 1（36，开放）；副词→形容词比较级 1（37）；对比介词 1（38）；情态后原形 1（39）；情态名词化 1（40，半开放）。
- 规则：R29.1 标题取全文关键词、中性（36）；R29.2 副词比较级转形容词比较级（37）；R29.3 句首对比看主句肯否（38）；R29.4 括注词优先核对（39）；R29.5 could→possibility（40）。
- 冲突与优先级：R29.3 与"照抄 like"冲突，肯否方向优先；R29.5 与"lead to cause"冲突，语义不重复优先。
- 未归类：36、40 为开放/半开放。
- 覆盖检查：本篇补上"标题中性"与"情态名词化"，标题类规则至此 3 例（G9、G12、G29）。

### 2024湖北专升本小蓝书预测卷十四 · Passage 5

It's time to leave school. To show how much they will miss the school, the students in Class 6, Grade 9 are excited to plan a graduation party in the dining hall. The students are going to make party posters, prepare food and drinks, think of games to play and prepare things they need for the games. They are also planning to invite their teachers and headmaster to the party.

The party starts at 9 o'clock this Friday morning. At the party, the students will give speeches to show thanks to their teachers and classmates. After that, they will enjoy the food and drinks, play the games, as well as sing and dance. It will be a lot of fun! They are looking forward to it!

Graduation Party

Time: 9:00 am, this ______ 36 ______ June 18.

Place: the ______ 37 ______ .

Participants: the students, the ______ 38 ______ and the headmaster.

Activities:



√ ______39______

√Play the games

√Sing and dance

______40______ and drinks available.

A party is more fun with more people! Hope you can make it!

**〔G30〕小蓝书预测卷十四 · Passage 5（36—40）十层研究**

**36｜Friday**
1｜表"Time: 9:00 am, this ____36____ June 18."；原文"The party starts at 9 o’clock this Friday morning."。空＝专有名词（this 与 June 18 之间）。
2｜候选：Friday / Friday morning / morning / weekend。
3｜Friday 与原文 this Friday morning 对应，this Friday, June 18 成立；Friday morning 与表内 9:00 am 重复且超位；morning 与 9:00 am 重复；weekend 无据。
4｜干扰：原文 this Friday morning 的 morning 紧随，诱人填 morning；9:00 am 已表上午，学生易忽略重复。
5｜意图：考"时间栏去重（am 已表上午）＋专名大写"，分"照抄 morning"者。
6｜边界：若表内只写"this ____"（无 9:00 am）可填 Friday morning；有 am 则只填 Friday。
7｜错：morning（与 am 重复）；friday（未大写）；Fridays（复数）。
8｜R30.1 时间栏空须避免与已给时间信息重复（9:00 am 已含"上午"），只补星期/日期；专名大写。适用条件：空位是专有名词（this 与 June 18 之间），且原文有唯一对应词。例外：若空位改由另一词性承担（名词位换动词/形容词位）；此时本条的适用前提不再成立，入空的就不再是「Friday」这一路，本条不成立，改按 A3（空位词性由空前一词定）。
9｜04→RC-1.1→时间信息；与 R18.5"数字+单位"互补（一个防重复、一个防截断）。与 A6「词性/同义转换」冲突：按「morning」改写，词性/搭配与原位不合；优先级：A1 原文唯一对应 ＞ A6。本空特有依据：原文 this Friday morning 的 morning 紧随，诱人填 morning。
10｜自检：与已给时间重复吗？大写？原文星期？

**37｜dining hall**
1｜表"Place: the ____37____."；原文"plan a graduation party in the dining hall"。空＝名词短语（the 后）。
2｜候选：dining hall / hall / dining room / school。
